PATH - src\vigil\agent\investigator.py
CODE:
"""Agentic Orchestrator and Lead Investigator coordinating multi-phase video forensics."""

import logging
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from vigil.agent.planner import InvestigationPlanner
from vigil.agent.registry import create_default_executor
from vigil.agent.specialized.audio_agent import AudioAgent
from vigil.agent.specialized.correlation_agent import TemporalCorrelationAgent
from vigil.agent.specialized.report_writer_agent import ReportWriterAgent
from vigil.agent.specialized.verification_agent import EvidenceVerificationAgent
from vigil.agent.specialized.visual_agent import VisualAgent
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.report import Report
from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.models.factory import get_llm_client
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import (
    InvestigationState,
    InvestigationStatus,
)
from vigil.orchestration.service import OrchestrationService, TaskExecutionOutcome
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest, TaskResult, TaskStatus
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvestigationOutcome:
    """Consolidated outcome of an end-to-end agentic forensic investigation."""

    investigation: Investigation
    state: InvestigationState
    report: Report | None
    task_results: list[TaskResult] = field(default_factory=list)
    observations: list[Observation] = field(default_factory=list)
    events: list[Event] = field(default_factory=list)
    assessments: list[EvidenceAssessment] = field(default_factory=list)
    coverage_records: list[CoverageRecord] = field(default_factory=list)


class AgenticOrchestrator:
    """Orchestrator that coordinates specialized agents across the full forensic lifecycle."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository | None = None,
        service: OrchestrationService | None = None,
        planner: InvestigationPlanner | None = None,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository or InvestigationRepository()
        self._custom_service = service
        self.llm = llm_client or get_llm_client()

    @property
    def llm(self) -> BaseLLMClient:
        return self._llm

    @llm.setter
    def llm(self, client: BaseLLMClient) -> None:
        self._llm = client
        self.planner = InvestigationPlanner(llm_client=self._llm)
        if self._custom_service is None:
            executor = create_default_executor(
                repository=self.repository, llm_client=self._llm
            )
            self.service = OrchestrationService(executor=executor)
        else:
            self.service = self._custom_service

        self.visual_agent = VisualAgent(
            repository=self.repository,
            service=self.service,
            llm_client=self._llm,
        )
        self.audio_agent = AudioAgent(
            repository=self.repository,
            service=self.service,
            llm_client=self._llm,
        )
        self.correlation_agent = TemporalCorrelationAgent(
            repository=self.repository,
            service=self.service,
            llm_client=self._llm,
        )
        self.verification_agent = EvidenceVerificationAgent(
            repository=self.repository,
            service=self.service,
            llm_client=self._llm,
        )
        self.report_writer = ReportWriterAgent(
            repository=self.repository,
            service=self.service,
            llm_client=self._llm,
        )

    def step(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        request: TaskRequest,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Execute one forensic task step and synchronize state and repository."""
        outcome = self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

        # Sync output IDs into state
        current_obs_ids = set(outcome.state.observation_ids)
        current_evt_ids = set(outcome.state.event_ids)

        new_obs_ids = [
            ref
            for ref in outcome.task_result.output_refs
            if ref.startswith("obs-")
        ]
        new_evt_ids = [
            ref
            for ref in outcome.task_result.output_refs
            if ref.startswith("evt-")
        ]

        updated_obs = list(current_obs_ids | set(new_obs_ids))
        updated_evts = list(current_evt_ids | set(new_evt_ids))

        synced_state = InvestigationState.model_validate(
            outcome.state.model_dump()
            | {
                "observation_ids": updated_obs,
                "event_ids": updated_evts,
                "updated_at": datetime.now(UTC),
            }
        )

        self.repository.save_state(synced_state)
        return TaskExecutionOutcome(
            state=synced_state, task_result=outcome.task_result
        )

    def run_investigation(
        self,
        investigation: Investigation,
        event_callback: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
    ) -> InvestigationOutcome:
        """Run the full autonomous agentic forensic investigation loop."""
        # Save investigation in repository
        self.repository.add_investigation(investigation)

        # Initialize investigation state
        state = InvestigationState(
            investigation_id=investigation.investigation_id,
            status=InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
        )
        self.repository.save_state(state)
        if event_callback:
            event_callback(
                "state_changed",
                {
                    "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                    "status": state.status.value,
                    "updated_at": state.updated_at.isoformat(),
                },
            )

        # Generate investigation plan
        planned_tasks = self.planner.plan_investigation(investigation)
        if event_callback:
            event_callback(
                "plan_created",
                {
                    "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                    "task_count": len(planned_tasks),
                    "tasks": [
                        {
                            "task_type": t.task_type,
                            "description": t.description,
                        }
                        for t in planned_tasks
                    ],
                },
            )
        task_results: list[TaskResult] = []

        # Execute each planned task through the orchestration service
        for planned in planned_tasks:
            task_req = planned.to_task_request(
                requested_by=investigation.created_by
                or "lead-investigator"
            )

            if event_callback:
                event_callback(
                    "task_started",
                    {
                        "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                        "task_id": task_req.task_id,
                        "task_type": task_req.task_type,
                        "description": planned.description,
                    },
                )

            prev_obs_ids = set(state.observation_ids)
            prev_evt_ids = set(state.event_ids)

            # Execute step
            outcome = self.step(state, task_req)
            state = outcome.state
            task_results.append(outcome.task_result)

            if event_callback:
                # Report new observations
                new_obs_ids = set(state.observation_ids) - prev_obs_ids
                if new_obs_ids:
                    new_obs = self.repository.list_observations(list(new_obs_ids))
                    for obs in new_obs:
                        event_callback(
                            "observation_detected",
                            {
                                "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                                "task_id": task_req.task_id,
                                "observation": obs.model_dump(mode="json"),
                            },
                        )

                # Report new events
                new_evt_ids = set(state.event_ids) - prev_evt_ids
                if new_evt_ids:
                    new_evts = self.repository.list_events(list(new_evt_ids))
                    for evt in new_evts:
                        event_callback(
                            "event_correlated",
                            {
                                "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                                "task_id": task_req.task_id,
                                "event": evt.model_dump(mode="json"),
                            },
                        )

                # Report coverage updates
                if task_req.task_type == "coverage_evaluation":
                    cov_records = self.repository.list_coverage_records(
                        investigation.investigation_id
                    )
                    for cov in cov_records:
                        event_callback(
                            "coverage_updated",
                            {
                                "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                                "task_id": task_req.task_id,
                                "coverage": cov.model_dump(mode="json"),
                            },
                        )

                event_callback(
                    "task_completed",
                    {
                        "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                        "task_id": task_req.task_id,
                        "task_type": task_req.task_type,
                        "status": outcome.task_result.status.value,
                        "output_refs": outcome.task_result.output_refs,
                        "error": outcome.task_result.error.model_dump(mode="json")
                        if outcome.task_result.error
                        else None,
                    },
                )

            # If task failed, stop the investigation pipeline
            if outcome.task_result.status == TaskStatus.FAILED:
                logger.error(
                    f"Investigation task {task_req.task_id} failed: "
                    f"{outcome.task_result.error}"
                )
                if event_callback:
                    event_callback(
                        "investigation_failed",
                        {
                            "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                            "failed_task_id": task_req.task_id,
                            "error": outcome.task_result.error.message
                            if outcome.task_result.error
                            else "Task execution failed",
                        },
                    )
                break

        # If all tasks succeeded or yielded partial results, complete the investigation
        final_report: Report | None = None
        if state.status == InvestigationStatus.RUNNING:
            state = self.service.complete_investigation(state)
            self.repository.save_state(state)

            reports = self.repository.list_reports(
                investigation.investigation_id
            )
            if reports:
                final_report = reports[-1]

            if event_callback:
                event_callback(
                    "state_changed",
                    {
                        "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                        "status": state.status.value,
                        "updated_at": state.updated_at.isoformat(),
                    },
                )
                event_callback(
                    "investigation_completed",
                    {
                        "investigation_id": investigation.investigation_id,
                        "status": state.status.value,
                        "report_id": final_report.report_id if final_report else None,
                        "report_title": final_report.title if final_report else None,
                    },
                )

        # Collect consolidated findings scoped strictly to this investigation
        inv_obs = self.repository.list_observations(state.observation_ids)
        inv_events = self.repository.list_events(state.event_ids)
        target_ids = set(state.event_ids) | set(state.observation_ids)
        all_assessments = self.repository.list_evidence_assessments()
        scoped_assessments = [a for a in all_assessments if a.target_id in target_ids]

        return InvestigationOutcome(
            investigation=investigation,
            state=state,
            report=final_report,
            task_results=task_results,
            observations=inv_obs,
            events=inv_events,
            assessments=scoped_assessments,
            coverage_records=self.repository.list_coverage_records(
                investigation.investigation_id
            ),
        )


# Backward-compatible alias
LeadInvestigatorAgent = AgenticOrchestrator

PATH - src\vigil\agent\planner.py
CODE:
"""Investigation planning and adaptive task generation."""

import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlannedTask:
    """A task planned by the agentic reasoner."""

    task_type: str
    description: str
    input_refs: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_task_request(
        self, requested_by: str = "investigation-planner"
    ) -> TaskRequest:
        """Convert planned task into a domain TaskRequest."""
        return TaskRequest(
            task_id=f"task-{self.task_type.replace('_', '-')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            task_type=self.task_type,
            input_refs=self.input_refs,
            parameters=self.parameters,
            requested_by=requested_by,
        )


class InvestigationPlanner:
    """Decomposes an investigation objective and scope into structured forensic tasks."""

    def __init__(self, llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None) -> None:
        self.llm = llm_client

    def plan_investigation(self, investigation: Investigation) -> list[PlannedTask]:
        """Construct the initial multi-phase forensic investigation plan."""
        inv_id = investigation.investigation_id
        media_refs = list(investigation.media_asset_ids)

        tasks: list[PlannedTask] = []

        # Phase 1: Frame Extraction / Interval Sampling
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="frame_sampling",
                description="Extract forensic sample frames from media assets across timeline",
                input_refs=media_refs,
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                    "sample_interval_seconds": 2.0,
                    "max_frames": 8,
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 2: Visual Multi-modal Analysis
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="visual_analysis",
                description="Analyze extracted frame artifacts for persons, vehicles, objects, and anomalies",
                input_refs=[],  # Dynamically resolved by worker or updated by agent
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                    "detect_entities": True,
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 3: Audio Transcription & Acoustic Analysis (if applicable)
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="audio_transcription",
                description="Transcribe audio tracks and detect acoustic trigger events",
                input_refs=media_refs,
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 4: Temporal Correlation & Event Link Discovery
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="event_correlation",
                description="Correlate observations chronologically into Events and EventLinks",
                input_refs=[],
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 5: Evidence Assessment
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="evidence_assessment",
                description="Assess evidence quality, support level, and uncertainty for identified events",
                input_refs=[],
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                    "assessed_by": "vigil-forensic-assessor",
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 6: Coverage Evaluation
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="coverage_evaluation",
                description="Evaluate and record source examination coverage across the investigation timeline",
                input_refs=media_refs,
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                },
            )
        )

        # Phase 7: Forensic Report Generation
        tasks.append(
            PlannedTask(
                task_type="report_generation",
                description="Synthesize all findings, observations, events, and assessments into a final Report",
                input_refs=[],
                parameters={
                    "investigation_id": inv_id,
                    "title": f"Forensic Investigation Report: {investigation.title}",
                    "generated_by": "vigil-lead-investigator",
                },
            )
        )

        return tasks

CODE:
"""Worker registry bootstrap for TaskExecutor."""

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.models.factory import get_llm_client
from vigil.orchestration.executor import TaskExecutor
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository
from vigil.tools.audio_transcriber import AudioTranscriberWorker
from vigil.tools.clip_slicer import ClipSlicerWorker
from vigil.tools.coverage_tracker import CoverageTrackerWorker
from vigil.tools.event_correlator import EventCorrelatorWorker
from vigil.tools.evidence_assessor import EvidenceAssessorWorker
from vigil.tools.frame_extractor import FrameExtractorWorker
from vigil.tools.media_ingestion import MediaIngestionWorker
from vigil.tools.report_generator import ReportGeneratorWorker
from vigil.tools.visual_detector import VisualDetectorWorker


def create_default_executor(
    repository: InvestigationRepository,
    llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
) -> TaskExecutor:
    """Build and configure a TaskExecutor with all standard VIGIL forensic tool workers."""
    llm = llm_client or get_llm_client()
    executor = TaskExecutor()

    # Register each tool worker
    executor.register(
        "media_ingestion", MediaIngestionWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm)
    )
    executor.register(
        "frame_sampling", FrameExtractorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm)
    )
    executor.register(
        "clip_extraction", FrameExtractorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm)
    )
    executor.register(
        "clip_slicing", ClipSlicerWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm)
    )
    executor.register(
        "incident_clip_extraction",
        ClipSlicerWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )
    executor.register(
        "visual_analysis", VisualDetectorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm)
    )
    executor.register(
        "audio_transcription",
        AudioTranscriberWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )
    executor.register(
        "event_correlation",
        EventCorrelatorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )
    executor.register(
        "evidence_assessment",
        EvidenceAssessorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )
    executor.register(
        "coverage_evaluation",
        CoverageTrackerWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )
    executor.register(
        "report_generation",
        ReportGeneratorWorker(repository=repository, llm_client=llm),
    )

    return executor

class AudioAgent:
    """Specialized agent responsible for acoustic and speech analysis of footage."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository,
        service: OrchestrationService,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository
        self.service = service
        self.llm = llm_client

    def analyze_audio(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        media_refs: list[str],
        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Transcribe speech and identify acoustic triggers (alarms, shouts, sirens)."""

        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="audio_transcription",
            input_refs=media_refs,
            parameters=parameters or {},
            requested_by="audio-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

"""Report Writer Agent compiling structured forensic findings into comprehensive reports."""

import uuid
from typing import Any

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationState
from vigil.orchestration.service import OrchestrationService, TaskExecutionOutcome
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


class ReportWriterAgent:
    """Specialized agent responsible for synthesizing validated evidence into forensic reports."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository,
        service: OrchestrationService,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository
        self.service = service
        self.llm = llm_client

    def generate_report(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        investigation_id: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Synthesize verified findings into a structured, evidence-grounded Report domain object."""
        task_id = f"task-report-generation-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        params = dict(parameters or {})
        params["investigation_id"] = investigation_id
        if title:
            params["title"] = title
        params.setdefault("generated_by", "report-writer-agent")

        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="report_generation",
            input_refs=[],
            parameters=params,
            requested_by="report-writer-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

import uuid
from typing import Any

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationState
from vigil.orchestration.service import OrchestrationService, TaskExecutionOutcome
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


class EvidenceVerificationAgent:
    """Specialized agent assessing evidence sufficiency, rating quality, and evaluating coverage."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository,
        service: OrchestrationService,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository
        self.service = service
        self.llm = llm_client

    def verify_evidence(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        target_ids: list[str],
        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Assess evidence quality, support level, and uncertainty for observations or events."""
        task_id = f"task-evidence-assessment-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="evidence_assessment",
            input_refs=target_ids,
            parameters=parameters or {"assessed_by": "evidence-verification-agent"},
            requested_by="evidence-verification-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

    def evaluate_coverage(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        investigation_id: str,
        source_ref_ids: list[str],
        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Evaluate whether footage intervals were thoroughly examined or have gaps."""
        task_id = f"task-coverage-evaluation-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        params = dict(parameters or {})
        params["investigation_id"] = investigation_id

        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="coverage_evaluation",
            input_refs=source_ref_ids,
            parameters=params,
            requested_by="evidence-verification-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

"""Visual Agent specializing in frame sampling, object/person detection, and scene analysis."""

import uuid
from typing import Any

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationState
from vigil.orchestration.service import OrchestrationService, TaskExecutionOutcome
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


class VisualAgent:
    """Specialized agent responsible for extracting and visually analyzing footage."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository,
        service: OrchestrationService,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository
        self.service = service
        self.llm = llm_client

    def extract_frames(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        media_asset_ids: list[str],
        sample_interval_seconds: float = 2.0,
        max_frames: int = 10,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Sample frames from media assets to generate Artifact records."""
        task_id = f"task-frame-sampling-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="frame_sampling",
            input_refs=media_asset_ids,
            parameters={
                "sample_interval_seconds": sample_interval_seconds,
                "max_frames": max_frames,
            },
            requested_by="visual-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

    def analyze_frames(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        artifact_ids: list[str],
        parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Analyze image artifacts using vision models to detect persons, vehicles, and objects."""
        task_id = f"task-visual-analysis-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        request = TaskRequest(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_type="visual_analysis",
            input_refs=artifact_ids,
            parameters=parameters or {"detect_entities": True},
            requested_by="visual-agent",
        )
        return self.service.execute_task(state=state, request=request)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\api\app.py
CODE:
"""FastAPI application factory for the VIGIL Video Forensics platform."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import http
import logging
import time
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles

from vigil.api.dependencies import (
    get_investigation_service,
    get_telemetry_manager,
)
from vigil.api.routes.investigations import router as investigations_router
from vigil.api.routes.websocket import router as websocket_router
from vigil.logging_config import configure_logging
from vigil.storage.repository import EntityNotFoundError

# Install timestamped logging as early as possible so every module that
# calls logging.getLogger(__name__) inherits the correct formatter.
configure_logging()

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    """Lifespan context manager registering active event loop and managing background shutdown."""
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    telemetry = get_telemetry_manager()
    telemetry.set_event_loop(loop)
    logger.info("VIGIL API services initialized.")
    yield
    logger.info("Shutting down VIGIL API services...")
    service = get_investigation_service()
    service.shutdown(wait=False)


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    """Create and configure the FastAPI application."""
    app = FastAPI(
        title="VIGIL: Autonomous Video-Forensics API",
        description=(
            "API Layer and Real-Time WebSocket Telemetry for autonomous multi-agent video "
            "and acoustic forensic investigation."
        ),
        version="0.1.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    # CORS configuration for human investigator review UI
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=["*"],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    # HTTP request/response logging middleware with colour-coded status output code context
    @app.middleware("http")
    async def request_logging_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
        start_time = time.perf_counter()
        response = await call_next(request)
        process_time_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000.0
        code = response.status_code
        try:
            phrase = http.HTTPStatus(code).phrase.upper()
        except ValueError:
            phrase = "RESPONSE"
        client_host = request.client.host if request.client else "127.0.0.1"
        access_log = logging.getLogger("vigil.access")
        access_log.info(
            f'{client_host} - "{request.method} {request.url.path}" {code} {phrase} ({process_time_ms:.1f}ms)'
        )
        return response

    # Global Exception Handlers
    @app.exception_handler(EntityNotFoundError)
    async def entity_not_found_handler(
        request: Request, exc: EntityNotFoundError
    ) -> JSONResponse:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            content={"detail": str(exc)},
        )

    # Health and readiness check
    @app.get("/health", tags=["system"])
    @app.get("/api/v1/health", tags=["system"])
    def health_check() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "status": "healthy",
            "service": "VIGIL Video Forensics API",
            "version": "0.1.0",
        }

    # Mount UI static assets and serve dashboard
    ui_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "ui"
    if ui_dir.is_dir():
        app.mount("/ui", StaticFiles(directory=str(ui_dir)), name="ui")

        @app.get("/", tags=["ui"], summary="VIGIL Web UI Mission Control")
        @app.get("/dashboard", tags=["ui"])
        def serve_dashboard() -> FileResponse:
            """Serve interactive VIGIL forensic frontend dashboard."""
            index_file = ui_dir / "index.html"
            return FileResponse(index_file)

    # Include routers
    app.include_router(investigations_router)
    app.include_router(websocket_router)

    return app


app = create_app()

PATH - src\vigil\api\dependencies.py
CODE:
"""FastAPI dependency providers for VIGIL services and storage."""

from __future__ import annotations

import functools
from pathlib import Path

from vigil.config import config
from vigil.services.investigation_service import InvestigationService
from vigil.services.telemetry import TelemetryManager
from vigil.storage.sqlite_repository import SqliteInvestigationRepository


@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_telemetry_manager() -> TelemetryManager:
    """Provide singleton TelemetryManager instance."""
    return TelemetryManager()


@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_investigation_service() -> InvestigationService:
    """Provide application InvestigationService backed by persistent SQLite."""
    storage_root = Path(config.storage_root).resolve()
    storage_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db_path = storage_root / "vigil.db"

    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(db_path=db_path)
    telemetry = get_telemetry_manager()

    return InvestigationService(
        repository=repo,
        telemetry=telemetry,
        storage_dir=storage_root,
    )

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\api\routes\investigations.py
CODE:
"""FastAPI route handlers for investigation management and reporting."""

from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    File,
    Form,
    HTTPException,
    Query,
    Request,
    Response,
    UploadFile,
    status,
)
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

from vigil.api.dependencies import get_investigation_service
from vigil.api.schemas import (
    EventEditRequest,
    EventReviewRequest,
    EventReviewResponse,
    IncidentClipSliceRequest,
    InvestigationCreateResponse,
    InvestigationStatusResponse,
    InvestigationSummaryResponse,
    ReportFinalizeRequest,
    ReportFinalizeResponse,
)
from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.report import Report
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationStatus
from vigil.services.investigation_service import InvestigationService
from vigil.storage.repository import EntityNotFoundError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/investigations", tags=["investigations"])


@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=InvestigationCreateResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED,
    summary="Upload video footage and initiate an investigation",
)
def create_investigation(
    file: Annotated[
        UploadFile | None,
        File(description="Video footage file (.mp4, .mkv, .avi, etc.)"),
    ] = None,
    file_path: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(
            description="Path to an existing video file on the server if not uploading directly"
        ),
    ] = None,
    title: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Investigation title"),
    ] = None,
    objective: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Forensic objective and questions to verify"),
    ] = None,
    scope_description: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Scope boundaries and camera details"),
    ] = None,
    camera_id: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Unique camera ID (e.g. cam-north-01)"),
    ] = None,
    camera_name: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Human readable camera name"),
    ] = None,
    camera_location: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Camera location label"),
    ] = None,
    camera_timezone: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Camera timezone (e.g. UTC, America/New_York)"),
    ] = None,
    sample_interval_seconds: Annotated[
        float,
        Form(
            description="Interval in seconds between extracted sample frames"
        ),
    ] = 2.0,
    max_frames: Annotated[
        int,
        Form(description="Maximum number of sample frames to extract"),
    ] = 50,
    extract_audio: Annotated[
        bool,
        Form(description="Whether to demux and analyze audio track"),
    ] = True,
    run_async: Annotated[
        bool,
        Form(
            description="If True, processes investigation asynchronously in background"
        ),
    ] = True,
    created_by: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Form(description="Investigator username or badge ID"),
    ] = None,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> InvestigationCreateResponse:
    """Upload video footage, perform physical media ingestion and frame/audio extraction,

    and initiate an autonomous agentic forensic investigation.
    """
    target_path: Path

    if file is not None and file.filename:
        upload_dir = service.storage_dir / "uploads"
        upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        unique_name = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}_{file.filename}"
        target_path = upload_dir / unique_name

        try:
            with open(target_path, "wb") as buffer:
                shutil.copyfileobj(file.file, buffer)
        finally:
            file.file.close()

    elif file_path:
        target_path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not target_path.is_file():
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=f"Specified file_path does not exist: {file_path}",
            )
    else:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail="Either a video file upload or a valid file_path is required.",
        )

    try:
        inv, result = service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
            file_path=target_path,
            title=title,
            objective=objective,
            scope_description=scope_description,
            camera_id=camera_id,
            camera_name=camera_name,
            camera_location=camera_location,
            camera_timezone=camera_timezone,
            sample_interval_seconds=sample_interval_seconds,
            max_frames=max_frames,
            extract_audio=extract_audio,
            created_by=created_by,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=f"Media ingestion failed: {exc}",
        ) from exc

    # Start investigation
    service.run_investigation(inv.investigation_id, run_async=run_async)

    curr_status = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)

    return InvestigationCreateResponse(
        investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
        title=inv.title,
        status=curr_status.status.value,
        media_asset_id=result.media_asset.media_asset_id,
        camera_id=result.camera.camera_id if result.camera else None,
        frame_count=len(result.frame_artifacts),
        has_audio=len(result.audio_artifacts) > 0,
        message=(
            "Investigation initiated and running in background."
            if run_async
            else "Investigation completed."
        ),
    )


@router.get(
    "/{investigation_id}/status",
    response_model=InvestigationStatusResponse,
    summary="Poll task progress and lifecycle state",
)
def get_investigation_status(
    investigation_id: str,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> InvestigationStatusResponse:
    """Poll the real-time status, completed tasks, observation counts,

    event counts, and report readiness for an investigation.
    """
    try:
        status_info = service.get_status(investigation_id)
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' not found.",
        ) from exc

    return InvestigationStatusResponse(
        investigation_id=status_info.investigation_id,
        status=status_info.status.value,
        created_at=status_info.created_at,
        updated_at=status_info.updated_at,
        started_at=status_info.started_at,
        completed_at=status_info.completed_at,
        completed_tasks=status_info.completed_tasks,
        task_ids=status_info.task_ids,
        observation_count=status_info.observation_count,
        event_count=status_info.event_count,
        coverage_count=status_info.coverage_count,
        has_report=status_info.has_report,
        report_id=status_info.report_id,
        error=status_info.error,
    )


@router.get(
    "/{investigation_id}/report",
    response_model=Report,
    summary="Retrieve the compiled forensic report",
)
def get_investigation_report(
    investigation_id: str,
    format: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Query(
            description="Set to 'markdown' or 'text' for formatted text"
        ),
    ] = None,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> Response | Report:
    """Retrieve the compiled forensic report with observations, events,

    evidence assessments, and coverage links.
    """
    try:
        status_info = service.get_status(investigation_id)
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' not found.",
        ) from exc

    if status_info.status in {
        InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
        InvestigationStatus.RUNNING,
    }:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=(
                f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' is still in status "
                f"'{status_info.status.value}'. Report has not yet been generated."
            ),
        )

    report = service.get_report(investigation_id)
    if report is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"No forensic report generated for investigation '{investigation_id}'.",
        )

    if format in {"markdown", "md", "text"}:
        md_text = service.export_report_markdown(investigation_id, report.report_id)
        return Response(content=md_text, media_type="text/markdown")

    if format == "html":
        html_content = service.export_report_html(investigation_id, report.report_id)
        return Response(content=html_content, media_type="text/html")

    if format == "pdf":
        pdf_bytes = service.export_report_pdf(investigation_id, report.report_id)
        return Response(
            content=pdf_bytes,
            media_type="application/pdf",
            headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="report_{investigation_id}.pdf"'},
        )

    return report


@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=list[InvestigationSummaryResponse],
    summary="List all investigations",
)
def list_investigations(
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> list[InvestigationSummaryResponse]:
    """List all registered forensic investigations."""
    investigations = service.list_investigations()
    summaries: list[InvestigationSummaryResponse] = []
    for inv in investigations:
        try:
            st = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)
            status_val = st.status.value
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            status_val = "created"

        summaries.append(
            InvestigationSummaryResponse(
                investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
                title=inv.title,
                objective=inv.objective,
                status=status_val,
                created_at=inv.created_at,
                media_asset_ids=inv.media_asset_ids,
            )
        )
    return summaries

@router.get(
    "/{investigation_id}/events",
    response_model=list[Event],
    summary="Get events correlated during investigation",
)

    investigation_id: str,
    event_status: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Query(
            alias="status",
            description="Filter events by review status: proposed, under_review, confirmed, rejected",
        ),
    ] = None,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> list[Event]:
    """Retrieve correlated events associated with an investigation, optionally filtered by status."""
    try:
        service.get_investigation(investigation_id)
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' not found.",
        ) from exc

    events = service.get_events(investigation_id)
    if event_status:
        events = [e for e in events if e.status.lower() == event_status.lower()]
    return events

@router.post(
    "/{investigation_id}/events/{event_id}/review",
    response_model=EventReviewResponse,
    summary="Human investigator review decision on a proposed event",
)
def review_event(
    investigation_id: str,
    event_id: str,
    payload: EventReviewRequest,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> EventReviewResponse:
    """Submit human verification (confirm or reject) or update review status for an event."""
    try:
        updated_event, audit_log = service.review_event(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_id=event_id,
            status=payload.status,
            reviewer=payload.reviewer,
            notes=payload.notes,
            description=payload.description,
            event_type=payload.event_type,
            start_seconds=payload.start_seconds,
            end_seconds=payload.end_seconds,
            start_time=payload.start_time,
            end_time=payload.end_time,
        )
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc

    return EventReviewResponse(
        event=updated_event,
        audit_log=audit_log,
        message=f"Event '{event_id}' review status updated to '{payload.status}'.",
    )


@router.patch(
    "/{investigation_id}/events/{event_id}",
    response_model=EventReviewResponse,
    summary="Directly edit event attributes with audit logging",
)
def edit_event(
    investigation_id: str,
    event_id: str,
    payload: EventEditRequest,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> EventReviewResponse:
    """Apply direct investigator edits to an event record."""
    updates = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True, exclude={"editor", "notes"})
    try:
        updated_event, audit_log = service.edit_event(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_id=event_id,
            editor=payload.editor,
            updates=updates,
            notes=payload.notes,
        )
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc

    return EventReviewResponse(
        event=updated_event,
        audit_log=audit_log,
        message=f"Event '{event_id}' updated successfully.",
    )


@router.post(
    "/{investigation_id}/events/{event_id}/clip",
    response_model=Artifact,
    summary="Cut playable incident video sub-clip for event",
)
def slice_event_clip(
    investigation_id: str,
    event_id: str,
    payload: IncidentClipSliceRequest | None = None,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> Artifact:
    """Extract playable incident video sub-clip around event interval."""
    padding = payload.padding_seconds if payload else 1.0
    try:
        return service.slice_incident_clip(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_id=event_id,
            padding_seconds=padding,
        )
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=f"Clip extraction failed: {exc}",
        ) from exc

@router.post(
    "/{investigation_id}/report/finalize",
    response_model=ReportFinalizeResponse,
    summary="Finalize forensic report with incident clips and review conclusions",
)
def finalize_investigation_report(
    investigation_id: str,
    payload: ReportFinalizeRequest,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> ReportFinalizeResponse:
    """Finalize the investigation report, incorporating verified event decisions and incident clips."""
    try:
        final_report, audit_log = service.finalize_report(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            finalized_by=payload.finalized_by,
            title=payload.title,
            summary=payload.summary,
            notes=payload.notes,
            auto_slice_clips=payload.auto_slice_clips,
        )
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=f"Report finalization failed: {exc}",
        ) from exc

    return ReportFinalizeResponse(
        report=final_report,
        audit_log=audit_log,
        version=final_report.version,
        clip_count=len(final_report.clip_artifact_ids),
        message=f"Report finalized as version {final_report.version} by {payload.finalized_by}.",
    )

        raise HTTPException(

        ) from exc

@router.get(
    "/{investigation_id}/artifacts/{artifact_id}/content",
    summary="Stream or download extracted media artifact file (video clip, frame image, audio)",
)
def get_artifact_content(
    investigation_id: str,
    artifact_id: str,
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> FileResponse:
    """Stream or download actual artifact file content for browser playback or viewing."""
    try:
        service.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        artifact = service.repository.get_artifact(artifact_id)
    except EntityNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc

    local_path = service.get_artifact_content_path(artifact_id)
    if not local_path or not local_path.is_file():
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Artifact media file for '{artifact_id}' not found on server storage.",
        )

    media_type = "video/mp4" if artifact.media_type == "video" else ("audio/wav" if artifact.media_type == "audio" else "image/jpeg")
    return FileResponse(path=local_path, media_type=media_type, filename=local_path.name)



@router.post(
    "/demo",
    response_model=InvestigationCreateResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Seed and initialize a rich demo forensic investigation",
)
def create_demo_investigation(
    service: Annotated[
        InvestigationService,
        Depends(get_investigation_service),
    ] = None,  # type: ignore[assignment]
) -> InvestigationCreateResponse:
    """Initialize a rich sample multi-modal investigation with synthetic surveillance video,

    extracted frames, acoustic cues, observations, events, and reports.
    """
    try:
        inv, result = service.create_demo_investigation()
        return InvestigationCreateResponse(
            investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
            title=inv.title,
            status="completed",
            media_asset_id=result.media_asset.media_asset_id,
            camera_id="cam-north-04",
            frame_count=len(result.frame_artifacts),
            has_audio=True,
            message="Demo forensic investigation seeded successfully.",
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            detail=f"Failed to create demo investigation: {exc}",
        ) from exc

    media_type = "video/mp4" if asset.media_type == "video" else ("audio/wav" if asset.media_type == "audio" else "image/jpeg")
    return FileResponse(path=local_path, media_type=media_type, filename=local_path.name)

PATH - src\vigil\api\routes\websocket.py
CODE:
"""WebSocket routes streaming live investigation telemetry to connected investigators."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect

from vigil.api.dependencies import get_telemetry_manager
from vigil.services.telemetry import TelemetryManager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter(tags=["telemetry"])


async def handle_telemetry_stream(
    websocket: WebSocket,
    investigation_id: str,
    telemetry: TelemetryManager,
) -> None:
    """Accept WebSocket connection, replay events, and stream real-time updates."""
    await websocket.accept()
    await telemetry.connect(investigation_id, websocket)
    logger.info(
        f"WebSocket client connected to telemetry stream for '{investigation_id}'"
    )

    try:
        while True:
            # Keep connection alive and accept incoming messages (e.g. pings)
            message = await websocket.receive_text()
            if message.strip().lower() == "ping":
                await websocket.send_text('{"event_type": "pong"}')
    except (WebSocketDisconnect, ConnectionResetError):
        logger.info(
            f"WebSocket client disconnected from investigation '{investigation_id}'"
        )
    finally:
        await telemetry.disconnect(investigation_id, websocket)


@router.websocket("/api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/telemetry")
async def investigation_telemetry_ws(
    websocket: WebSocket,
    investigation_id: str,
    telemetry: Annotated[TelemetryManager, Depends(get_telemetry_manager)],
) -> None:
    """Stream live agent actions, newly detected observations,

    event correlations, and coverage updates in real-time.
    """
    await handle_telemetry_stream(websocket, investigation_id, telemetry)


@router.websocket("/ws/investigations/{investigation_id}")
async def investigation_ws_alias(
    websocket: WebSocket,
    investigation_id: str,
    telemetry: Annotated[TelemetryManager, Depends(get_telemetry_manager)],
) -> None:
    """Convenience alias route for WebSocket telemetry."""
    await handle_telemetry_stream(websocket, investigation_id, telemetry)

PATH - src\vigil\api\schemas.py
CODE:
"""Pydantic schemas for the VIGIL REST and WebSocket API."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.report import Report


class InvestigationCreateResponse(BaseModel):
    """Response returned upon successfully creating/initiating an investigation."""

    investigation_id: str = Field(description="Unique investigation ID")
    title: str = Field(description="Investigation title")
    status: str = Field(description="Current status (e.g. 'created', 'running')")
    media_asset_id: str = Field(description="Primary ingested media asset ID")
    camera_id: str | None = Field(
        default=None, description="Registered camera ID if any"
    )
    frame_count: int = Field(
        default=0, description="Number of sample frames extracted"
    )
    has_audio: bool = Field(
        default=False, description="Whether an audio stream was extracted"
    )
    message: str = Field(description="Informational status message")


class InvestigationStatusResponse(BaseModel):
    """Detailed status response for investigation polling."""

    investigation_id: str
    status: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    started_at: datetime | None = None
    completed_at: datetime | None = None
    completed_tasks: int
    task_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    observation_count: int
    event_count: int
    coverage_count: int
    has_report: bool
    report_id: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None


class InvestigationSummaryResponse(BaseModel):
    """Summary item for listing investigations."""

    investigation_id: str
    title: str
    objective: str
    status: str
    created_at: datetime
    media_asset_ids: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class TelemetryEventMessage(BaseModel):
    """WebSocket event envelope for real-time telemetry."""

    investigation_id: str
    event_type: str
    timestamp: str
    data: dict[str, Any]


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Phase 5: Human Review & Audit Schemas
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------


class EventReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request payload for an investigator reviewing a proposed event."""

    status: Literal["confirmed", "rejected", "under_review", "proposed"] = (
        Field(
            description="Decision status: confirmed (verified), rejected (dismissed), or under_review"
        )
    )
    reviewer: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Investigator username, badge number, or reviewer identifier",
    )
    notes: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Forensic rationale, corroborating evidence notes, or justification",
    )
    description: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional edited human-readable event description",
    )
    event_type: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional edited category classification",
    )
    start_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        ge=0.0,
        description="Optional footage-relative start offset in seconds",
    )
    end_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        ge=0.0,
        description="Optional footage-relative end offset in seconds",
    )
    start_time: datetime | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional wall-clock start datetime",
    )
    end_time: datetime | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional wall-clock end datetime",
    )


class EventReviewResponse(BaseModel):
    """Response returned upon successfully reviewing or modifying an event."""

    event: Event = Field(description="Updated Event domain model")
    audit_log: AuditLog = Field(
        description="Immutable audit log entry tracking the decision"
    )
    message: str = Field(description="Status confirmation message")


class EventEditRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request payload for direct investigator edits to an event record."""

    editor: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Investigator badge or username applying the edits",
    )
    description: str | None = None
    event_type: str | None = None
    start_seconds: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0)
    end_seconds: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=0.0)
    start_time: datetime | None = None
    end_time: datetime | None = None
    notes: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Investigator notes explaining the edit",
    )


class ReportFinalizeRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request payload for finalizing a report and locking evidence findings."""

    finalized_by: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Lead investigator username or badge number finalizing the investigation",
    )
    title: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional customized title for the finalized report deliverable",
    )
    summary: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional finalized executive summary incorporating human review insights",
    )
    notes: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Concluding forensic notes, legal remarks, or chain of custody sign-off",
    )
    auto_slice_clips: bool = Field(
        default=True,
        description="Whether to automatically slice playable video sub-clips for confirmed incidents",
    )


class ReportFinalizeResponse(BaseModel):
    """Response returned upon successfully finalizing an investigation report."""

    report: Report = Field(description="Finalized Report domain model")
    audit_log: AuditLog = Field(
        description="Audit entry recording the report finalization"
    )
    version: int = Field(description="Report version number")
    clip_count: int = Field(
        description="Number of incident clips linked in report"
    )
    message: str = Field(description="Confirmation message")


class IncidentClipSliceRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request payload for cutting a sub-clip for an event."""

    padding_seconds: float = Field(
        default=1.0,
        ge=0.0,
        description="Padding in seconds added before start and after end",
    )

PATH - src\vigil\cli.py
CODE:
"""VIGIL Command-Line Interface supporting server management, human review, audit logs, and report export."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from vigil.config import config
from vigil.services.investigation_service import InvestigationService
from vigil.storage.sqlite_repository import SqliteInvestigationRepository


def create_cli_service() -> InvestigationService:
    """Create InvestigationService backed by the persistent SQLite database."""
    db_path = Path(config.storage_root) / "vigil.db"
    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(db_path=db_path)
    return InvestigationService(
        repository=repo,
        storage_dir=config.storage_root,
    )


def handle_serve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Start FastAPI server."""
    import uvicorn

    host = args.host
    port = args.port
    print(f"Starting VIGIL API server on {host}:{port}...")
    uvicorn.run("vigil.api.app:app", host=host, port=port, reload=False)


def handle_review_event(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Review an event (confirm, reject, or under_review) via CLI."""
    service = create_cli_service()
    try:
        event, audit_log = service.review_event(
            investigation_id=args.investigation_id,
            event_id=args.event_id,
            status=args.status,
            reviewer=args.reviewer,
            notes=args.notes,
            description=args.description,
        )
        print(f"\n[SUCCESS] Event '{event.event_id}' review updated to '{event.status.upper()}'.")
        print(f"  Reviewer : {event.reviewer}")
        print(f"  Notes    : {event.review_notes or '-'}")
        print(f"  Audit ID : {audit_log.audit_id}")
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def handle_list_events(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """List events for an investigation with their review status."""
    service = create_cli_service()
    try:
        events = service.get_events(args.investigation_id)
        if args.status:
            events = [e for e in events if e.status.lower() == args.status.lower()]

        print(f"\nEvents for Investigation '{args.investigation_id}' ({len(events)} total):")
        print("-" * 80)
        print(f"{'Event ID':<16} {'Category':<20} {'Status':<14} {'Interval':<16} {'Reviewer'}")
        print("-" * 80)
        for e in events:
            time_str = "-"
            if e.start_seconds is not None and e.end_seconds is not None:
                time_str = f"{e.start_seconds:.1f}s - {e.end_seconds:.1f}s"
            elif e.start_time and e.end_time:
                time_str = f"{e.start_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} - {e.end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}"

            reviewer = e.reviewer or "-"
            print(f"{e.event_id:<16} {e.event_type[:19]:<20} {e.status.upper():<14} {time_str:<16} {reviewer}")
        print("-" * 80)
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def handle_audit(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Print the chronological audit trail for an investigation."""
    service = create_cli_service()
    try:
        logs = service.get_audit_logs(
            investigation_id=args.investigation_id,
            target_id=args.target_id,
            actor=args.actor,
        )
        print(f"\nAudit Log for Investigation '{args.investigation_id}' ({len(logs)} entries):")
        print("-" * 85)
        print(f"{'Timestamp (UTC)':<20} {'Actor':<16} {'Action':<16} {'Target':<14} {'Changes / Notes'}")
        print("-" * 85)
        for l in logs:
            ts_str = l.timestamp.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            notes = l.notes or ""
            if l.changes:
                notes = f"{l.changes} {notes}"
            print(f"{ts_str:<20} {l.actor[:15]:<16} {l.action[:15]:<16} {l.target_id[:13]:<14} {notes[:40]}")
        print("-" * 85)
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def handle_finalize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Finalize forensic report with incident clips."""
    service = create_cli_service()
    try:
        report, audit_log = service.finalize_report(
            investigation_id=args.investigation_id,
            finalized_by=args.finalized_by,
            title=args.title,
            summary=args.summary,
            notes=args.notes,
            auto_slice_clips=not args.no_clips,
        )
        print(f"\n[SUCCESS] Report finalized successfully for investigation '{args.investigation_id}'.")
        print(f"  Report ID   : {report.report_id}")
        print(f"  Version     : v{report.version}")
        print(f"  Finalized By: {report.finalized_by}")
        print(f"  Clips Linked: {len(report.clip_artifact_ids)}")
        print(f"  Audit ID    : {audit_log.audit_id}")
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def handle_export(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Export report to PDF, HTML, or Markdown."""
    service = create_cli_service()
    try:
        fmt = args.format.lower()
        out_path: Path
        if args.output:
            out_path = Path(args.output).resolve()
        else:
            ext = "pdf" if fmt == "pdf" else ("html" if fmt == "html" else "md")
            out_path = Path(f"report_{args.investigation_id}.{ext}").resolve()

        if fmt == "html":
            content_str = service.export_report_html(
                investigation_id=args.investigation_id,
                report_id=args.report_id,
            )
            out_path.write_text(content_str, encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"\n[SUCCESS] Exported interactive HTML report to: {out_path}")

        elif fmt == "pdf":
            content_bytes = service.export_report_pdf(
                investigation_id=args.investigation_id,
                report_id=args.report_id,
            )
            out_path.write_bytes(content_bytes)
            print(f"\n[SUCCESS] Exported publication PDF report to: {out_path} ({len(content_bytes)} bytes)")

        elif fmt in {"markdown", "md", "text"}:
            report = service.get_report(args.investigation_id)
            if not report:
                print(f"[ERROR] No report found for '{args.investigation_id}'.")
                return
            md_text = (
                f"# {report.title}\n\n"
                f"**Investigation ID**: {report.investigation_id}\n"
                f"**Report ID**: {report.report_id} (v{report.version} - {report.status})\n"
                f"**Generated By**: {report.generated_by}\n\n"
                f"## Executive Summary\n\n{report.summary}\n"
            )
            out_path.write_text(md_text, encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"\n[SUCCESS] Exported Markdown report to: {out_path}")
        else:
            print(f"[ERROR] Unsupported export format '{fmt}'. Choose from: html, pdf, markdown.")
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    """Build argument parser for VIGIL CLI."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="vigil",
        description="VIGIL: Autonomous Video Forensics System CLI",
    )
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", help="Available subcommands")

    # serve
    serve_parser = subparsers.add_parser("serve", help="Start FastAPI REST and WebSocket server")
    serve_parser.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0", help="Binding host (default: 0.0.0.0)")
    serve_parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8000, help="Port (default: 8000)")
    serve_parser.set_defaults(func=handle_serve)

    # review
    review_parser = subparsers.add_parser("review", help="Human review workflow commands")
    review_sub = review_parser.add_subparsers(dest="review_command")

    # review event
    rev_event = review_sub.add_parser("event", help="Review an individual proposed event")
    rev_event.add_argument("investigation_id", help="Investigation ID")
    rev_event.add_argument("event_id", help="Event ID")
    rev_event.add_argument(
        "--status",
        required=True,
        choices=["confirmed", "rejected", "under_review", "proposed"],
        help="Review decision status",
    )
    rev_event.add_argument("--reviewer", required=True, help="Investigator badge or username")
    rev_event.add_argument("--notes", help="Forensic review justification or notes")
    rev_event.add_argument("--description", help="Updated event description")
    rev_event.set_defaults(func=handle_review_event)

    # review list-events
    rev_list = review_sub.add_parser("list-events", help="List events and review statuses")
    rev_list.add_argument("investigation_id", help="Investigation ID")
    rev_list.add_argument("--status", help="Filter by review status")
    rev_list.set_defaults(func=handle_list_events)

    # audit
    audit_parser = subparsers.add_parser("audit", help="Inspect investigation audit trail")
    audit_parser.add_argument("investigation_id", help="Investigation ID")
    audit_parser.add_argument("--target-id", help="Filter by targeted record ID")
    audit_parser.add_argument("--actor", help="Filter by investigator/actor")
    audit_parser.set_defaults(func=handle_audit)

    # finalize-report
    finalize_parser = subparsers.add_parser("finalize-report", help="Finalize report and link incident clips")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("investigation_id", help="Investigation ID")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("--finalized-by", required=True, help="Investigator badge or username")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("--title", help="Custom finalized report title")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("--summary", help="Custom finalized executive summary")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("--notes", help="Closing remarks or reviewer notes")
    finalize_parser.add_argument("--no-clips", action="store_true", help="Skip automatic incident clip slicing")
    finalize_parser.set_defaults(func=handle_finalize)

    # export
    export_parser = subparsers.add_parser("export", help="Export report to PDF, HTML, or Markdown")
    export_parser.add_argument("investigation_id", help="Investigation ID")
    export_parser.add_argument("--format", default="html", choices=["html", "pdf", "markdown", "md"], help="Export format")
    export_parser.add_argument("--output", "-o", help="Target output file path")
    export_parser.add_argument("--report-id", help="Specific report ID to export")
    export_parser.set_defaults(func=handle_export)

    return parser


def main() -> None:
    """Main CLI entrypoint."""
    parser = build_parser()
    if len(sys.argv) <= 1:
        print("=================================================================")
        print("  VIGIL: Autonomous Video-Forensics Agentic System")
        print("=================================================================")
        print(f"Active LLM Provider: {config.llm_provider}")
        print(f"Storage Directory  : {config.storage_root}")
        print("Status             : Engine Ready")
        print("Run tests with     : pytest")
        print("Start API with     : vigil serve (or uvicorn vigil.api.app:app)")
        print("Review events with : vigil review event <inv_id> <event_id> --status confirmed --reviewer <name>")
        print("Export reports with: vigil export <inv_id> --format pdf (or --format html)")
        print("=================================================================")
        parser.print_help()
        return

    args = parser.parse_args()
    if hasattr(args, "func"):
        args.func(args)
    else:
        parser.print_help()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

PATH - src\vigil\config.py
CODE:
"""Configuration management for VIGIL."""

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path


def load_env_file(dotenv_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    """Load key-value pairs from a .env file into os.environ if not already set."""
    if dotenv_path is None:
        dotenv_path = Path.cwd() / ".env"

    if not dotenv_path.is_file():
        return

    try:
        content = dotenv_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for line in content.splitlines():
            line = line.strip()
            if not line or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if "=" in line:
                key, _, value = line.partition("=")
                key = key.strip()
                value = value.strip().strip("'\"")
                if key and key not in os.environ:
                    os.environ[key] = value
    except OSError:
        # Ignore errors reading .env in restricted environments
        return


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VigilConfig:
    """Runtime configuration for VIGIL models, storage, and orchestration."""

    llm_provider: str = "mock"
    gemini_api_key: str | None = None
    gemini_model: str = "gemini-2.0-flash"
    groq_api_key: str | None = None
    groq_reasoning_model: str = "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"
    groq_vision_model: str = "llama-3.2-11b-vision-preview"
    groq_audio_model: str = "whisper-large-v3"
    ollama_base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434"
    ollama_model: str = "llama3.2-vision"
    openrouter_api_key: str | None = None
    openrouter_model: str = "google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free"
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # NVIDIA NIM (Llama 3.2 11B Vision VLM + Nemotron 3 Nano 30B LLM)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    nvidia_api_key: str | None = None
    nvidia_vlm_model: str = "meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct"
    nvidia_llm_model: str = "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"
    nvidia_llm_base_url: str = "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
    nvidia_vlm_base_url: str = "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    storage_root: str = "./storage_data"
    log_level: str = "INFO"

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls) -> "VigilConfig":
        """Build configuration from environment variables and .env file."""
        load_env_file()

        gemini_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        groq_key = os.getenv("GROQ_API_KEY")
        openrouter_key = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
        nvidia_key = os.getenv("NVIDIA_API_KEY")

        # Determine default provider intelligently
        provider = os.getenv("VIGIL_LLM_PROVIDER")
        if not provider:
            if nvidia_key:
                provider = "nvidia_nim"
            elif gemini_key:
                provider = "gemini"
            elif groq_key:
                provider = "groq"
            elif openrouter_key:
                provider = "openrouter"
            else:
                provider = "mock"

        return cls(
            llm_provider=provider.lower().strip(),
            gemini_api_key=gemini_key,
            gemini_model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL", "gemini-2.0-flash"),
            groq_api_key=groq_key,
            groq_reasoning_model=os.getenv(
                "GROQ_REASONING_MODEL", "openai/gpt-oss-20b"
            ),
            groq_vision_model=os.getenv(
                "GROQ_VISION_MODEL", "openai/gpt-oss-20b"
            ),
            groq_audio_model=os.getenv("GROQ_AUDIO_MODEL", "whisper-large-v3"),
            ollama_base_url=os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:11434"),
            ollama_model=os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "llama3.2-vision"),
            openrouter_api_key=openrouter_key,
            openrouter_model=os.getenv(
                "OPENROUTER_MODEL", "google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free"
            ),
            nvidia_api_key=nvidia_key,
            nvidia_vlm_model=os.getenv(
                "NVIDIA_VLM_MODEL", "meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct"
            ),
            nvidia_llm_model=os.getenv(
                "NVIDIA_LLM_MODEL", "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"
            ),
            nvidia_llm_base_url=os.getenv(
                "NVIDIA_LLM_BASE_URL", "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
            ),
            nvidia_vlm_base_url=os.getenv(
                "NVIDIA_VLM_BASE_URL", "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
            ),
            storage_root=os.getenv("VIGIL_STORAGE_ROOT", "./storage_data"),
            log_level=os.getenv("VIGIL_LOG_LEVEL", "INFO"),
        )


# Global default configuration instance
config = VigilConfig.from_env()
