PATH - src\vigil\domain\artifact.py
CODE:
"""Derived media artifact schema."""

import re
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, field_validator, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, Timestamp
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference


class Artifact(DomainModel):
    """Represents media derived from an original source."""

    artifact_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for the derived artifact.",
    )

    artifact_type: Literal[
        "frame",
        "crop",
        "clip",
        "audio_segment",
        "derived_media",
    ] = Field(
        description="Operation-specific kind of derived media.",
    )

    media_type: Literal["video", "audio", "image"] = Field(
        description="Actual media type stored by this artifact.",
    )

    storage_uri: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Storage location of the derived media.",
    )

    source_reference: SourceReference = Field(
        description=(
            "Original media and timeline interval from which this artifact was derived."
        ),
    )

    sha256: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional SHA-256 checksum of the derived artifact, "
            "represented by exactly 64 hexadecimal characters."
        ),
    )

    created_at: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware timestamp when the artifact was created.",
    )

    @field_validator("storage_uri")
    @classmethod
    def validate_storage_uri(cls, value: str) -> str:
        """Reject blank artifact locations."""
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("Storage URI must not be blank.")
        return value

    @field_validator("sha256")
    @classmethod
    def validate_sha256(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
        """Validate the optional SHA-256 checksum."""
        if value is not None and not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{64}", value):
            raise ValueError("SHA-256 must contain 64 hexadecimal characters.")
        return value

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_artifact_type(self) -> "Artifact":
        """Ensure the artifact kind matches its media type."""
        expected_types = {
            "frame": "image",
            "crop": "image",
            "clip": "video",
            "audio_segment": "audio",
        }

        expected = expected_types.get(self.artifact_type)
        if expected is not None and self.media_type != expected:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Artifact type '{self.artifact_type}' "
                f"requires media type '{expected}'."
            )

        return self

"""Audit log schema for tracking investigator review decisions and provenance."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import Field, field_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, Timestamp


class AuditLog(DomainModel):
    """Immutable record tracking human review decisions and lifecycle modifications."""

    audit_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Unique identifier for this audit entry.",
    )

    investigation_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="ID of the investigation associated with this audit entry.",
    )

    action: Literal[
        "event_review",
        "event_edit",
        "report_finalize",
        "report_regenerate",
        "investigation_update",
    ] | str = Field(
        description="Action performed (e.g. event_review, report_finalize).",
    )

    actor: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Investigator badge, username, or agent ID who executed the action.",
    )

    timestamp: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware timestamp when the action occurred.",
    )

    target_type: Literal["event", "report", "investigation", "artifact"] = Field(
        description="Domain entity type targeted by the action.",
    )

    target_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Identifier of the targeted domain record.",
    )

    changes: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Dictionary detailing previous and updated values.",
    )

    notes: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Investigator rationale, remarks, or justification.",
    )

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Additional forensic context or parameter metadata.",
    )

    @field_validator("actor")
    @classmethod
    def reject_blank_actor(cls, value: str) -> str:
        """Reject blank actor identifiers."""
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("Actor must not be blank")
        return value.strip()

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class DomainModel(BaseModel):
    """Base model for validated VIGIL domain objects."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        extra="forbid",
        str_strip_whitespace=True,
        validate_assignment=True,
        frozen=True,
    )

PATH - src\vigil\domain\camera.py
CODE:
"""Camera metadata schema."""

import math
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo, ZoneInfoNotFoundError

from pydantic import Field, field_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier


class Camera(DomainModel):
    """Represents a camera or recording source."""

    camera_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for this camera.",
    )

    name: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Human-readable name assigned to the camera.",
    )

    location_label: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional physical location, such as Parking Entrance.",
    )

    timezone: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional IANA timezone, such as Asia/Kolkata.",
    )

    clock_offset_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional correction in seconds applied to the camera's "
            "reported clock to estimate reference time."
        ),
    )

    @field_validator("timezone")
    @classmethod
    def validate_timezone(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
        """Reject unknown IANA timezone names."""
        if value is None:
            return None

        try:
            ZoneInfo(value)
        except (ZoneInfoNotFoundError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ValueError("Unknown IANA timezone.") from exc

        return value

    @field_validator("clock_offset_seconds")
    @classmethod
    def validate_clock_offset(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
        """Reject infinite and NaN clock offsets."""
        if value is not None and not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("Clock offset must be finite.")

        return value

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\domain\common.py
CODE:
"""Reusable types and validation rules for VIGIL domain schemas."""

from typing import Annotated

from pydantic import (
    AfterValidator,
    AwareDatetime,
    Field,
    StringConstraints,
)

Identifier = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(
        strip_whitespace=True,
        min_length=1,
    ),
]
"""A nonblank string identifier for a VIGIL object."""


Timestamp = AwareDatetime
"""A datetime that must include timezone information."""


ConfidenceScore = Annotated[
    float,
    Field(
        ge=0.0,
        le=1.0,
        allow_inf_nan=False,
    ),
]
"""An optional-to-use, normalized confidence score between 0 and 1."""


def validate_unique_reference_ids(values: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Reject blank or duplicate identifiers in a reference list."""
    normalized = [value.strip() for value in values]

    if any(not value for value in normalized):
        raise ValueError("Reference IDs must not be blank")

    if len(normalized) != len(set(normalized)):
        raise ValueError("Reference IDs must be unique")

    return normalized


ReferenceIds = Annotated[
    list[Identifier],
    AfterValidator(validate_unique_reference_ids),
]
"""A list of nonblank, unique object reference IDs."""

PATH - src\vigil\domain\coverage.py
CODE:
"""Domain schema for investigation coverage."""

from enum import Enum

from pydantic import Field, field_validator, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, ReferenceIds, Timestamp


class CoverageStatus(str, Enum):
    """Outcome of examining a source or time interval."""

    EXAMINED = "examined"
    PARTIAL = "partial"
    FAILED = "failed"
    NOT_EXAMINED = "not_examined"


class CoverageRecord(DomainModel):
    """Records the examination status of a source or interval."""

    coverage_id: Identifier = Field(min_length=1)
    investigation_id: Identifier = Field(min_length=1)
    source_reference_id: Identifier = Field(min_length=1)

    status: CoverageStatus

    interval_start: Timestamp | None = None
    interval_end: Timestamp | None = None
    examined_at: Timestamp | None = None

    notes: str | None = None
    artifact_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(default_factory=list)

    @field_validator("notes")
    @classmethod
    def reject_blank_notes(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
        """Reject blank notes when supplied."""
        if value is not None and not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("notes must not be blank")
        return value

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_interval_and_status(self) -> "CoverageRecord":
        """Validate interval order and examination timestamps."""
        if (self.interval_start is None) != (self.interval_end is None):
            raise ValueError(
                "interval_start and interval_end must be provided together"
            )

        if (
            self.interval_start is not None
            and self.interval_end is not None
            and self.interval_end < self.interval_start
        ):
            raise ValueError("interval_end must not precede interval_start")

        if self.status == CoverageStatus.EXAMINED and self.examined_at is None:
            raise ValueError("examined status requires examined_at")

        return self

PATH - src\vigil\domain\entity.py

"""Entity schema for detected or tracked objects."""

import math
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, field_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, ReferenceIds


class Entity(DomainModel):
    """Represents a detected or tracked entity in an investigation."""

    entity_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable identifier for this tracked entity.",
    )

    entity_type: Literal[
        "person",
        "vehicle",
        "animal",
        "object",
        "unknown",
    ] = Field(description="Category assigned to the detected or tracked entity.")

    label: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional human-readable label; not necessarily a verified identity."
        ),
    )

    associated_observation_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="IDs of observations associated with this entity.",
    )

    attributes: dict[str, str | int | float | bool | None] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Optional structured attributes, such as color or vehicle type.",
    )

    @field_validator("attributes")
    @classmethod
    def validate_attributes(
        cls,
        values: dict[str, str | int | float | bool | None],
    ) -> dict[str, str | int | float | bool | None]:
        """Reject blank attribute names and non-finite numeric values."""
        for key, value in values.items():
            if not key.strip():
                raise ValueError("Attribute names must not be blank.")
            if isinstance(value, float) and not math.isfinite(value):
                raise ValueError("Numeric attributes must be finite.")
        return values

from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, ReferenceIds, Timestamp


class Event(DomainModel):
    """Represents an occurrence supported by investigation evidence."""

    event_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for this event.",
    )

    event_type: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description=(
            "Category of occurrence, such as restricted_area_entry "
            "or vehicle_departure."
        ),
    )

    description: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Human-readable description of the occurrence.",
    )

    observation_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Observation IDs supporting this event.",
    )

    entity_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="Optional entity IDs associated with the event.",
    )

    status: Literal[
        "proposed",
        "under_review",
        "confirmed",
        "rejected",
    ] = Field(default="proposed", description="Current review state of the event.")

    start_time: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional timezone-aware estimated wall-clock start time.",
    )

    end_time: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional timezone-aware estimated wall-clock end time.",
    )

    start_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        ge=0.0,
        description="Optional footage-relative start offset in seconds.",
    )

    end_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        ge=0.0,
        description="Optional footage-relative end offset in seconds.",
    )

    reviewer: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Investigator identifier who reviewed or edited this event.",
    )

    review_notes: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Investigator notes or rationale recorded during review.",
    )

    reviewed_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Timezone-aware timestamp when review occurred.",
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_time_order(self) -> "Event":
        """Reject an end time earlier than the start time."""
        if (
            self.start_time is not None
            and self.end_time is not None
            and self.end_time < self.start_time
        ):
            raise ValueError("Event end time cannot precede start time.")
        if (
            self.start_seconds is not None
            and self.end_seconds is not None
            and self.end_seconds < self.start_seconds
        ):
            raise ValueError("Event end seconds cannot precede start seconds.")
        return self

"""Relationship schema between investigation events."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import ConfidenceScore, Identifier, Timestamp


class EventLink(DomainModel):
    """Represents a typed relationship between two distinct events."""

    event_link_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for this relationship.",
    )

    from_event_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="ID of the source event in the relationship.",
    )

    to_event_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="ID of the destination event in the relationship.",
    )

    relationship_type: Literal[
        "before",
        "after",
        "overlaps",
        "related_to",
        "possible_same_incident",
    ] = Field(description="Type of relationship between the two events.")

    rationale: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Explanation for recording this relationship.",
    )

    confidence: ConfidenceScore | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional producer-reported confidence score; "
            "not necessarily a calibrated probability."
        ),
    )

    created_at: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware time when the relationship was recorded.",
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_distinct_events(self) -> "EventLink":
        """Prevent an event from being linked to itself."""
        if self.from_event_id == self.to_event_id:
            raise ValueError("An event cannot be linked to itself.")
        return self

PATH - src\vigil\domain\media_asset.py
CODE:
"""Original media asset schema."""

import math
import re
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, field_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, Timestamp


class MediaAsset(DomainModel):
    """Represents an original video, audio, or image asset."""

    media_asset_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for the original media.",
    )

    media_type: Literal["video", "audio", "image"] = Field(
        description="Type of original media.",
    )

    storage_uri: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description=(
            "Location or storage key for the original media, "
            "such as a file URI or object-storage URI."
        ),
    )

    camera_id: Identifier | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional ID of the camera that produced this media.",
    )

    duration_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        gt=0,
        description=(
            "Optional media duration in seconds; omit when unknown "
            "or not applicable, such as for a still image."
        ),
    )

    recorded_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional timezone-aware wall-clock time corresponding "
            "to the beginning of the recording."
        ),
    )

    sha256: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional SHA-256 checksum of the original media, "
            "represented by exactly 64 hexadecimal characters."
        ),
    )

    width: int | None = Field(
        default=None,
        gt=0,
        description="Optional width in pixels for video or image media.",
    )

    height: int | None = Field(
        default=None,
        gt=0,
        description="Optional height in pixels for video or image media.",
    )

    fps: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        gt=0,
        description="Optional frame rate in frames per second for video media.",
    )

    audio_sample_rate: int | None = Field(
        default=None,
        gt=0,
        description="Optional audio sample rate in Hz for audio or video media.",
    )

    @field_validator("storage_uri")
    @classmethod
    def validate_storage_uri(cls, value: str) -> str:
        """Reject blank storage references."""
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("Storage URI must not be blank.")
        return value

    @field_validator("duration_seconds", "fps")
    @classmethod
    def validate_duration(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
        """Reject non-finite durations and frame rates."""
        if value is not None and not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("Value must be finite.")
        return value

    @field_validator("sha256")
    @classmethod
    def validate_sha256(cls, value: str | None) -> str | None:
        """Validate the optional SHA-256 checksum."""
        if value is not None and not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{64}", value):
            raise ValueError("SHA-256 must contain 64 hexadecimal characters.")
        return value

PATH - src\vigil\domain\source_reference.py
CODE:
"""Source evidence reference schema."""

import math

from pydantic import Field, field_validator, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, Timestamp


class SourceReference(DomainModel):
    """Identifies a precise interval in an original media asset."""

    media_asset_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="ID of the original media containing the evidence.",
    )

    start_seconds: float = Field(
        ge=0,
        description=(
            "Inclusive start position in seconds on the original media timeline."
        ),
    )

    end_seconds: float = Field(
        gt=0,
        description=(
            "Exclusive end position in seconds on the original media timeline."
        ),
    )

    source_timestamp: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Optional timezone-aware wall-clock timestamp corresponding "
            "to start_seconds; retain its timezone or normalize to UTC."
        ),
    )

    @field_validator("start_seconds", "end_seconds")
    @classmethod
    def validate_finite_time(cls, value: float) -> float:
        """Reject NaN and infinite timeline positions."""
        if not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("Timeline positions must be finite.")
        return value

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_interval(self) -> "SourceReference":
        """Require a positive-length half-open interval."""
        if self.end_seconds <= self.start_seconds:
            raise ValueError("End time must be greater than start time.")
        return self

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\logging_config.py
CODE:
"""Centralised logging configuration for VIGIL with ANSI colour coding.

Installs a consistent, timestamped, colour-coded log format across every module,
FastAPI access middleware, and uvicorn access/error loggers. HTTP response codes
are augmented with contextual status phrases (e.g., 200 OK, 404 NOT FOUND) and
color-coded by outcome severity.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import http
import logging
import logging.config
import os
import re
import sys
from typing import Any

# Enable ANSI escape sequences on Windows console
if sys.platform == "win32":
    try:
        import ctypes
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        # STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE = -11
        h_stdout = kernel32.GetStdHandle(-11)
        mode = ctypes.c_ulong()
        if kernel32.GetConsoleMode(h_stdout, ctypes.byref(mode)):
            # ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_PROCESSING = 0x0004
            kernel32.SetConsoleMode(h_stdout, mode.value | 0x0004)
        # STD_ERROR_HANDLE = -12
        h_stderr = kernel32.GetStdHandle(-12)
        if kernel32.GetConsoleMode(h_stderr, ctypes.byref(mode)):
            kernel32.SetConsoleMode(h_stderr, mode.value | 0x0004)
    except Exception:
        # Fallback to empty command enabling ANSI
        try:
            os.system("")
        except Exception:
            pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ANSI Colour Palette
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
RESET = "\033[0m"
BOLD = "\033[1m"
DIM = "\033[2m"

COLOR_DEBUG = "\033[36m"         # Cyan
COLOR_INFO = "\033[32m"          # Green
COLOR_WARNING = "\033[33m"       # Yellow
COLOR_ERROR = "\033[31m"         # Red
COLOR_CRITICAL = "\033[97;41m"   # Bold white on red

COLOR_HTTP_2XX = "\033[1;92m"    # Bold Bright Green
COLOR_HTTP_3XX = "\033[1;96m"    # Bold Bright Cyan
COLOR_HTTP_4XX = "\033[1;93m"    # Bold Bright Yellow
COLOR_HTTP_5XX = "\033[1;91m"    # Bold Bright Red

LEVEL_COLORS = {
    logging.DEBUG: COLOR_DEBUG,
    logging.INFO: COLOR_INFO,
    logging.WARNING: COLOR_WARNING,
    logging.ERROR: COLOR_ERROR,
    logging.CRITICAL: COLOR_CRITICAL,
}

# Pre-compile regex for HTTP status codes in messages
# Matches patterns like ' 200 ', ' 200 OK', ' 404 NOT FOUND', ' 404 Not Found', '" 200 '
HTTP_STATUS_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r'(?P<prefix>[\s"\'])'
    r'(?P<code>[1-5]\d{2})'
    r'(?:\s+(?P<phrase>[A-Za-z_\s\-]+?))?'
    r'(?P<suffix>[\s"\'\(\)\,\.\|]|$)',
)


def _get_status_phrase(code: int) -> str:
    """Retrieve standard uppercase HTTP reason phrase for status code."""
    try:
        return http.HTTPStatus(code).phrase.upper()
    except ValueError:
        return "RESPONSE"


def _colorize_http_status_match(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
    """Colorize an HTTP status code and ensure its contextual phrase is present."""
    prefix = match.group("prefix")
    code_str = match.group("code")
    raw_phrase = match.group("phrase")
    suffix = match.group("suffix")

    try:
        code_int = int(code_str)
    except ValueError:
        return match.group(0)

    # Determine standard phrase
    phrase = raw_phrase.strip().upper() if raw_phrase and len(raw_phrase.strip()) > 1 else _get_status_phrase(code_int)

    # Pick colour by status class
    if 200 <= code_int < 300:
        color = COLOR_HTTP_2XX
    elif 300 <= code_int < 400:
        color = COLOR_HTTP_3XX
    elif 400 <= code_int < 500:
        color = COLOR_HTTP_4XX
    elif 500 <= code_int < 600:
        color = COLOR_HTTP_5XX
    else:
        color = BOLD

    return f"{prefix}{color}{code_int} {phrase}{RESET}{suffix}"


class VigilColorizedFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    """Custom formatter preserving existing format with ANSI colour enhancements.

    Standard format:
      %(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s

    Features:
      - Colorized log levels (INFO, WARNING, ERROR, etc.)
      - Colorized HTTP status codes with output code context (e.g., 200 OK, 404 NOT FOUND)
      - Preserves clean alignment and exact field ordering
    """

    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        # Create a copy or handle record message
        orig_msg = record.getMessage()

        # Format asctime
        asctime = self.formatTime(record, self.datefmt)

        # Colorize levelname
        level_color = LEVEL_COLORS.get(record.levelno, "")
        raw_level = f"{record.levelname:<8}"
        if level_color:
            colored_level = f"{level_color}{raw_level}{RESET}"
        else:
            colored_level = raw_level

        # Augment HTTP status codes with context phrase and color in access logs / messages
        colored_msg = HTTP_STATUS_PATTERN.sub(_colorize_http_status_match, orig_msg)

        # Assemble format: %(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s
        formatted = f"{asctime} | {colored_level} | {record.name} | {colored_msg}"

        if record.exc_info:
            if not record.exc_text:
                record.exc_text = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
            formatted = f"{formatted}\n{record.exc_text}"
        if record.stack_info:
            formatted = f"{formatted}\n{self.formatStack(record.stack_info)}"

        return formatted


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def get_log_config(log_level: str = "INFO") -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a ``logging.config.dictConfig``-compatible configuration dict.

    The format preserves:
      * ISO-8601 timestamp (local time with timezone offset)
      * Log level (right-padded to 8 chars, colour coded)
      * Logger name
      * Message (with colour coded HTTP status output code context)
    """
    datefmt = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %z"

    return {
        "version": 1,
        "disable_existing_loggers": False,
        "formatters": {
            "timestamped": {
                "()": VigilColorizedFormatter,
                "datefmt": datefmt,
            },
        },
        "handlers": {
            "console": {
                "class": "logging.StreamHandler",
                "stream": "ext://sys.stdout",
                "formatter": "timestamped",
            },
        },
        "root": {
            "handlers": ["console"],
            "level": log_level.upper(),
        },
        # Ensure uvicorn's own loggers also use our formatter.
        "loggers": {
            "uvicorn": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": "INFO",
                "propagate": False,
            },
            "uvicorn.error": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": "INFO",
                "propagate": False,
            },
            "uvicorn.access": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": "INFO",
                "propagate": False,
            },
            "fastapi": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": "INFO",
                "propagate": False,
            },
            "vigil": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": log_level.upper(),
                "propagate": False,
            },
            "vigil.access": {
                "handlers": ["console"],
                "level": "INFO",
                "propagate": False,
            },
        },
    }


def configure_logging(log_level: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Install VIGIL's timestamped colour-coded logging configuration.

    Safe to call multiple times – subsequent calls are no-ops unless the
    level is explicitly changed.
    """
    from vigil.config import config  # local import avoids circular deps at module load

    level = log_level or config.log_level
    logging.config.dictConfig(get_log_config(level))

PATH - src\vigil\media\decoder.py
CODE:
"""Physical video and audio decoding, JPEG extraction, and WAV demuxing."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import wave
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import av
import cv2


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExtractedFrame:
    """Represents an extracted physical JPEG image frame."""

    frame_index: int
    timestamp_seconds: float
    file_path: str
    storage_uri: str
    sha256: str
    width: int
    height: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DemuxedAudio:
    """Represents a demuxed WAV audio file."""

    file_path: str
    storage_uri: str
    sha256: str
    duration_seconds: float
    sample_rate: int
    channels: int


class MediaDecoder:
    """Decodes video files (.mp4, .mkv, .avi) to JPEG frames and demuxes audio tracks to WAV."""

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
        """Compute SHA-256 checksum from bytes."""
        return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

    @classmethod
    def _compute_sha256_file(cls, path: Path) -> str:
        """Compute SHA-256 checksum from file."""
        hasher = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            while chunk := f.read(65536):
                hasher.update(chunk)
        return hasher.hexdigest()

    @classmethod
    def extract_frames(
        cls,
        file_path: str | Path,
        output_dir: str | Path,
        sample_interval_seconds: float = 2.0,
        start_seconds: float = 0.0,
        end_seconds: float | None = None,
        max_frames: int = 100,
        jpeg_quality: int = 95,
    ) -> list[ExtractedFrame]:
        """Decode video frames at intervals and write actual JPEG files to disk."""
        src_path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not src_path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Media file does not exist: {src_path}")

        out_path = Path(output_dir).resolve()
        out_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        extracted: list[ExtractedFrame] = []

        # Attempt extraction via PyAV
        try:
            with av.open(str(src_path)) as container:
                if len(container.streams.video) == 0:
                    return []

                stream = container.streams.video[0]
                time_base = float(stream.time_base) if stream.time_base else 1.0 / 24.0

                stream_duration = (
                    float(stream.duration * stream.time_base)
                    if stream.duration is not None and stream.time_base
                    else None
                )
                total_duration = stream_duration or (
                    float(container.duration) / float(av.time_base)
                    if container.duration
                    else 3600.0
                )

                stop_at = (
                    min(end_seconds, total_duration)
                    if end_seconds is not None
                    else total_duration
                )

                next_target_t = max(0.0, start_seconds)
                frame_idx = 0

                for frame in container.decode(stream):
                    if frame_idx >= max_frames:
                        break

                    pts = frame.pts
                    current_t = float(pts * time_base) if pts is not None else (frame_idx * sample_interval_seconds)

                    if current_t < next_target_t:
                        continue
                    if current_t > stop_at:
                        break

                    # Convert PyAV VideoFrame to numpy BGR for OpenCV JPEG encoding
                    img_bgr = frame.to_ndarray(format="bgr24")
                    h, w = img_bgr.shape[:2]

                    file_name = f"frame_{frame_idx:04d}.jpg"
                    jpeg_path = out_path / file_name

                    # Encode to JPEG
                    success, enc_buf = cv2.imencode(
                        ".jpg",
                        img_bgr,
                        [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), jpeg_quality],
                    )
                    if not success:
                        continue

                    raw_bytes = enc_buf.tobytes()
                    jpeg_path.write_bytes(raw_bytes)
                    sha256_sum = cls._compute_sha256_bytes(raw_bytes)

                    extracted.append(
                        ExtractedFrame(
                            frame_index=frame_idx,
                            timestamp_seconds=round(current_t, 3),
                            file_path=str(jpeg_path),
                            storage_uri=jpeg_path.as_uri(),
                            sha256=sha256_sum,
                            width=w,
                            height=h,
                        )
                    )

                    frame_idx += 1
                    next_target_t = current_t + sample_interval_seconds

            if extracted:
                return extracted
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
            # Fallback to OpenCV VideoCapture
            pass

        # OpenCV Fallback
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(src_path))
        if not cap.isOpened():
            return []

        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) or 24.0
        total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
        duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0

        stop_t = min(end_seconds, duration) if end_seconds is not None else duration
        next_t = max(0.0, start_seconds)
        frame_idx = 0

        while frame_idx < max_frames:
            target_frame_num = int(next_t * fps)
            if target_frame_num >= total_frames > 0:
                break

            cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, target_frame_num)
            ret, img_bgr = cap.read()
            if not ret or img_bgr is None:
                break

            actual_pos_msec = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC)
            actual_t = actual_pos_msec / 1000.0 if actual_pos_msec > 0 else next_t

            if actual_t > stop_t:
                break

            h, w = img_bgr.shape[:2]
            jpeg_path = out_path / f"frame_{frame_idx:04d}.jpg"
            success, enc_buf = cv2.imencode(
                ".jpg",
                img_bgr,
                [int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), jpeg_quality],
            )
            if success:
                raw_bytes = enc_buf.tobytes()
                jpeg_path.write_bytes(raw_bytes)
                sha256_sum = cls._compute_sha256_bytes(raw_bytes)

                extracted.append(
                    ExtractedFrame(
                        frame_index=frame_idx,
                        timestamp_seconds=round(actual_t, 3),
                        file_path=str(jpeg_path),
                        storage_uri=jpeg_path.as_uri(),
                        sha256=sha256_sum,
                        width=w,
                        height=h,
                    )
                )
                frame_idx += 1

            next_t += sample_interval_seconds

        cap.release()
        return extracted

    @classmethod
    def demux_audio_to_wav(
        cls,
        file_path: str | Path,
        output_path: str | Path,
        start_seconds: float = 0.0,
        end_seconds: float | None = None,
        target_sample_rate: int = 16000,
        mono: bool = False,
    ) -> DemuxedAudio:
        """Demux audio track from a video/audio file and write standard 16-bit PCM WAV."""
        src_path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not src_path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Media file does not exist: {src_path}")

        wav_path = Path(output_path).resolve()
        wav_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        layout = "mono" if mono else "stereo"
        channels = 1 if mono else 2

        with av.open(str(src_path)) as container:
            if len(container.streams.audio) == 0:
                raise ValueError(f"No audio stream found in media file: {src_path}")

            stream = container.streams.audio[0]
            resampler = av.AudioResampler(
                format="s16",
                layout=layout,
                rate=target_sample_rate,
            )

            time_base = float(stream.time_base) if stream.time_base else 1.0 / stream.rate
            audio_chunks: list[bytes] = []
            total_samples = 0

            for frame in container.decode(stream):
                pts = frame.pts
                frame_t = float(pts * time_base) if pts is not None else 0.0

                if end_seconds is not None and frame_t > end_seconds:
                    break
                if frame_t + (float(frame.samples) / stream.rate) < start_seconds:
                    continue

                resampled = resampler.resample(frame)
                if resampled:
                    for r_frame in resampled:
                        data = r_frame.to_ndarray().tobytes()
                        audio_chunks.append(data)
                        total_samples += r_frame.samples

        # Write WAV file using Python standard wave module
        with wave.open(str(wav_path), "wb") as wf:
            wf.setnchannels(channels)
            wf.setsampwidth(2)  # 16-bit
            wf.setframerate(target_sample_rate)
            wf.writeframes(b"".join(audio_chunks))

        duration_sec = total_samples / float(target_sample_rate) if target_sample_rate > 0 else 0.0
        sha256_sum = cls._compute_sha256_file(wav_path)

        return DemuxedAudio(
            file_path=str(wav_path),
            storage_uri=wav_path.as_uri(),
            sha256=sha256_sum,
            duration_seconds=round(duration_sec, 3),
            sample_rate=target_sample_rate,
            channels=channels,
        )

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\media\ingestion.py
CODE:
"""Media ingestion and indexing service connecting physical files to VIGIL repository."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.camera import Camera
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference
from vigil.media.decoder import MediaDecoder
from vigil.media.metadata import MediaMetadata, MediaMetadataExtractor
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IngestionResult:
    """Outcome of ingesting and indexing a physical media asset."""

    media_asset: MediaAsset
    camera: Camera | None
    frame_artifacts: list[Artifact]
    audio_artifacts: list[Artifact]

    @property
    def all_artifact_ids(self) -> list[str]:
        return [a.artifact_id for a in self.frame_artifacts + self.audio_artifacts]


class MediaIngestionService:
    """Service that validates media, extracts metadata, extracts frames & audio, and registers assets in repository."""

    def __init__(self, repository: InvestigationRepository) -> None:
        self.repository = repository

    def ingest_file(
        self,
        file_path: str | Path,
        media_asset_id: str | None = None,
        camera_id: str | None = None,
        camera_name: str | None = None,
        camera_location: str | None = None,
        camera_timezone: str | None = None,
        recorded_at: datetime | None = None,
        output_artifacts_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        sample_interval_seconds: float = 2.0,
        max_frames: int = 50,
        extract_audio: bool = True,
    ) -> IngestionResult:
        """Ingest a physical media file, populate MediaAsset and Camera, extract JPEG frames & WAV audio, and store in repository."""
        path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Media file does not exist: {path}")

        # 1. Metadata extraction
        metadata: MediaMetadata = MediaMetadataExtractor.extract(path)

        # 2. Camera setup if camera info provided
        created_camera: Camera | None = None
        if camera_id is not None:
            try:
                created_camera = self.repository.get_camera(camera_id)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                created_camera = Camera(
                    camera_id=camera_id,
                    name=camera_name or f"Camera {camera_id}",
                    location_label=camera_location,
                    timezone=camera_timezone,
                )
                self.repository.add_camera(created_camera)

        # 3. Create MediaAsset
        asset_id = media_asset_id or f"media-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        media_asset = MediaMetadataExtractor.to_media_asset(
            metadata=metadata,
            media_asset_id=asset_id,
            camera_id=camera_id,
            storage_uri=path.as_uri(),
            recorded_at=recorded_at,
        )
        self.repository.add_media_asset(media_asset)

        # 4. Storage directory for extracted artifacts
        if output_artifacts_dir is not None:
            art_dir = Path(output_artifacts_dir).resolve() / asset_id
        else:
            art_dir = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "vigil_artifacts" / asset_id
        art_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        frame_artifacts: list[Artifact] = []
        audio_artifacts: list[Artifact] = []

        # 5. Extract JPEG frames for visual detection
        if metadata.has_video:
            extracted_frames = MediaDecoder.extract_frames(
                file_path=path,
                output_dir=art_dir / "frames",
                sample_interval_seconds=sample_interval_seconds,
                start_seconds=0.0,
                end_seconds=metadata.duration_seconds if metadata.duration_seconds > 0 else None,
                max_frames=max_frames,
            )

            duration = metadata.duration_seconds or 10.0
            for ef in extracted_frames:
                frame_end = min(ef.timestamp_seconds + sample_interval_seconds, duration)
                if frame_end <= ef.timestamp_seconds:
                    frame_end = ef.timestamp_seconds + 0.1

                art_id = f"art-{asset_id}-frame-{ef.frame_index:04d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4]}"
                source_ref = SourceReference(
                    media_asset_id=asset_id,
                    start_seconds=ef.timestamp_seconds,
                    end_seconds=frame_end,
                )
                artifact = Artifact(
                    artifact_id=art_id,
                    artifact_type="frame",
                    media_type="image",
                    storage_uri=ef.storage_uri,
                    source_reference=source_ref,
                    sha256=ef.sha256,
                )
                self.repository.add_artifact(artifact)
                frame_artifacts.append(artifact)

        # 6. Demux audio track to .wav for acoustic transcription
        if metadata.has_audio and extract_audio:
            wav_out_path = art_dir / "audio" / "audio_track.wav"
            try:
                demuxed = MediaDecoder.demux_audio_to_wav(
                    file_path=path,
                    output_path=wav_out_path,
                    start_seconds=0.0,
                    end_seconds=metadata.duration_seconds if metadata.duration_seconds > 0 else None,
                )
                art_id = f"art-{asset_id}-audio-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
                source_ref = SourceReference(
                    media_asset_id=asset_id,
                    start_seconds=0.0,
                    end_seconds=demuxed.duration_seconds if demuxed.duration_seconds > 0 else 0.1,
                )
                audio_artifact = Artifact(
                    artifact_id=art_id,
                    artifact_type="audio_segment",
                    media_type="audio",
                    storage_uri=demuxed.storage_uri,
                    source_reference=source_ref,
                    sha256=demuxed.sha256,
                )
                self.repository.add_artifact(audio_artifact)
                audio_artifacts.append(audio_artifact)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                # If audio demuxing fails on non-standard stream, proceed with frames
                pass

        return IngestionResult(
            media_asset=media_asset,
            camera=created_camera,
            frame_artifacts=frame_artifacts,
            audio_artifacts=audio_artifacts,
        )

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\media\metadata.py
CODE:
"""Media metadata extraction and domain model populator for VIGIL."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import av
import cv2

from vigil.domain.camera import Camera
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MediaMetadata:
    """Rich container metadata extracted from a physical media file."""

    file_path: str
    storage_uri: str
    sha256: str
    duration_seconds: float
    media_type: Literal["video", "audio", "image"]
    file_size_bytes: int
    format_name: str | None = None
    width: int | None = None
    height: int | None = None
    fps: float | None = None
    video_codec: str | None = None
    frame_count: int | None = None
    audio_sample_rate: int | None = None
    audio_channels: int | None = None
    audio_codec: str | None = None
    has_video: bool = False
    has_audio: bool = False


class MediaMetadataExtractor:
    """Extracts physical media attributes using PyAV and OpenCV."""

    @staticmethod
    def compute_sha256(file_path: str | Path) -> str:
        """Compute the SHA-256 checksum of a file without loading it entirely into RAM."""
        path = Path(file_path)
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Media file not found: {path}")

        hasher = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            while chunk := f.read(65536):
                hasher.update(chunk)
        return hasher.hexdigest()

    @classmethod
    def extract(cls, file_path: str | Path) -> MediaMetadata:
        """Probe a media file and return structured MediaMetadata."""
        path = Path(file_path).resolve()
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Media file not found: {path}")

        file_size = path.stat().st_size
        sha256_sum = cls.compute_sha256(path)
        file_uri = path.as_uri()

        # Attempt extraction via PyAV first
        try:
            with av.open(str(path)) as container:
                format_name = container.format.name if container.format else None
                container_duration: float | None = None
                if container.duration is not None and container.duration > 0:
                    container_duration = float(container.duration) / float(av.time_base)

                has_video = len(container.streams.video) > 0
                has_audio = len(container.streams.audio) > 0

                width: int | None = None
                height: int | None = None
                fps: float | None = None
                video_codec: str | None = None
                frame_count: int | None = None
                video_duration: float | None = None

                if has_video:
                    v_stream = container.streams.video[0]
                    width = v_stream.width
                    height = v_stream.height
                    video_codec = v_stream.codec_context.name if v_stream.codec_context else None
                    if v_stream.average_rate is not None and float(v_stream.average_rate) > 0:
                        fps = float(v_stream.average_rate)
                    elif v_stream.rate is not None and float(v_stream.rate) > 0:
                        fps = float(v_stream.rate)

                    frame_count = v_stream.frames if v_stream.frames > 0 else None
                    if v_stream.duration is not None and v_stream.time_base is not None:
                        video_duration = float(v_stream.duration * v_stream.time_base)

                audio_sample_rate: int | None = None
                audio_channels: int | None = None
                audio_codec: str | None = None
                audio_duration: float | None = None

                if has_audio:
                    a_stream = container.streams.audio[0]
                    audio_sample_rate = a_stream.rate
                    audio_channels = a_stream.channels
                    audio_codec = a_stream.codec_context.name if a_stream.codec_context else None
                    if a_stream.duration is not None and a_stream.time_base is not None:
                        audio_duration = float(a_stream.duration * a_stream.time_base)

                # Determine effective duration
                duration = container_duration or video_duration or audio_duration
                if (duration is None or duration <= 0) and has_video and fps and frame_count:
                    duration = float(frame_count) / fps
                if duration is None or duration <= 0:
                    duration = 0.0

                # Determine media type
                if has_video:
                    media_type: Literal["video", "audio", "image"] = "video"
                elif has_audio:
                    media_type = "audio"
                elif path.suffix.lower() in {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".bmp", ".webp", ".tiff"}:
                    media_type = "image"
                else:
                    media_type = "video"

                return MediaMetadata(
                    file_path=str(path),
                    storage_uri=file_uri,
                    sha256=sha256_sum,
                    duration_seconds=round(duration, 3) if duration else 0.0,
                    media_type=media_type,
                    file_size_bytes=file_size,
                    format_name=format_name,
                    width=width,
                    height=height,
                    fps=round(fps, 3) if fps else None,
                    video_codec=video_codec,
                    frame_count=frame_count,
                    audio_sample_rate=audio_sample_rate,
                    audio_channels=audio_channels,
                    audio_codec=audio_codec,
                    has_video=has_video,
                    has_audio=has_audio,
                )
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
            # Fallback to OpenCV probing for images/videos
            pass

        # OpenCV Fallback
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
        if cap.isOpened():
            w = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
            h = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
            cap_fps = float(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS))
            frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
            duration = (frames / cap_fps) if cap_fps > 0 and frames > 0 else 0.0
            cap.release()

            return MediaMetadata(
                file_path=str(path),
                storage_uri=file_uri,
                sha256=sha256_sum,
                duration_seconds=round(duration, 3) if duration else 0.0,
                media_type="video",
                file_size_bytes=file_size,
                width=w if w > 0 else None,
                height=h if h > 0 else None,
                fps=round(cap_fps, 3) if cap_fps > 0 else None,
                frame_count=frames if frames > 0 else None,
                has_video=True,
                has_audio=False,
            )

        # Image check
        img = cv2.imread(str(path))
        if img is not None:
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            return MediaMetadata(
                file_path=str(path),
                storage_uri=file_uri,
                sha256=sha256_sum,
                duration_seconds=0.0,
                media_type="image",
                file_size_bytes=file_size,
                width=w,
                height=h,
                has_video=False,
                has_audio=False,
            )

        raise ValueError(f"Unable to decode or probe media file: {path}")

    @classmethod
    def to_media_asset(
        cls,
        metadata: MediaMetadata,
        media_asset_id: str | None = None,
        camera_id: str | None = None,
        storage_uri: str | None = None,
        recorded_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> MediaAsset:
        """Populate a valid MediaAsset domain model from extracted metadata."""
        asset_id = media_asset_id or f"media-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        uri = storage_uri or metadata.storage_uri
        duration = metadata.duration_seconds if metadata.duration_seconds > 0 else None
        if duration is not None and not math.isfinite(duration):
            duration = None

        return MediaAsset(
            media_asset_id=asset_id,
            media_type=metadata.media_type,
            storage_uri=uri,
            camera_id=camera_id,
            duration_seconds=duration,
            recorded_at=recorded_at,
            sha256=metadata.sha256,
            width=metadata.width,
            height=metadata.height,
            fps=metadata.fps,
            audio_sample_rate=metadata.audio_sample_rate,
        )

    @classmethod
    def create_camera(
        cls,
        camera_id: str,
        name: str,
        location_label: str | None = None,
        timezone: str | None = None,
        clock_offset_seconds: float | None = None,
    ) -> Camera:
        """Create a validated Camera domain model."""
        return Camera(
            camera_id=camera_id,
            name=name,
            location_label=location_label,
            timezone=timezone,
            clock_offset_seconds=clock_offset_seconds,
        )


def extract_media_metadata(file_path: str | Path) -> MediaMetadata:
    """Convenience function to extract metadata from a physical media file."""
    return MediaMetadataExtractor.extract(file_path)

PATH - src\vigil\media\slicer.py
CODE:
"""Incident clip cutting tool for extracting playable video sub-clips."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import av
import cv2


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SliceResult:
    """Represents an extracted incident video sub-clip."""

    clip_path: str
    storage_uri: str
    sha256: str
    start_seconds: float
    end_seconds: float
    duration_seconds: float
    width: int
    height: int
    fps: float


class ClipSlicer:
    """Slices video intervals ([start_seconds, end_seconds)) into playable MP4 clips."""

    @staticmethod
    def _compute_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
        hasher = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            while chunk := f.read(65536):
                hasher.update(chunk)
        return hasher.hexdigest()

    @classmethod
    def slice_clip(
        cls,
        source_path: str | Path,
        output_path: str | Path,
        start_seconds: float,
        end_seconds: float,
        padding_seconds: float = 0.0,
    ) -> SliceResult:
        """Cut a playable MP4 sub-clip between start_seconds and end_seconds."""
        src = Path(source_path).resolve()
        if not src.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source video file not found: {src}")

        if start_seconds < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"start_seconds must be non-negative, got {start_seconds}")
        if end_seconds <= start_seconds:
            raise ValueError(
                f"end_seconds ({end_seconds}) must be strictly greater than start_seconds ({start_seconds})"
            )

        out = Path(output_path).resolve()
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        eff_start = max(0.0, start_seconds - padding_seconds)
        eff_end = end_seconds + padding_seconds

        # Attempt slicing with PyAV for full audio+video encoding
        try:
            with av.open(str(src)) as in_container:
                if len(in_container.streams.video) == 0:
                    raise ValueError(f"No video stream found in {src}")

                in_video = in_container.streams.video[0]
                fps = (
                    float(in_video.average_rate)
                    if in_video.average_rate and float(in_video.average_rate) > 0
                    else 24.0
                )
                width = in_video.width
                height = in_video.height
                v_time_base = float(in_video.time_base) if in_video.time_base else 1.0 / fps

                has_audio = len(in_container.streams.audio) > 0
                in_audio = in_container.streams.audio[0] if has_audio else None

                with av.open(str(out), mode="w", format="mp4") as out_container:
                    out_video = out_container.add_stream("h264", rate=round(fps))
                    out_video.width = width
                    out_video.height = height
                    out_video.pix_fmt = "yuv420p"

                    out_audio = None
                    if in_audio is not None:
                        try:
                            out_audio = out_container.add_stream("aac", rate=in_audio.rate or 44100)
                            out_audio.format = "fltp"
                            out_audio.layout = "stereo" if in_audio.channels == 2 else "mono"
                        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                            out_audio = None

                    # Decode and write video
                    frame_count = 0
                    for frame in in_container.decode(in_video):
                        pts = frame.pts
                        t = float(pts * v_time_base) if pts is not None else (frame_count / fps)
                        if t < eff_start:
                            continue
                        if t >= eff_end:
                            break

                        # Re-encode video frame
                        for packet in out_video.encode(frame):
                            out_container.mux(packet)
                        frame_count += 1

                    for packet in out_video.encode():
                        out_container.mux(packet)

                    # Audio slicing if available
                    if in_audio is not None and out_audio is not None:
                        a_time_base = float(in_audio.time_base) if in_audio.time_base else 1.0 / 44100
                        try:
                            in_container.seek(0)
                            for a_frame in in_container.decode(in_audio):
                                a_pts = a_frame.pts
                                a_t = float(a_pts * a_time_base) if a_pts is not None else 0.0
                                if a_t < eff_start:
                                    continue
                                if a_t >= eff_end:
                                    break
                                for packet in out_audio.encode(a_frame):
                                    out_container.mux(packet)
                            for packet in out_audio.encode():
                                out_container.mux(packet)
                        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                            pass

                if out.is_file() and out.stat().st_size > 0:
                    sha256_sum = cls._compute_sha256(out)
                    actual_duration = frame_count / fps if fps > 0 else (eff_end - eff_start)
                    return SliceResult(
                        clip_path=str(out),
                        storage_uri=out.as_uri(),
                        sha256=sha256_sum,
                        start_seconds=round(eff_start, 3),
                        end_seconds=round(eff_end, 3),
                        duration_seconds=round(actual_duration, 3),
                        width=width,
                        height=height,
                        fps=round(fps, 3),
                    )
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
            # Fall back to OpenCV VideoWriter
            pass

        # OpenCV Fallback
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(src))
        if not cap.isOpened():
            raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to open video file with OpenCV: {src}")

        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) or 24.0
        width = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
        height = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
        total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))

        start_frame = int(eff_start * fps)
        end_frame = int(eff_end * fps)
        if total_frames > 0:
            end_frame = min(end_frame, total_frames)

        fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*"mp4v")
        writer = cv2.VideoWriter(str(out), fourcc, fps, (width, height))

        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, start_frame)
        written_frames = 0

        for _ in range(start_frame, end_frame):
            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if not ret or frame is None:
                break
            writer.write(frame)
            written_frames += 1

        writer.release()
        cap.release()

        if not out.is_file() or out.stat().st_size == 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Clip generation failed, empty output at {out}")

        sha256_sum = cls._compute_sha256(out)
        actual_duration = written_frames / fps if fps > 0 else (eff_end - eff_start)

        return SliceResult(
            clip_path=str(out),
            storage_uri=out.as_uri(),
            sha256=sha256_sum,
            start_seconds=round(eff_start, 3),
            end_seconds=round(eff_end, 3),
            duration_seconds=round(actual_duration, 3),
            width=width,
            height=height,
            fps=round(fps, 3),
        )

PATH - src\vigil\models\factory.py
CODE:
"""Factory for initializing LLM clients based on configuration."""

import logging
from typing import Literal

from vigil.config import VigilConfig, config
from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.models.gemini import GeminiClient
from vigil.models.groq import GroqClient
from vigil.models.local import OpenAICompatibleClient
from vigil.models.mock import MockLLMClient
from vigil.models.nvidia_nim import NvidiaNIMClient

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ProviderType = Literal["gemini", "groq", "ollama", "openrouter", "nvidia_nim", "mock"]


def get_llm_client(
    app_config: VigilConfig | None = None,
    override_provider: str | None = None,
) -> BaseLLMClient:
    """Create and return an LLM client based on configuration.

    Supported providers
    -------------------
    * ``nvidia_nim``  — NVIDIA Llama 3.2 11B Vision (VLM) +
                        Nemotron 3 Nano 30B A3B Reasoning (LLM) via NIM API.
    * ``gemini``      — Google Gemini 2.0 Flash (multimodal, free tier).
    * ``groq``        — Groq Cloud free tier (GPT-OSS 20B / Whisper).
    * ``ollama``      — Local Ollama server (any vision model).
    * ``openrouter``  — OpenRouter free-tier models.
    * ``mock``        — Offline deterministic mock (testing / CI).

    Args:
        app_config: Optional custom configuration. Defaults to system config.
        override_provider: Force a specific provider (e.g. ``"mock"``).

    Returns:
        Configured ``BaseLLMClient`` instance.
    """
    cfg = app_config or config
    provider = (override_provider or cfg.llm_provider).lower().strip()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # NVIDIA NIM — primary provider (Cosmos 3 Nano VLM + Nemotron 3 LLM)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if provider == "nvidia_nim":
        if not cfg.nvidia_api_key:
            logger.warning(
                "NVIDIA_API_KEY is not set. Falling back to MockLLMClient. "
                "Get a free key at https://build.nvidia.com and set "
                "NVIDIA_API_KEY in your .env file."
            )
            return MockLLMClient(model_name="fallback-mock-nvidia-nim")
        return NvidiaNIMClient(
            api_key=cfg.nvidia_api_key,
            vlm_model=cfg.nvidia_vlm_model,
            llm_model=cfg.nvidia_llm_model,
            vlm_base_url=cfg.nvidia_vlm_base_url,
            llm_base_url=cfg.nvidia_llm_base_url,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Google Gemini
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if provider == "gemini":
        if not cfg.gemini_api_key:
            logger.warning(
                "GEMINI_API_KEY is not set. Falling back to MockLLMClient. "
                "Set GEMINI_API_KEY in .env to use Google Gemini free tier."
            )
            return MockLLMClient(model_name="fallback-mock-gemini")
        return GeminiClient(
            api_key=cfg.gemini_api_key,
            model=cfg.gemini_model,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Groq Cloud
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if provider == "groq":
        if not cfg.groq_api_key:
            logger.warning(
                "GROQ_API_KEY is not set. Falling back to MockLLMClient. "
                "Set GROQ_API_KEY in .env to use Groq free tier."
            )
            return MockLLMClient(model_name="fallback-mock-groq")
        return GroqClient(
            api_key=cfg.groq_api_key,
            reasoning_model=cfg.groq_reasoning_model,
            vision_model=cfg.groq_vision_model,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Ollama (local / offline)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if provider == "ollama":
        return OpenAICompatibleClient(
            base_url=f"{cfg.ollama_base_url}/v1",
            api_key="ollama",
            model=cfg.ollama_model,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # OpenRouter free tier
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if provider == "openrouter":
        if not cfg.openrouter_api_key:
            logger.warning(
                "OPENROUTER_API_KEY is not set. Falling back to MockLLMClient."
            )
            return MockLLMClient(model_name="fallback-mock-openrouter")
        return OpenAICompatibleClient(
            base_url="https://openrouter.ai/api/v1",
            api_key=cfg.openrouter_api_key,
            model=cfg.openrouter_model,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fallback — deterministic mock (CI / testing / missing key)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    logger.warning(
        "Unknown provider '%s'. Falling back to MockLLMClient.", provider
    )
    return MockLLMClient()

PATH - src\vigil\models\gemini.py
CODE:
"""Google Gemini Free Tier client using standard library HTTP."""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
import logging
import random
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient, LLMResponse

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# HTTP status codes that are transient and worth retrying automatically.
_RETRYABLE_HTTP_CODES = {429, 500, 502, 503, 504}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global API-call throttle
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Gemini Free Tier allows 15 requests/minute per API key.  When multiple
# investigations run in parallel they all share the same key, so we cap the
# number of *concurrent* outbound API calls to avoid hammering the quota and
# triggering 503 / read-timeout cascades.
#
# The semaphore does NOT serialise calls entirely – up to MAX_CONCURRENT_CALLS
# requests may be in flight simultaneously.  Callers that cannot acquire the
# semaphore block until a slot opens, which gracefully queues work rather than
# failing fast.
_MAX_CONCURRENT_CALLS: int = 2  # tune via VIGIL_GEMINI_MAX_CONCURRENT env var
try:
    import os as _os
    _MAX_CONCURRENT_CALLS = int(_os.getenv("VIGIL_GEMINI_MAX_CONCURRENT", "2"))
except (TypeError, ValueError):
    pass

_api_semaphore: threading.Semaphore = threading.Semaphore(_MAX_CONCURRENT_CALLS)


def _ensure_base64_image(image_data: str) -> str:
    """Resolve file URI or local path into base64 string if necessary."""
    try:
        if image_data.startswith("file://"):
            parsed = urlparse(image_data)
            path_str = unquote(parsed.path)
            if path_str.startswith("/") and len(path_str) > 2 and path_str[2] == ":":
                path_str = path_str[1:]
            p = Path(path_str)
            if p.is_file():
                return base64.b64encode(p.read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
        elif Path(image_data).is_file():
            return base64.b64encode(Path(image_data).read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return image_data


class GeminiAPIError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the Gemini API returns an error or fails."""


class GeminiClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """Client for Google Gemini (Free Tier: Gemini 2.0 Flash / 1.5 Flash).

    Improvements over the original implementation
    --------------------------------------------
    * A **process-wide semaphore** caps concurrent outbound requests so that
      parallel investigations cannot flood the Free-Tier quota (15 RPM).
    * **Exponential back-off with full jitter** spreads retries across the
      time window instead of letting all threads wake up simultaneously.
    * **More retries by default** (5 instead of 3) to handle transient 503s
      and read timeouts that are common under load.
    * ``max_retries`` now applies uniformly to both HTTP errors *and* OS-level
      socket/timeout errors (previously OS errors stopped retrying after the
      retry counter was already 0).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        model: str = "gemini-2.0-flash",
        timeout_seconds: float = 120.0,
        max_retries: int = 5,
        retry_backoff_base: float = 2.0,
    ) -> None:
        if not api_key:
            raise ValueError("Gemini API key must be provided.")
        self.api_key = api_key.strip()
        self.model = model.strip()
        self.timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds
        self.max_retries = max_retries
        self.retry_backoff_base = retry_backoff_base

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _backoff_delay(self, attempt: int) -> float:
        """Return a jittered exponential back-off delay for the given attempt number.

        Using *full jitter* (AWS recommendation) prevents the thundering-herd
        problem when multiple threads all hit a rate-limit at the same time.

        ``attempt`` is 1-based (first retry = attempt 1).
        """
        cap = 60.0  # never sleep more than 60 s
        base_delay = min(cap, self.retry_backoff_base ** attempt)
        return random.uniform(0.0, base_delay)  # noqa: S311

    def _call_api(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        url = (
            f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/"
            f"{self.model}:generateContent?key={self.api_key}"
        )
        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")

        last_exc: Exception | None = None
        resp_data: dict[str, Any] = {}

        # Acquire the global semaphore *before* entering the retry loop so
        # that only MAX_CONCURRENT_CALLS threads are ever speaking to the API
        # at once.  The semaphore is released after the entire retry sequence
        # (success or final failure).
        with _api_semaphore:
            for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
                if attempt > 0:
                    delay = self._backoff_delay(attempt)
                    logger.warning(
                        "GeminiClient: attempt %d/%d failed (%s); retrying in %.1fs",
                        attempt,
                        self.max_retries,
                        last_exc,
                        delay,
                    )
                    time.sleep(delay)

                req = urllib.request.Request(
                    url,
                    data=data,
                    headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
                    method="POST",
                )
                try:
                    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout_seconds) as resp:
                        resp_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    break  # success — exit retry loop
                except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
                    err_body = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                    if exc.code in _RETRYABLE_HTTP_CODES and attempt < self.max_retries:
                        last_exc = RuntimeError(f"HTTP Error {exc.code}: {exc.reason}")
                        continue
                    raise GeminiAPIError(
                        f"Gemini API request failed with status {exc.code}: {err_body}"
                    ) from exc
                except OSError as exc:
                    # Covers socket.timeout, TimeoutError, ConnectionResetError, etc.
                    last_exc = exc
                    if attempt < self.max_retries:
                        continue
                    raise GeminiAPIError(f"Gemini API request failed: {exc}") from exc
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise GeminiAPIError(f"Gemini API request failed: {exc}") from exc

        try:
            candidates = resp_data.get("candidates", [])
            if not candidates:
                raise GeminiAPIError("Gemini API returned no candidates.")
            content_parts = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [])
            text_parts = [
                part.get("text", "") for part in content_parts if "text" in part
            ]
            return "".join(text_parts)
        except (KeyError, IndexError) as exc:
            raise GeminiAPIError(
                f"Failed to parse Gemini API response: {resp_data}"
            ) from exc

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API (implements BaseLLMClient)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def generate_text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "contents": [
                {
                    "parts": [{"text": prompt}],
                }
            ],
            "generationConfig": {},
        }

        if system_instruction:
            payload["systemInstruction"] = {
                "parts": [{"text": system_instruction}],
            }

        if json_mode:
            payload["generationConfig"]["responseMimeType"] = "application/json"

        content = self._call_api(payload)
        return LLMResponse(content=content, model_name=self.model)

    def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data: str,
        mime_type: str = "image/jpeg",
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        parts: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            {"text": prompt},
            {
                "inlineData": {
                    "mimeType": mime_type,
                    "data": _ensure_base64_image(image_data),
                }
            },
        ]

        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "contents": [
                {
                    "parts": parts,
                }
            ],
            "generationConfig": {},
        }

        if system_instruction:
            payload["systemInstruction"] = {
                "parts": [{"text": system_instruction}],
            }

        if json_mode:
            payload["generationConfig"]["responseMimeType"] = "application/json"

        content = self._call_api(payload)
        return LLMResponse(content=content, model_name=self.model)
