"""Groq Cloud Free Tier client using standard library HTTP."""

import base64
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse


def _ensure_base64_image(image_data: str) -> str:
    """Resolve file URI or local path into base64 string if necessary."""
    try:
        if image_data.startswith("file://"):
            parsed = urlparse(image_data)
            path_str = unquote(parsed.path)
            if path_str.startswith("/") and len(path_str) > 2 and path_str[2] == ":":
                path_str = path_str[1:]
            p = Path(path_str)
            if p.is_file():
                return base64.b64encode(p.read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
        elif Path(image_data).is_file():
            return base64.b64encode(Path(image_data).read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return image_data

import json
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from typing import Any

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient, LLMResponse

# Cloudflare (error code 1010) blocks requests with the default Python-urllib
# User-Agent. Use a browser-like UA so the Groq API edge passes the request.
_USER_AGENT = (
    "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) "
    "AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) "
    "Chrome/124.0.0.0 Safari/537.36"
)


class GroqAPIError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when Groq API returns an error."""


class GroqClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """Client for Groq Cloud Free Tier (GPT-OSS 20B reasoning, Whisper audio)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        reasoning_model: str = "llama-3.3-70b-versatile",
        vision_model: str = "llama-3.2-11b-vision-preview",
        timeout_seconds: float = 60.0,
    ) -> None:
        if not api_key:
            raise ValueError("Groq API key must be provided.")
        self.api_key = api_key.strip()
        self.reasoning_model = reasoning_model.strip()
        self.vision_model = vision_model.strip()
        self.timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds

    def _call_completions(
        self, model: str, messages: list[dict[str, Any]], json_mode: bool
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        url = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions"
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": 0.1,
        }
        if json_mode:
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            data=data,
            headers={
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}",
                "User-Agent": _USER_AGENT,
            },
            method="POST",
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout_seconds) as resp:
                resp_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            err_body = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            raise GroqAPIError(f"Groq API error {exc.code}: {err_body}") from exc
        except Exception as exc:
            raise GroqAPIError(f"Groq API request failed: {exc}") from exc

        choices = resp_data.get("choices", [])
        if not choices:
            raise GroqAPIError("Groq API returned no completion choices.")

        content = choices[0].get("message", {}).get("content", "")
        usage = resp_data.get("usage", {})
        return LLMResponse(
            content=content,
            model_name=model,
            prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens"),
            completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens"),
        )

    def generate_text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        return self._call_completions(self.reasoning_model, messages, json_mode)

    def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data: str,
        mime_type: str = "image/jpeg",
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        b64_data = _ensure_base64_image(image_data)
        image_url = (
            f"data:{mime_type};base64,{b64_data}"
            if not image_data.startswith("http")
            else image_data
        )
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append(
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": [
                    {"type": "text", "text": prompt},
                    {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_url}},
                ],
            }
        )
        return self._call_completions(self.vision_model, messages, json_mode)

"""OpenAI-compatible client for Ollama, OpenRouter, and local models."""

import json
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from typing import Any

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient, LLMResponse


class LocalAPIError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when an OpenAI-compatible API returns an error."""


class OpenAICompatibleClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """Client for Ollama, OpenRouter, or other OpenAI-compatible endpoints."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434/v1",
        api_key: str | None = None,
        model: str = "llama3.2-vision",
        timeout_seconds: float = 60.0,
    ) -> None:
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.api_key = api_key
        self.model = model.strip()
        self.timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds

    def _call_completions(
        self, messages: list[dict[str, Any]], json_mode: bool
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": 0.1,
        }
        if json_mode:
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        if self.api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.api_key}"

        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            data=data,
            headers=headers,
            method="POST",
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout_seconds) as resp:
                resp_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            err_body = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            raise LocalAPIError(
                f"API request failed with {exc.code}: {err_body}"
            ) from exc
        except Exception as exc:
            raise LocalAPIError(f"API request failed: {exc}") from exc

        choices = resp_data.get("choices", [])
        if not choices:
            raise LocalAPIError("API returned no completion choices.")

        content = choices[0].get("message", {}).get("content", "")
        usage = resp_data.get("usage", {})
        return LLMResponse(
            content=content,
            model_name=self.model,
            prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens"),
            completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens"),
        )

    def generate_text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        return self._call_completions(messages, json_mode)

    def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data: str,
        mime_type: str = "image/jpeg",
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        image_url = (
            f"data:{mime_type};base64,{image_data}"
            if not image_data.startswith("http")
            else image_data
        )
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append(
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": [
                    {"type": "text", "text": prompt},
                    {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_url}},
                ],
            }
        )
        return self._call_completions(messages, json_mode)

"""High-fidelity mock LLM client for testing and offline forensic analysis."""

import json

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient, LLMResponse


class MockLLMClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """Deterministic and context-aware mock LLM for offline development and testing."""

    def __init__(self, model_name: str = "mock-forensic-model") -> None:
        self.model_name = model_name
        self.call_count = 0

    def generate_text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        self.call_count += 1
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()

        if "plan" in prompt_lower or "strategy" in prompt_lower:
            response_obj = {
                "phases": [
                    {
                        "phase_name": "frame_sampling",
                        "objective": "Extract key frames and interval samples",
                        "sample_rate_seconds": 2.0,
                    },
                    {
                        "phase_name": "visual_detection",
                        "objective": "Detect persons, vehicles, and objects",
                    },
                    {
                        "phase_name": "event_correlation",
                        "objective": "Synthesize chronological events and link occurrences",
                    },
                    {
                        "phase_name": "evidence_assessment",
                        "objective": "Assess evidence quality and uncertainty",
                    },
                    {
                        "phase_name": "report_generation",
                        "objective": "Generate final forensic findings report",
                    },
                ]
            }
        elif "report" in prompt_lower or "summary" in prompt_lower:
            response_obj = {
                "title": "Forensic Video Analysis Findings",
                "summary": (
                    "Forensic examination identified unauthorized individual entry at the perimeter "
                    "followed by vehicle arrival. High quality evidence with verified timestamps."
                ),
                "key_findings": [
                    "Unauthorized subject entered through perimeter gate.",
                    "White sedan observed in perimeter zone at t=14.0s.",
                ],
            }
        elif "correlat" in prompt_lower or "event" in prompt_lower:
            response_obj = {
                "events": [
                    {
                        "event_type": "restricted_area_entry",
                        "description": "Subject observed entering restricted area.",
                        "confidence": 0.92,
                    }
                ],
                "links": [
                    {
                        "relationship_type": "before",
                        "rationale": "Perimeter breach observed prior to vehicle arrival.",
                        "confidence": 0.88,
                    }
                ],
            }
        elif "assess" in prompt_lower:
            response_obj = {
                "rating": "high",
                "assessment_type": "evidence_quality",
                "rationale": "High resolution visual evidence with clear unobstructed visibility.",
            }
        else:
            response_obj = {
                "findings": "Standard forensic detection completed.",
                "confidence": 0.85,
            }

        content = (
            json.dumps(response_obj)
            if json_mode
            else str(response_obj.get("summary", response_obj))
        )
        return LLMResponse(content=content, model_name=self.model_name)

    def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data: str,
        mime_type: str = "image/jpeg",
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        self.call_count += 1
        response_obj = {
            "observations": [
                {
                    "observation_type": "person_detection",
                    "description": "Adult male in dark jacket and denim trousers walking eastward.",
                    "confidence": 0.94,
                    "entity": {
                        "entity_type": "person",
                        "label": "Suspect-1",
                        "attributes": {
                            "clothing_upper": "dark jacket",
                            "clothing_lower": "denim trousers",
                        },
                    },
                },
                {
                    "observation_type": "vehicle_detection",
                    "description": "White 4-door sedan stationary near loading bay.",
                    "confidence": 0.89,
                    "entity": {
                        "entity_type": "vehicle",
                        "label": "Vehicle-1",
                        "attributes": {"color": "white", "vehicle_type": "sedan"},
                    },
                },
            ]
        }
        content = json.dumps(response_obj)
        return LLMResponse(content=content, model_name=self.model_name)

PATH - src\vigil\models\nvidia_nim.py
CODE:
"""NVIDIA NIM client for hosted VLM and LLM inference.

Dual-model design
-----------------
* **VLM** (visual tasks):  ``meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct``
  – Cosmos 3 Nano Reasoner is optimised for structured physical-scene reasoning
  from images/video frames; used by ``analyze_image``.
  Endpoint: ``https://ai.api.nvidia.com/v1``

* **LLM** (text reasoning):  ``nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning``
  – Nemotron 3 Nano 30B A3B (Reasoning) excels at timeline reconstruction,
  investigator Q&A, and structured report generation; used by ``generate_text``.
  Endpoint: ``https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1``

Note on endpoints
-----------------
NVIDIA operates two base URLs:
* ``integrate.api.nvidia.com`` — LLM / text models (Nemotron, Llama, Mistral…)
* ``ai.api.nvidia.com``        — VLM / multimodal models (Cosmos Reasoner…)

The client automatically routes each call to the correct base URL so callers
only need one API key.

Rate-limiting / reliability
---------------------------
A process-wide **global lock** (``threading.Lock``) serialises all outbound
calls to a single in-flight request at a time, preventing the free-tier
"Worker local total request limit" exhaustion (``ResourceExhausted 503``).

After every successful call a mandatory inter-request cool-down is observed
(``VIGIL_NVIDIA_INTER_REQUEST_DELAY`` env var, default 2 s) to keep the
per-minute request rate well under quota.

All transient HTTP errors (429, 500–504) and socket-level failures trigger
exponential back-off with full jitter (up to ``_MAX_BACKOFF_SECONDS``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import json
import logging
import os
import random
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient, LLMResponse

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Endpoint routing — NVIDIA uses two separate base URLs by model family
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LLM / text models  (Nemotron, Llama, Mistral …)
NVIDIA_LLM_BASE_URL = "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"
# VLM / multimodal models  (Cosmos Reasoner, NVLM …)
NVIDIA_VLM_BASE_URL = "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1"

# Default model identifiers (can be overridden via env / constructor)
DEFAULT_VLM_MODEL = "meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct"
DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL = "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"

# HTTP status codes that are transient and worth retrying automatically
_RETRYABLE_HTTP_CODES: frozenset[int] = frozenset({429, 500, 502, 503, 504})

# Maximum back-off ceiling (seconds)
_MAX_BACKOFF_SECONDS: float = 60.0

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Global call serialiser + inter-request cool-down
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The NVIDIA NIM free-tier has a very small worker pool (16 slots).  Sending
# even two requests simultaneously reliably triggers:
#   ResourceExhausted: Worker local total request limit reached (16/16)
#
# Strategy:
#   1. A process-wide Lock serialises ALL outbound calls — only one request
#      is ever in-flight at a time regardless of threading.
#   2. After every successful call we sleep for INTER_REQUEST_DELAY seconds
#      before releasing the lock, spreading calls across the minute window.
#   3. On 503/429 we apply full-jitter exponential back-off starting from a
#      MINIMUM of 5 s so we give the worker pool time to drain.

_api_lock: threading.Lock = threading.Lock()

_INTER_REQUEST_DELAY: float = 2.0  # seconds between requests (success path)
try:
    _INTER_REQUEST_DELAY = float(os.getenv("VIGIL_NVIDIA_INTER_REQUEST_DELAY", "2.0"))
except (TypeError, ValueError):
    pass

_MIN_RETRY_DELAY: float = 5.0  # minimum back-off on first retry


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _ensure_base64_image(image_data: str) -> str:
    """Resolve a file URI or local filesystem path to a base64-encoded string.

    If ``image_data`` is already a base64 string (or an ``http(s)://`` URL),
    it is returned unchanged.
    """
    try:
        if image_data.startswith("file://"):
            parsed = urlparse(image_data)
            path_str = unquote(parsed.path)
            # On Windows the path looks like /C:/...; strip the leading slash.
            if path_str.startswith("/") and len(path_str) > 2 and path_str[2] == ":":
                path_str = path_str[1:]
            p = Path(path_str)
            if p.is_file():
                return base64.b64encode(p.read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
        elif Path(image_data).is_file():
            return base64.b64encode(Path(image_data).read_bytes()).decode("utf-8")
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pass
    return image_data


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Error type
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class NvidiaNIMError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the NVIDIA NIM API returns an error or the request fails."""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Client
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class NvidiaNIMClient(BaseLLMClient):
    """OpenAI-compatible client for NVIDIA NIM hosted endpoints.

    Routes calls to the correct NVIDIA base URL by model type:
    * LLM text calls  → ``integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1``
    * VLM image calls → ``ai.api.nvidia.com/v1``

    A process-wide lock serialises all outbound requests to prevent the
    free-tier worker-pool exhaustion (``ResourceExhausted: Worker local total
    request limit reached``).  A mandatory inter-request delay is observed
    after each successful call to spread load across the RPM window.

    Parameters
    ----------
    api_key:
        Your ``nvapi-*`` key from https://build.nvidia.com.
    vlm_model:
        Model used for image/video-frame analysis (``analyze_image``).
        Defaults to ``meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct``.
    llm_model:
        Model used for text generation and reasoning (``generate_text``).
        Defaults to ``nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning``.
    base_url:
        Deprecated — kept for API compatibility but ignored; each model
        family is routed to its own NVIDIA endpoint automatically.
    timeout_seconds:
        Per-request socket timeout in seconds.
    max_retries:
        Maximum number of retry attempts for transient failures.
    retry_backoff_base:
        Base for exponential back-off calculation (``base ** attempt``).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        vlm_model: str = DEFAULT_VLM_MODEL,
        llm_model: str = DEFAULT_LLM_MODEL,
        base_url: str = NVIDIA_LLM_BASE_URL,  # kept for compat; auto-routed
        vlm_base_url: str | None = None,
        llm_base_url: str | None = None,
        timeout_seconds: float = 120.0,
        max_retries: int = 5,
        retry_backoff_base: float = 2.0,
    ) -> None:
        if not api_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "NVIDIA NIM API key must be provided. "
                "Get a free key at https://build.nvidia.com."
            )
        self.api_key = api_key.strip()
        self.vlm_model = vlm_model.strip()
        self.llm_model = llm_model.strip()
        # Allow per-instance base URL override (reads from env/config)
        import os as _os
        self._llm_base_url = (
            llm_base_url
            or _os.getenv("NVIDIA_LLM_BASE_URL")
            or NVIDIA_LLM_BASE_URL
        )
        self._vlm_base_url = (
            vlm_base_url
            or _os.getenv("NVIDIA_VLM_BASE_URL")
            or NVIDIA_VLM_BASE_URL
        )
        self.timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds
        self.max_retries = max_retries
        self.retry_backoff_base = retry_backoff_base

        logger.info(
            "NvidiaNIMClient initialised — LLM: %s [%s] | VLM: %s [%s]",
            self.llm_model,
            self._llm_base_url,
            self.vlm_model,
            self._vlm_base_url,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _backoff_delay(self, attempt: int) -> float:
        """Return a jittered exponential back-off delay (full-jitter strategy).

        ``attempt`` is 1-based (first retry = attempt 1).
        Always at least ``_MIN_RETRY_DELAY`` seconds to give the NIM worker
        pool time to drain, never exceeds ``_MAX_BACKOFF_SECONDS``.
        """
        cap = _MAX_BACKOFF_SECONDS
        raw = min(cap, self.retry_backoff_base ** attempt)
        # Clamp lower bound to MIN_RETRY_DELAY so we never hammer a saturated pool
        lower = max(_MIN_RETRY_DELAY, raw * 0.5)
        return random.uniform(lower, raw)  # noqa: S311

    def _call_completions(
        self,
        model: str,
        base_url: str,
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
        json_mode: bool = False,
        temperature: float = 0.1,
        max_tokens: int = 2048,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Send a chat-completions request to a NVIDIA NIM endpoint.

        All calls are serialised through ``_api_lock`` (one in-flight request
        at a time) and separated by ``_INTER_REQUEST_DELAY`` seconds on
        success to prevent ResourceExhausted / worker-pool exhaustion.

        Args:
            model: The model identifier string (VLM or LLM).
            base_url: The NIM base URL for this model family.
            messages: OpenAI-format message list.
            json_mode: Whether to request structured JSON output.
            temperature: Sampling temperature (lower = more deterministic).
            max_tokens: Maximum tokens to generate in the response.

        Returns:
            Parsed ``LLMResponse`` with content and token counts.

        Raises:
            NvidiaNIMError: On unrecoverable API or network failures.
        """
        url = f"{base_url.rstrip('/')}/chat/completions"

        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "top_p": 0.7,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
            "stream": False,
        }
        if json_mode:
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")

        last_exc: Exception | None = None
        resp_data: dict[str, Any] = {}

        # Serialise ALL outbound calls through the global lock.
        # One request in-flight at a time prevents worker-pool exhaustion.
        with _api_lock:
            for attempt in range(self.max_retries + 1):
                if attempt > 0:
                    delay = self._backoff_delay(attempt)
                    logger.warning(
                        "NvidiaNIMClient: attempt %d/%d failed (%s); retrying in %.1fs",
                        attempt,
                        self.max_retries,
                        last_exc,
                        delay,
                    )
                    time.sleep(delay)

                req = urllib.request.Request(
                    url,
                    data=data,
                    headers={
                        "Content-Type": "application/json",
                        "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}",
                        "Accept": "application/json",
                    },
                    method="POST",
                )

                try:
                    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout_seconds) as resp:
                        resp_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    # Mandatory cool-down between successful requests
                    if _INTER_REQUEST_DELAY > 0:
                        time.sleep(_INTER_REQUEST_DELAY)
                    break  # success — exit retry loop
                except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
                    err_body = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                    if exc.code in _RETRYABLE_HTTP_CODES and attempt < self.max_retries:
                        last_exc = RuntimeError(
                            f"HTTP {exc.code}: {exc.reason} — {err_body[:200]}"
                        )
                        continue
                    raise NvidiaNIMError(
                        f"NVIDIA NIM API error {exc.code}: {err_body}"
                    ) from exc
                except OSError as exc:
                    # Covers socket.timeout, TimeoutError, ConnectionResetError, etc.
                    last_exc = exc
                    if attempt < self.max_retries:
                        continue
                    raise NvidiaNIMError(
                        f"NVIDIA NIM network failure: {exc}"
                    ) from exc
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise NvidiaNIMError(
                        f"NVIDIA NIM unexpected error: {exc}"
                    ) from exc

        # Parse response
        choices = resp_data.get("choices", [])
        if not choices:
            raise NvidiaNIMError(
                f"NVIDIA NIM API returned no completion choices. "
                f"Raw response: {resp_data}"
            )

        content = choices[0].get("message", {}).get("content", "")
        usage = resp_data.get("usage", {})
        return LLMResponse(
            content=content,
            model_name=model,
            prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens"),
            completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens"),
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API — implements BaseLLMClient
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def generate_text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Generate text or structured JSON via Nemotron 3 Nano 30B A3B (Reasoning).

        Routes to ``integrate.api.nvidia.com`` (LLM endpoint).

        Used for: timeline reconstruction, investigator Q&A, report generation,
        and any reasoning task that does NOT require visual input.

        Args:
            prompt: The user-facing prompt.
            system_instruction: Optional system/role context for the model.
            json_mode: When True, instructs the model to return valid JSON.

        Returns:
            ``LLMResponse`` with the generated text and token usage.
        """
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        return self._call_completions(
            self.llm_model,
            self._llm_base_url,
            messages,
            json_mode=json_mode,
        )

    def analyze_image(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        image_data: str,
        mime_type: str = "image/jpeg",
        system_instruction: str | None = None,
        json_mode: bool = False,
    ) -> LLMResponse:
        """Analyse a video frame or image via NVIDIA Cosmos 3 Nano Reasoner (VLM).

        Routes to ``ai.api.nvidia.com`` (VLM/multimodal endpoint).

        Cosmos 3 Nano Reasoner is designed for structured physical-scene
        understanding: interpreting actions, object interactions, spatial
        relationships, and temporal event sequences from visual input.

        Args:
            prompt: Analytical question or instruction for the scene.
            image_data: Base64-encoded image bytes, a local file path,
                        a ``file://`` URI, or a public ``https://`` URL.
            mime_type: MIME type of the image (default ``image/jpeg``).
            system_instruction: Optional system/role context for the model.
            json_mode: When True, instructs the model to return valid JSON.

        Returns:
            ``LLMResponse`` with the visual analysis and token usage.
        """
        # Resolve file path / URI → base64; leave http(s) URLs untouched.
        if image_data.startswith("http://") or image_data.startswith("https://"):
            image_url_value = image_data
        else:
            b64 = _ensure_base64_image(image_data)
            image_url_value = f"data:{mime_type};base64,{b64}"

        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        if system_instruction:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_instruction})
        messages.append(
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": [
                    {"type": "text", "text": prompt},
                    {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": image_url_value}},
                ],
            }
        )
        return self._call_completions(
            self.vlm_model,
            self._vlm_base_url,
            messages,
            json_mode=json_mode,
        )

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATH - src\vigil\orchestration\executor.py
CODE:
"""Synchronous task execution and worker routing."""

from typing import Protocol

from vigil.orchestration.task_types import (
    TaskRequest,
    TaskResult,
    TaskStatus,
)


class TaskWorker(Protocol):
    """Contract for an object capable of executing a task."""

    def execute(self, request: TaskRequest) -> TaskResult:
        """Execute a task and return its result."""
        ...


class ExecutorError(Exception):
    """Base exception for executor errors."""


class UnknownTaskTypeError(ExecutorError):
    """Raised when no worker is registered for a task type."""


class TaskResultContractError(ExecutorError):
    """Raised when a worker violates the result contract."""


class WorkerExecutionError(ExecutorError):
    """Raised when a worker encounters an expected execution failure."""


class TaskExecutor:
    """Route task requests to registered workers."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._workers: dict[str, TaskWorker] = {}

    def register(self, task_type: str, worker: TaskWorker) -> None:
        """Register one worker for a task type."""
        normalized_type = task_type.strip()

        if not normalized_type:
            raise ValueError("task_type must not be blank")

        if normalized_type in self._workers:
            raise ValueError(
                f"A worker is already registered for task type '{normalized_type}'"
            )

        self._workers[normalized_type] = worker

    def execute(self, request: TaskRequest) -> TaskResult:
        """Execute a request using its registered worker."""
        worker = self._workers.get(request.task_type)

        if worker is None:
            raise UnknownTaskTypeError(
                f"No worker registered for task type '{request.task_type}'"
            )

        result = worker.execute(request)

        if not isinstance(result, TaskResult):
            raise TaskResultContractError("Worker must return a TaskResult")

        if result.task_id != request.task_id:
            raise TaskResultContractError(
                "Worker result task_id must match request task_id"
            )

        if result.status in {TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING}:
            raise TaskResultContractError(
                "Synchronous workers must return a final task status"
            )

        return result

PATH - src\vigil\orchestration\investigation_state.py
CODE:
"""Runtime state of a VIGIL investigation workflow."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum

from pydantic import Field, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import Identifier, ReferenceIds, Timestamp


class InvestigationStatus(str, Enum):
    """Lifecycle status of an investigation workflow."""

    CREATED = "created"
    RUNNING = "running"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"


class InvestigationState(DomainModel):
    """Tracks progress and outputs of an investigation workflow."""

    investigation_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable identifier for the investigation.",
    )

    status: InvestigationStatus = Field(
        default=InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
        description="Current lifecycle status of the investigation.",
    )

    task_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="IDs of tasks associated with this investigation.",
    )

    observation_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="IDs of observations produced during the investigation.",
    )

    event_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="IDs of events identified during the investigation.",
    )

    started_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional timezone-aware time when the investigation began.",
    )

    updated_at: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware time when the state was last updated.",
    )

    completed_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "Timezone-aware time when the investigation reached a terminal state."
        ),
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_state(self) -> "InvestigationState":
        """Ensure investigation status and timestamps are consistent."""
        if (
            self.status
            in {
                InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
                InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
            }
            and self.completed_at is None
        ):
            raise ValueError("Completed or failed investigations require completed_at.")

        if (
            self.started_at is not None
            and self.completed_at is not None
            and self.completed_at < self.started_at
        ):
            raise ValueError("Completion time cannot precede investigation start time.")

        return self

PATH - src\vigil\orchestration\service.py
CODE:
"""Service coordinating task execution and investigation state."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from vigil.orchestration.executor import (
    TaskExecutor,
    TaskResultContractError,
    UnknownTaskTypeError,
    WorkerExecutionError,
)
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import (
    InvestigationState,
    InvestigationStatus,
)
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import (
    ErrorDetail,
    TaskRequest,
    TaskResult,
    TaskStatus,
)
from vigil.orchestration.transitions import (
    transition_state,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TaskExecutionOutcome:
    """Outcome of executing one task within an investigation."""

    state: InvestigationState
    task_result: TaskResult


class OrchestrationServiceError(RuntimeError):
    """Base exception for orchestration service errors."""


class InvalidInvestigationStateError(OrchestrationServiceError):
    """Raised when the investigation cannot accept a new task."""


class DuplicateTaskError(OrchestrationServiceError):
    """Raised when a task ID already exists in the investigation state."""


class OrchestrationService:
    """Coordinate one synchronous task execution."""

    def __init__(self, executor: TaskExecutor) -> None:
        self._executor = executor

    def execute_task(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
        request: TaskRequest,
    ) -> TaskExecutionOutcome:
        """Execute one task and return its result and updated state."""
        if state.status not in {
            InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
            InvestigationStatus.RUNNING,
        }:
            raise InvalidInvestigationStateError(
                "Tasks can only run in CREATED or RUNNING investigations."
            )

        if request.task_id in state.task_ids:
            raise DuplicateTaskError(
                f"Task '{request.task_id}' is already recorded in this investigation."
            )

        started_at = datetime.now(UTC)

        if state.status == InvestigationStatus.CREATED:
            running_state = transition_state(
                state,
                InvestigationStatus.RUNNING,
            )
        else:
            running_state = state

        try:
            task_result = self._executor.execute(request)
        except (UnknownTaskTypeError, TaskResultContractError):
            # Configuration and contract violations must remain visible.
            raise
        except WorkerExecutionError:
            # Convert an explicitly reported worker failure into TaskResult.
            finished_at = datetime.now(UTC)
            task_result = TaskResult(
                task_id=request.task_id,
                status=TaskStatus.FAILED,
                output_refs=[],
                error=ErrorDetail(
                    code="WORKER_EXECUTION_ERROR",
                    message="The worker reported an execution failure.",
                    retryable=False,
                ),
                started_at=started_at,
                completed_at=finished_at,
            )

        finished_at = datetime.now(UTC)

        # Add the task ID to a new immutable state.
        state_after_execution = InvestigationState.model_validate(
            running_state.model_dump()
            | {
                "task_ids": [*running_state.task_ids, request.task_id],
                "updated_at": finished_at,
            }
        )

        if task_result.status == TaskStatus.FAILED:
            updated_state = transition_state(
                state_after_execution,
                InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
            )
        else:
            # A completed or partial task does not complete the investigation.
            updated_state = state_after_execution

        return TaskExecutionOutcome(
            state=updated_state,
            task_result=task_result,
        )

    def complete_investigation(
        self,
        state: InvestigationState,
    ) -> InvestigationState:
        """Mark a running investigation as completed."""
        if state.status != InvestigationStatus.RUNNING:
            raise InvalidInvestigationStateError(
                "Only a RUNNING investigation can be completed."
            )

        return transition_state(
            state,
            InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
        )

PATH - src\vigil\orchestration\task_types.py
CODE:
"""Shared task and model-output contracts for VIGIL."""

import math
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import Enum

from pydantic import Field, JsonValue, field_validator, model_validator

from vigil.domain.base import DomainModel
from vigil.domain.common import (
    ConfidenceScore,
    Identifier,
    ReferenceIds,
    Timestamp,
)


class TaskStatus(str, Enum):
    """Lifecycle status of an execution task."""

    PENDING = "pending"
    RUNNING = "running"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    PARTIAL = "partial"
    FAILED = "failed"


class ErrorDetail(DomainModel):
    """Structured information describing an execution error."""

    code: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Stable machine-readable error code.",
    )

    message: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Human-readable explanation of the error.",
    )

    retryable: bool = Field(
        description="Whether retrying the operation may succeed.",
    )

    retry_after_seconds: float | None = Field(
        default=None,
        ge=0,
        description="Optional minimum delay before retrying, in seconds.",
    )

    @field_validator("retry_after_seconds")
    @classmethod
    def validate_retry_delay(cls, value: float | None) -> float | None:
        """Reject non-finite retry delays."""
        if value is not None and not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ValueError("Retry delay must be finite.")
        return value

    @field_validator("code", "message")
    @classmethod
    def validate_nonblank_text(cls, value: str) -> str:
        """Reject blank error codes and messages."""
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("Error text must not be blank.")
        return value

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_retry_policy(self) -> "ErrorDetail":
        """Allow retry delays only for retryable errors."""
        if self.retry_after_seconds is not None and not self.retryable:
            raise ValueError("A retry delay requires a retryable error.")
        return self


class TaskRequest(DomainModel):
    """Defines a request for a tool or agent to perform work."""

    task_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for this task.",
    )

    task_type: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description=(
            "Operation to perform, such as frame_sampling or visual_analysis."
        ),
    )

    input_refs: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description=(
            "IDs of the media assets, artifacts or other inputs required for the task."
        ),
    )

    parameters: dict[str, JsonValue] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="JSON-compatible parameters controlling the operation.",
    )

    requested_by: Identifier | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional identifier of the agent or component requesting work.",
    )

    created_at: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware time when the task request was created.",
    )

    @field_validator("task_type")
    @classmethod
    def validate_task_type(cls, value: str) -> str:
        """Reject blank task types."""
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("Task type must not be blank.")
        return value


class TaskResult(DomainModel):
    """Records the execution outcome of a task."""

    task_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="ID of the task whose execution produced this result.",
    )

    status: TaskStatus = Field(
        description="Current execution outcome or lifecycle status.",
    )

    output_refs: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description=(
            "IDs of outputs produced by the task, such as observations or artifacts."
        ),
    )

    error: ErrorDetail | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Structured failure or partial-execution details, if any.",
    )

    started_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional timezone-aware time when execution began.",
    )

    completed_at: Timestamp | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description="Optional timezone-aware time when execution ended.",
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_result(self) -> "TaskResult":
        """Keep task status, errors and timestamps consistent."""
        if self.status == TaskStatus.FAILED and self.error is None:
            raise ValueError("A failed task must include error details.")

        if self.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED and self.error is not None:
            raise ValueError("A completed task must not include an error.")

        if (
            self.started_at is not None
            and self.completed_at is not None
            and self.completed_at < self.started_at
        ):
            raise ValueError("Completion time cannot precede start time.")

        return self


class ModelOutput(DomainModel):
    """Represents structured output from a model or analysis tool."""

    output_id: Identifier = Field(
        description="Stable unique identifier for this output.",
    )

    producer_name: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Name of the model or analysis tool producing the output.",
    )

    output_type: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description=(
            "Kind of output, such as frame_description, detection or transcription."
        ),
    )

    payload: dict[str, JsonValue] = Field(
        description="JSON-compatible structured output produced by the tool.",
    )

    confidence: ConfidenceScore | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=("Optional producer-reported confidence score between 0 and 1."),
    )

    artifact_ids: ReferenceIds = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="IDs of artifacts associated with the output.",
    )

    created_at: Timestamp = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(UTC),
        description="Timezone-aware time when the output was created.",
    )

PATH - src\vigil\orchestration\transitions.py
CODE:
"""Lifecycle transitions for investigation state."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime

from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import (
    InvestigationState,
    InvestigationStatus,
)


class InvalidStateTransitionError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an investigation transition is not allowed."""


_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: dict[InvestigationStatus, frozenset[InvestigationStatus]] = {
    InvestigationStatus.CREATED: frozenset(
        {
            InvestigationStatus.RUNNING,
            InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
        }
    ),
    InvestigationStatus.RUNNING: frozenset(
        {
            InvestigationStatus.PAUSED,
            InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
            InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
        }
    ),
    InvestigationStatus.PAUSED: frozenset(
        {
            InvestigationStatus.RUNNING,
            InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
        }
    ),
    InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED: frozenset(),
    InvestigationStatus.FAILED: frozenset(),
}


def transition_state(
    state: InvestigationState,
    new_status: InvestigationStatus,
) -> InvestigationState:
    """Return a new state with a validated lifecycle transition."""
    if new_status not in _ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[state.status]:
        raise InvalidStateTransitionError(
            f"Cannot transition investigation from "
            f"'{state.status.value}' to '{new_status.value}'."
        )

    now = datetime.now(UTC)

    updates: dict[str, object] = {
        "status": new_status,
        "updated_at": now,
    }

    if new_status == InvestigationStatus.RUNNING and state.started_at is None:
        updates["started_at"] = now

    if new_status in {
        InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
        InvestigationStatus.FAILED,
    }:
        updates["completed_at"] = now

    # Revalidate through Pydantic instead of using model_copy(update=...),
    # which does not validate updated values by default.
    return InvestigationState.model_validate(state.model_dump() | updates)

"""Forensic report export engine generating HTML and PDF deliverables with embedded incident clips."""

from __future__ import annotations

import html
import io
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse
from urllib.request import url2pathname

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.report import Report


def _uri_to_local_path(uri: str) -> Path | None:
    """Resolve file URI or local path string to existing Path."""
    if not uri:
        return None
    if uri.startswith("file://"):
        parsed = urlparse(uri)
        path = url2pathname(parsed.path)
        if path.startswith("\\") and len(path) > 3 and path[2] == ":":
            path = path[1:]
        p = Path(path)
        return p if p.is_file() else None
    p = Path(uri)
    return p if p.is_file() else None

def _format_timecode(seconds: float | None) -> str:
    """Format seconds into MM:SS.ss timecode."""
    if seconds is None:
        return "N/A"
    mins = int(seconds // 60)
    secs = seconds % 60
    return f"{mins:02d}:{secs:05.2f}"


def _format_interval(start: float | None, end: float | None) -> str:
    if start is None and end is None:
        return "Timeline: N/A"
    if start is not None and end is not None:
        return f"{_format_timecode(start)} &ndash; {_format_timecode(end)} ({start:.1f}s &ndash; {end:.1f}s)"
    if start is not None:
        return f"From {_format_timecode(start)} ({start:.1f}s)"
    return f"Until {_format_timecode(end)} ({end:.1f}s)"


def _clean_rationale(text: str) -> str:
    """Clean raw markdown tags, bold rating/rationale headers from rationale."""
    if not text:
        return ""
    import re
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\*\*Rating\s*:\s*[^\*]+\*\*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\*\*Rationale\s*:\s*\*\*", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^(Rating\s*:\s*[^\n]+\n?)", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^(Rationale\s*:\s*)", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^[\s\-*•]+", "", cleaned.strip())
    lines = [line.strip().lstrip("-*• ") for line in cleaned.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    return " ".join(lines) if lines else text.strip()



class ReportExporter:
    """Exports structured VIGIL forensic reports into interactive HTML and publication-ready PDF formats."""

    @staticmethod
    def export_html(
        investigation: Investigation,
        report: Report,
        events: list[Event],
        observations: list[Observation],
        assessments: list[EvidenceAssessment],
        coverages: list[CoverageRecord],
        artifacts: list[Artifact],
        audit_logs: list[AuditLog],
        media_assets: list[MediaAsset] | None = None,
        base_url: str = "",
    ) -> str:
        """Render complete forensic report as a standalone responsive HTML document with embedded incident clips."""
        media_assets = media_assets or []
        clip_artifacts = [
            a
            for a in artifacts
            if a.artifact_type in {"clip", "subclip", "video_clip"}
        ]

        # Pre-index clips by event or time overlap
        event_clips: dict[str, list[Artifact]] = {e.event_id: [] for e in events}
        unassigned_clips: list[Artifact] = []

        for clip in clip_artifacts:
            matched = False
            sr = clip.source_reference
            for evt in events:
                if (
                    sr.start_seconds is not None
                    and sr.end_seconds is not None
                    and evt.start_seconds is not None
                    and evt.end_seconds is not None
                    and not (
                        sr.end_seconds < evt.start_seconds
                        or sr.start_seconds > evt.end_seconds
                    )
                ):
                    event_clips[evt.event_id].append(clip)
                    matched = True
                    break
            if not matched:
                unassigned_clips.append(clip)

        events = sorted(events, key=lambda e: (e.start_seconds if e.start_seconds is not None else 0.0))
        confirmed_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.status == "confirmed")
        rejected_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.status == "rejected")
        proposed_count = sum(
            1 for e in events if e.status in {"proposed", "under_review"}
        )

        media_sha = (
            media_assets[0].sha256[:16] + "..."
            if (media_assets and media_assets[0].sha256)
            else "N/A"
        )
        media_name = (
            Path(media_assets[0].storage_uri).name
            if media_assets
            else "Primary Media"
        )

        # Build HTML content
        doc = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>VIGIL Forensic Report - {html.escape(report.title)}</title>
  <style>
    :root {{
      --bg: #0b0f19;
      --card-bg: #111827;
      --border: #1f2937;
      --text: #f3f4f6;
      --text-muted: #9ca3af;
      --accent: #2563eb;
      --accent-hover: #1d4ed8;
      --success: #10b981;
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --warning: #f59e0b;
      --info: #3b82f6;
    }}
    * {{ box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }}
    body {{
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
      background-color: var(--bg);
      color: var(--text);
      line-height: 1.6;
      padding: 2rem 1rem;
    }}
    .container {{
      max-width: 1100px;
      margin: 0 auto;
    }}
    header.report-header {{
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b, #0f172a);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 2rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
      position: relative;
    }}
    .badge-classified {{
      display: inline-block;
      padding: 0.25rem 0.75rem;
      border-radius: 9999px;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      letter-spacing: 0.05em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      background-color: rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.2);
      color: #60a5fa;
      border: 1px solid #3b82f6;
      margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    }}
    h1.report-title {{
      font-size: 2rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      color: #ffffff;
      margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    }}
    .header-grid {{
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr));
      gap: 1rem;
      margin-top: 1.5rem;
      padding-top: 1.5rem;
      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
      font-size: 0.875rem;
    }}
    .header-grid div strong {{ color: var(--text-muted); display: block; font-size: 0.75rem; text-transform: uppercase; }}

      white-space: pre-line;
      background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6);
      padding: 1.25rem;
      border-radius: 8px;
      border-left: 4px solid var(--accent);
    }}

    .event-card {{
      background-color: #0f172a;
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 1.25rem;
      margin-bottom: 1.25rem;
    }}
    .event-header {{
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 0.5rem;
    }}
    .event-title {{
      font-size: 1.1rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: #fff;
    }}
    .status-pill {{
      display: inline-block;
      padding: 0.25rem 0.6rem;
      border-radius: 9999px;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }}
    .status-confirmed {{ background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2); color: #34d399; border: 1px solid var(--success); }}
    .status-rejected {{ background-color: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2); color: #f87171; border: 1px solid var(--danger); }}
    .status-proposed {{ background-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2); color: #fbbf24; border: 1px solid var(--warning); }}
    .status-under_review {{ background-color: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.2); color: #60a5fa; border: 1px solid var(--info); }}

    .event-meta {{
      display: flex;
      gap: 1.5rem;
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      color: var(--text-muted);
      margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
    }}
    .event-desc {{
      color: #e2e8f0;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
      font-size: 0.95rem;
    }}
    .review-note-box {{
      background-color: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.7);
      border-left: 3px solid #38bdf8;
