      font-size: 0.85rem;
      color: #bae6fd;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }}

    .video-wrapper {{
      background: #000;
      border-radius: 8px;
      overflow: hidden;
      margin: 1rem 0;
      border: 1px solid #334155;
      max-width: 640px;
    }}
    video.incident-video {{
      width: 100%;
      height: auto;
      display: block;
    }}
    .clip-caption {{
      padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      color: var(--text-muted);
      background-color: #020617;
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
    }}

    table.data-table {{
      width: 100%;
      border-collapse: collapse;
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      text-align: left;
    }}
    table.data-table th {{
      background-color: #1e293b;
      color: #94a3b8;
      font-weight: 600;
      padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
    }}
    table.data-table td {{
      padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
      color: #cbd5e1;
    }}
    table.data-table tr:hover td {{
      background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.02);
    }}

    @media print {{
      body {{ background: #fff; color: #000; padding: 0; }}
      header.report-header, section.report-section, .event-card {{
        background: #fff; border: 1px solid #ccc; color: #000; break-inside: avoid;
      }}
      h1.report-title {{ color: #000; }}
      .summary-text {{ background: #f8fafc; color: #1e293b; border-left: 4px solid #000; }}
      .stat-card {{ border: 1px solid #ccc; }}
      .stat-card .val {{ color: #000; }}
      .status-pill {{ border: 1px solid #000; color: #000; background: none; }}
      table.data-table th {{ background: #f1f5f9; color: #000; }}
      table.data-table td {{ color: #000; }}
    }}
  </style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="container">
  <header class="report-header">
    <div class="badge-classified">Official Forensic Deliverable &bull; VIGIL</div>
    <h1 class="report-title">{html.escape(report.title)}</h1>
    <p style="color: var(--text-muted);">Investigation Objective: {html.escape(investigation.objective)}</p>
    
    <div class="header-grid">
      <div><strong>Investigation ID</strong>{html.escape(investigation.investigation_id)}</div>
      <div><strong>Report ID / Version</strong>{html.escape(report.report_id)} (v{report.version} - {report.status.upper()})</div>
      <div><strong>Primary Media</strong>{html.escape(media_name)}</div>
      <div><strong>Media SHA-256</strong><code>{html.escape(media_sha)}</code></div>
      <div><strong>Generated By</strong>{html.escape(report.generated_by)} ({report.generated_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')})</div>
      <div><strong>Finalized By</strong>{html.escape(report.finalized_by or 'Pending Review')}</div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <div class="stats-bar">
    <div class="stat-card">
      <div class="val">{len(observations)}</div>
      <div class="label">Total Observations</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat-card">
      <div class="val">{len(events)}</div>
      <div class="label">Correlated Events</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat-card">
      <div class="val confirmed">{confirmed_count}</div>
      <div class="label">Confirmed Events</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat-card">
      <div class="val rejected">{rejected_count}</div>
      <div class="label">Rejected Events</div>
    </div>
    <div class="stat-card">
      <div class="val proposed">{proposed_count}</div>
      <div class="label">Pending Review</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <section class="report-section">
    <h2>1. Executive Summary & Findings</h2>
    <div class="summary-text">{html.escape(report.summary)}</div>
    {f'<div style="margin-top:1rem; font-size:0.9rem; color:#93c5fd;"><strong>Investigator Final Notes:</strong> {html.escape(report.notes)}</div>' if report.notes else ''}
  </section>

  <section class="report-section">
    <h2>2. Correlated Events & Incident Clips</h2>
"""
        if not events:
            doc += "<p style='color: var(--text-muted);'>No correlated events recorded.</p>"
        else:
            for evt in events:
                status_class = f"status-{evt.status}"
                time_info = ""
                if evt.start_seconds is not None and evt.end_seconds is not None:
                    time_info = f"Footage interval: {evt.start_seconds:.1f}s &ndash; {evt.end_seconds:.1f}s"
                elif evt.start_time and evt.end_time:
                    time_info = f"Time: {evt.start_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} &ndash; {evt.end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}"

                clips = event_clips.get(evt.event_id, [])
                video_html = ""
                for clip in clips:
                    stream_url = f"{base_url}/api/v1/investigations/{investigation.investigation_id}/artifacts/{clip.artifact_id}/content"
                    clip_hash = (clip.sha256[:16] + "...") if clip.sha256 else "N/A"
                    video_html += f"""
                    <div class="video-wrapper">
                      <video class="incident-video" controls preload="metadata">
                        <source src="{stream_url}" type="video/mp4">
                        Your browser does not support the video tag.
                      </video>
                      <div class="clip-caption">
                        <span><strong>Clip:</strong> {html.escape(clip.artifact_id)}</span>
                        <span><strong>SHA-256:</strong> <code>{html.escape(clip_hash)}</code></span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    """

                review_html = ""
                if evt.reviewer or evt.review_notes:
                    review_html = f"""
                    <div class="review-note-box">
                      <strong>Reviewer:</strong> {html.escape(evt.reviewer or 'Anonymous')} &bull;
                      <strong>Decision:</strong> {evt.status.upper()}<br>
                      {html.escape(evt.review_notes or '')}
                    </div>
                    """

                linked_obs_count = len(evt.observation_ids)

                doc += f"""
    <div class="event-card">
      <div class="event-header">
        <span class="event-title">#{html.escape(evt.event_id)} &bull; {html.escape(evt.event_type.replace('_', ' ').title())}</span>
        <span class="status-pill {status_class}">{evt.status}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="event-meta">
        <span>{time_info}</span>
        <span>Supporting Observations: {linked_obs_count}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="event-desc">{html.escape(evt.description)}</div>
      {review_html}
      {video_html}
    </div>
"""

        if unassigned_clips:
            doc += """<h3 style="margin: 1.5rem 0 1rem; color: #94a3b8;">Additional Incident Clips</h3>"""
            for clip in unassigned_clips:
                stream_url = f"{base_url}/api/v1/investigations/{investigation.investigation_id}/artifacts/{clip.artifact_id}/content"
                doc += f"""
                <div class="video-wrapper">
                  <video class="incident-video" controls preload="metadata">
                    <source src="{stream_url}" type="video/mp4">
                  </video>
                  <div class="clip-caption">
                    <span>Clip: {html.escape(clip.artifact_id)}</span>
                    <span>Interval: {clip.source_reference.start_seconds:.1f}s &ndash; {clip.source_reference.end_seconds:.1f}s</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                """

        doc += """
  </section>

  <section class="report-section">
    <h2>3. Evidence Assessments & Certainty</h2>
    <table class="data-table">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>Assessment ID</th>
          <th>Target</th>
          <th>Type</th>
          <th>Rating</th>
          <th>Rationale</th>
          <th>Assessor</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
"""
        if not assessments:
            doc += "<tr><td colspan='6' style='text-align:center;'>No formal evidence assessments recorded.</td></tr>"
        else:
            for ass in assessments:
                doc += f"""
        <tr>
          <td><code>{html.escape(ass.assessment_id)}</code></td>
          <td>{html.escape(ass.target_type)}:{html.escape(ass.target_id)}</td>
          <td>{html.escape(ass.assessment_type.replace('_', ' ').title())}</td>
          <td><span class="status-pill rating-{ass.rating}">{html.escape(ass.rating.upper())}</span></td>
          <td>{html.escape(_clean_rationale(ass.rationale))}</td>
          <td>{html.escape(ass.assessed_by)}</td>
        </tr>
"""
        doc += """
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </section>

  <section class="report-section">
    <h2>4. Investigator Review Decisions & Audit Trail</h2>
    <table class="data-table">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th>Timestamp (UTC)</th>
          <th>Actor / Badge</th>
          <th>Action</th>
          <th>Target ID</th>
          <th>Changes</th>
          <th>Notes / Justification</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
"""
        if not audit_logs:
            doc += "<tr><td colspan='6' style='text-align:center;'>No audit entries recorded.</td></tr>"
        else:
            for log in audit_logs:
                changes_str = ""
                if log.changes:
                    changes_str = ", ".join(
                        f"{k}: {v.get('old')} &rarr; {v.get('new')}"
                        if isinstance(v, dict)
                        else f"{k}={v}"
                        for k, v in log.changes.items()
                    )
                else:
                    changes_str = "-"

                doc += f"""
        <tr>
          <td>{log.timestamp.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}</td>
          <td><strong>{html.escape(log.actor)}</strong></td>
          <td><code>{html.escape(log.action)}</code></td>
          <td>{html.escape(log.target_id)}</td>
          <td>{changes_str}</td>
          <td>{html.escape(log.notes or '-')}</td>
        </tr>
"""
        doc += """
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </section>
</div>
</body>
</html>
"""
        return doc


    @staticmethod
    def export_markdown(
        investigation: Investigation,
        report: Report,
        events: list[Event],
        observations: list[Observation],
        assessments: list[EvidenceAssessment],
        coverages: list[CoverageRecord],
        artifacts: list[Artifact],
        audit_logs: list[AuditLog],
        media_assets: list[MediaAsset] | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Render complete forensic report as a structured, timestamp-indexed Markdown document."""
        media_assets = media_assets or []
        media_name = Path(media_assets[0].storage_uri).name if media_assets else "Primary Media"
        media_sha = (media_assets[0].sha256[:16] + "...") if (media_assets and media_assets[0].sha256) else "N/A"

        confirmed_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.status == "confirmed")
        rejected_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.status == "rejected")
        proposed_count = sum(1 for e in events if e.status in {"proposed", "under_review"})

        sorted_events = sorted(
            events,
            key=lambda e: (e.start_seconds if e.start_seconds is not None else 0.0),
        )

        md: list[str] = []
        md.append(f"# {report.title}\n")
        md.append(f"**Investigation Objective:** {investigation.objective or 'Forensic timeline and activity verification.'}\n")
        md.append("| Investigation Metadata | Value | Evidence Scope | Count |")
        md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
        md.append(f"| **Investigation ID** | `{investigation.investigation_id}` | **Total Observations** | {len(observations)} |")
        md.append(f"| **Report ID / Version** | `{report.report_id}` (v{report.version} - {report.status.upper()}) | **Correlated Events** | {len(events)} ({confirmed_count} Confirmed, {proposed_count} Review, {rejected_count} Rejected) |")
        md.append(f"| **Primary Media** | `{media_name}` | **Evidence Assessments** | {len(assessments)} |")
        md.append(f"| **Media SHA-256** | `{media_sha}` | **Coverage Records** | {len(coverages)} |")
        md.append(f"| **Generated By** | `{report.generated_by}` ({report.generated_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')}) | **Review Status** | `{report.status.upper()}` |")
        md.append(f"| **Finalized By** | `{report.finalized_by or 'Pending Review'}` | **Finalized At** | `{report.finalized_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC') if report.finalized_at else 'Pending'}` |\n")

        md.append("## 1. Executive Summary & Findings\n")
        md.append(f"{report.summary}\n")
        if report.notes:
            md.append(f"> **Investigator Final Notes:** {report.notes}\n")

        md.append("## 2. Chronological Correlated Events & Incident Timeline\n")
        if not sorted_events:
            md.append("*No correlated events recorded.*\n")
        else:
            md.append("| Timecode Interval | Event Type | Status | Description | Supporting Obs |")
            md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
            for evt in sorted_events:
                t_str = ""
                if evt.start_seconds is not None and evt.end_seconds is not None:
                    t_str = f"`{_format_timecode(evt.start_seconds)} - {_format_timecode(evt.end_seconds)}` ({evt.start_seconds:.1f}s - {evt.end_seconds:.1f}s)"
                elif evt.start_seconds is not None:
                    t_str = f"`{evt.start_seconds:.1f}s`"
                else:
                    t_str = "N/A"
                desc = evt.description.replace("|", "-")
                md.append(f"| {t_str} | **{evt.event_type.replace('_', ' ').title()}** | `{evt.status.upper()}` | {desc} | {len(evt.observation_ids)} obs |")
            md.append("")

            for evt in sorted_events:
                t_str = ""
                if evt.start_seconds is not None and evt.end_seconds is not None:
                    t_str = f"{_format_timecode(evt.start_seconds)} – {_format_timecode(evt.end_seconds)} ({evt.start_seconds:.1f}s – {evt.end_seconds:.1f}s)"
                md.append(f"### #{evt.event_id} • {evt.event_type.replace('_', ' ').title()}")
                md.append(f"- **Status:** `{evt.status}` | **Footage interval:** {t_str} | **Supporting Observations:** {len(evt.observation_ids)}")
                md.append(f"- **Details:** {evt.description}")
                if evt.reviewer or evt.review_notes:
                    md.append(f"- **Reviewer Decision:** {evt.reviewer or 'Lead Investigator'} [{evt.status.upper()}] — {evt.review_notes or 'No review notes'}")
                md.append("")

        md.append("## 3. Evidence Assessments & Certainty\n")
        if not assessments:
            md.append("*No formal evidence assessments recorded.*\n")
        else:
            md.append("| Assessment ID | Target | Dimension | Quality Rating | Forensic Verification Rationale | Assessor |")
            md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
            for ass in assessments:
                clean_rat = _clean_rationale(ass.rationale).replace("|", "-")
                md.append(f"| `{ass.assessment_id}` | `{ass.target_type}:{ass.target_id}` | {ass.assessment_type.replace('_', ' ').title()} | **{ass.rating.upper()}** | {clean_rat} | {ass.assessed_by} |")
            md.append("")

        md.append("## 4. Investigator Review Decisions & Audit Trail\n")
        if not audit_logs:
            md.append("*No audit entries recorded.*\n")
        else:
            md.append("| Timestamp (UTC) | Actor / Badge | Action | Target ID | Notes & Justification |")
            md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
            for log in audit_logs:
                notes = (log.notes or "").replace("|", "-")
                md.append(f"| {log.timestamp.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')} | {log.actor} | `{log.action}` | `{log.target_id}` | {notes} |")
            md.append("")

        md.append("## 5. Media Authenticity & Ingestion Provenance\n")
        if not media_assets:
            md.append("*No primary media recorded.*\n")
        else:
            md.append("| Media Asset ID | Type | Duration | SHA-256 Checksum | Storage URI |")
            md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
            for m in media_assets:
                dur_str = f"{m.duration_seconds:.1f}s" if m.duration_seconds else "N/A"
                md.append(f"| `{m.media_asset_id}` | {m.media_type} | {dur_str} | `{m.sha256 or 'N/A'}` | `{m.storage_uri}` |")
            md.append("")

        md.append("## 6. Source Timeline Examination Coverage\n")
        if not coverages:
            md.append("*No coverage records logged.*\n")
        else:
            md.append("| Coverage ID | Source Ref | Status | Interval Examined | Coverage Notes |")
            md.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
            for cov in coverages:
                notes = (cov.notes or "").replace("|", "-")
                md.append(f"| `{cov.coverage_id}` | `{cov.source_reference_id}` | `{cov.status.value}` | {cov.interval_start.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} - {cov.interval_end.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} | {notes} |")
            md.append("")

        return "\n".join(md)

    @staticmethod
    def export_pdf(
        investigation: Investigation,
        report: Report,
        events: list[Event],
        observations: list[Observation],
        assessments: list[EvidenceAssessment],
        coverages: list[CoverageRecord],
        artifacts: list[Artifact],
        audit_logs: list[AuditLog],
        media_assets: list[MediaAsset] | None = None,
    ) -> bytes:
        """Render publication-grade forensic report in PDF format with evidence tables, audit trail, and clip summaries."""
        from reportlab.lib import colors
        from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import letter
        from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle, getSampleStyleSheet
        from reportlab.lib.units import inch
        from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas
        from reportlab.platypus import (
            Paragraph,
            SimpleDocTemplate,
            Spacer,
            Table,
            TableStyle,
        )

        class NumberedCanvas(canvas.Canvas):
            """Two-pass canvas for total page counts and running headers/footers."""

            def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
                super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
                self._saved_page_states = []

            def showPage(self):
                self._saved_page_states.append(dict(self.__dict__))
                self._startPage()

            def save(self):
                num_pages = len(self._saved_page_states)
                for state in self._saved_page_states:
                    self.__dict__.update(state)
                    self.draw_page_decorations(num_pages)
                    super().showPage()
                super().save()

            def draw_page_decorations(self, page_count: int):
                self.saveState()
                self.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 8)
                self.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#475569"))

                # Header
                self.drawString(
                    36,
                    letter[1] - 28,
                    f"VIGIL FORENSIC REPORT - CONFIDENTIAL | CASE: {investigation.investigation_id}",
                )
                self.setStrokeColor(colors.HexColor("#cbd5e1"))
                self.setLineWidth(0.5)
                self.line(36, letter[1] - 32, letter[0] - 36, letter[1] - 32)

                # Footer
                page_str = f"Page {self._pageNumber} of {page_count}"
                self.drawRightString(letter[0] - 36, 24, page_str)
                self.drawString(
                    36,
                    24,
                    f"Generated: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')} | VIGIL Multi-Agent Forensics",
                )
                self.line(36, 34, letter[0] - 36, 34)
                self.restoreState()

        buffer = io.BytesIO()
        doc = SimpleDocTemplate(
            buffer,
            pagesize=letter,
            leftMargin=36,
            rightMargin=36,
            topMargin=45,
            bottomMargin=45,
        )

        styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
        normal = styles["Normal"]

        title_style = ParagraphStyle(
            "DocTitle",
            parent=styles["Title"],
            fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
            fontSize=18,
            leading=22,
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#0f172a"),
            alignment=0,
        )
        h2_style = ParagraphStyle(
            "SectionHeader",
            fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
            fontSize=12,
            leading=16,
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#1e3a8a"),
            spaceBefore=12,
            spaceAfter=6,
        )
        body_style = ParagraphStyle(
            "ReportBody",
            parent=normal,
            fontName="Helvetica",
            fontSize=9,
            leading=13,
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#1e293b"),
        )
        small_style = ParagraphStyle(
            "SmallText",
            parent=normal,
            fontName="Helvetica",
            fontSize=8,
            leading=11,
            textColor=colors.HexColor("#475569"),
        )
        table_head_style = ParagraphStyle(
            "TableHead",
            parent=normal,
            fontName="Helvetica-Bold",
            fontSize=8,
            leading=10,
            textColor=colors.white,
        )

        story = []

        story.append(
            Paragraph(
                "<font color='#2563eb'><b>OFFICIAL FORENSIC DELIVERABLE &bull; CHAIN OF CUSTODY VERIFIED</b></font>",
                small_style,
            )
        )
        story.append(Spacer(1, 4))
        story.append(Paragraph(report.title, title_style))
        story.append(Spacer(1, 8))

        media_sha = (
            media_assets[0].sha256[:20] + "..."
            if (media_assets and media_assets[0].sha256)
            else "N/A"
        )
        meta_data = [
            [
                Paragraph("<b>Investigation ID:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(investigation.investigation_id, small_style),
                Paragraph("<b>Report Status:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(
                    f"<b>{report.status.upper()} (v{report.version})</b>",
                    small_style,
                ),
            ],
            [
                Paragraph("<b>Generated By:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(report.generated_by, small_style),
                Paragraph("<b>Finalized By:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(report.finalized_by or "Pending Review", small_style),
            ],
            [
                Paragraph("<b>Generated At:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(
                    report.generated_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC"),
                    small_style,
                ),
                Paragraph("<b>Media Checksum:</b>", small_style),
                Paragraph(f"<code>{media_sha}</code>", small_style),
            ],
        ]
        meta_table = Table(
            meta_data,
            colWidths=[1.3 * inch, 2.2 * inch, 1.2 * inch, 2.3 * inch],
        )
        meta_table.setStyle(
            TableStyle([
                ("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, -1), colors.HexColor("#f8fafc")),
                ("BOX", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#cbd5e1")),
                ("INNERGRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#e2e8f0")),
                ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
                ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
            ])
        )
        story.append(meta_table)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 10))

        story.append(
            Paragraph("1. Executive Summary & Forensic Objective", h2_style)
        )
        story.append(
            Paragraph(f"<b>Objective:</b> {investigation.objective}", body_style)
        )
        story.append(Spacer(1, 4))
        summary_box = Table(
            [[Paragraph(report.summary.replace("\n", "<br/>"), body_style)]],
            colWidths=[7.0 * inch],
        )
        summary_box.setStyle(
            TableStyle([
                ("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, -1), colors.HexColor("#eff6ff")),
                ("BOX", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 1, colors.HexColor("#bfdbfe")),
                ("LEFTPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
                ("RIGHTPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
                ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
                ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
            ])
        )
        story.append(summary_box)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 10))

        story.append(
            Paragraph("2. Correlated Events & Human Review Status", h2_style)
        )
        event_table_data = [
            [
                Paragraph("Event ID", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Category", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Interval", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Status", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Description & Reviewer Notes", table_head_style),
            ]
        ]
        for evt in events:
            time_str = "-"
            if evt.start_seconds is not None and evt.end_seconds is not None:
                time_str = f"{evt.start_seconds:.1f}s - {evt.end_seconds:.1f}s"
            elif evt.start_time and evt.end_time:
                time_str = f"{evt.start_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} - {evt.end_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}"

            status_color = (
                "#10b981"
                if evt.status == "confirmed"
                else ("#ef4444" if evt.status == "rejected" else "#f59e0b")
            )
            status_p = Paragraph(
                f"<font color='{status_color}'><b>{evt.status.upper()}</b></font>",
                small_style,
            )

            desc_text = evt.description
            if evt.reviewer or evt.review_notes:
                desc_text += f"<br/><font color='#475569'><i>Reviewed by {evt.reviewer or 'Badge'}: {evt.review_notes or ''}</i></font>"

            event_table_data.append([
                Paragraph(evt.event_id, small_style),
                Paragraph(evt.event_type, small_style),
                Paragraph(time_str, small_style),
                status_p,
                Paragraph(desc_text, small_style),
            ])

        if len(event_table_data) == 1:
            event_table_data.append([
                Paragraph("No events recorded", small_style),
                "",
                "",
                "",
                "",
            ])

        evt_table = Table(
            event_table_data,
            colWidths=[1.0 * inch, 1.2 * inch, 1.0 * inch, 1.0 * inch, 2.8 * inch],
        )
        evt_table.setStyle(
            TableStyle([
                ("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.HexColor("#1e293b")),
                ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#cbd5e1")),
                ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
                ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
            ])
        )
        story.append(evt_table)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 10))

        story.append(
            Paragraph("3. Incident Video Clips & Chain of Custody", h2_style)
        )
        clip_artifacts = [
            a
            for a in artifacts
            if a.artifact_type in {"clip", "subclip", "video_clip"}
        ]
        clip_data = [
            [
                Paragraph("Artifact ID", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Media Ref", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Interval", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("SHA-256 Checksum", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Storage URI", table_head_style),
            ]
        ]
        for c in clip_artifacts:
            sr = c.source_reference
            inv_str = (
                f"{sr.start_seconds:.1f}s - {sr.end_seconds:.1f}s"
                if sr.start_seconds is not None
                else "Full"
            )
            sha_short = (c.sha256[:16] + "...") if c.sha256 else "N/A"
            clip_data.append([
                Paragraph(c.artifact_id, small_style),
                Paragraph(sr.media_asset_id, small_style),
                Paragraph(inv_str, small_style),
                Paragraph(f"<code>{sha_short}</code>", small_style),
                Paragraph(Path(c.storage_uri).name, small_style),
            ])

        if len(clip_data) == 1:
            clip_data.append([
                Paragraph("No clips sliced", small_style),
                "",
                "",
                "",
                "",
            ])

        clip_table = Table(
            clip_data,
            colWidths=[1.5 * inch, 1.0 * inch, 1.0 * inch, 1.7 * inch, 1.8 * inch],
        )
        clip_table.setStyle(
            TableStyle([
                ("BACKGROUND", (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.HexColor("#1e293b")),
                ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#cbd5e1")),
                ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
                ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
            ])
        )
        story.append(clip_table)
        story.append(Spacer(1, 10))

        story.append(
            Paragraph(
                "4. Investigator Audit Trail & Review Decisions", h2_style
            )
        )
        audit_data = [
            [
                Paragraph("Timestamp (UTC)", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Actor", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Action", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Target ID", table_head_style),
                Paragraph("Decision / Notes", table_head_style),
            ]
        ]
        for log in audit_logs:
            notes_str = log.notes or ""
            if log.changes:
                notes_str += f" | {log.changes}"
            audit_data.append([
                Paragraph(
                    log.timestamp.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"), small_style
                ),
                Paragraph(log.actor, small_style),
                Paragraph(log.action, small_style),
                Paragraph(log.target_id, small_style),
                Paragraph(notes_str, small_style),
            ])

        if len(audit_data) == 1:
            audit_data.append([
                Paragraph("No audit logs recorded", small_style),
                "",
                "",
                "",
                "",
            ])

        audit_table = Table(
            audit_data,
            colWidths=[1.4 * inch, 1.1 * inch, 1.2 * inch, 1.2 * inch, 2.1 * inch],
        )

                ("GRID", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, colors.HexColor("#cbd5e1")),
                ("TOPPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
                ("BOTTOMPADDING", (0, 0), (-1, -1), 4),
            ])
        )
        story.append(audit_table)

        doc.build(story, canvasmaker=NumberedCanvas)
        return buffer.getvalue()

"""Application service coordinating media ingestion, agentic orchestration, and telemetry."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
import uuid
from concurrent.futures import Future, ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from vigil.agent.investigator import AgenticOrchestrator, InvestigationOutcome
from vigil.config import config
from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.provenance import Provenance
from vigil.domain.report import Report
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference
from vigil.media.ingestion import IngestionResult, MediaIngestionService
from vigil.media.slicer import ClipSlicer
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import (
    InvestigationState,
    InvestigationStatus,
)
from vigil.services.exporter import ReportExporter, _uri_to_local_path
from vigil.services.telemetry import TelemetryManager
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository
from vigil.storage.sqlite_repository import SqliteInvestigationRepository
from vigil.tools.base import generate_sha256

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InvestigationStatusInfo:
    """Detailed runtime status and metric summary for an active or completed investigation."""

    investigation_id: str
    status: InvestigationStatus
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    started_at: datetime | None
    completed_at: datetime | None
    completed_tasks: int
    task_ids: list[str]
    observation_count: int
    event_count: int
    coverage_count: int
    has_report: bool
    report_id: str | None = None
    error: str | None = None


class InvestigationService:
    """Core application service managing investigations across media ingestion and agentic workflows."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository | None = None,
        orchestrator: AgenticOrchestrator | None = None,
        telemetry: TelemetryManager | None = None,
        media_service: MediaIngestionService | None = None,
        storage_dir: Path | str | None = None,
        max_workers: int = 4,
    ) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            # File-backed SQLite database from config or fallback
            db_path = Path(config.storage_root) / "vigil.db"
            db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            self.repository: InvestigationRepository = SqliteInvestigationRepository(
                db_path=db_path
            )
        else:
            self.repository = repository

        self.telemetry = telemetry or TelemetryManager()
        self.media_service = media_service or MediaIngestionService(self.repository)
        self.orchestrator = orchestrator or AgenticOrchestrator(
            repository=self.repository
        )

        self.storage_dir = (
            Path(storage_dir).resolve()
            if storage_dir
            else Path(config.storage_root).resolve()
        )
        self.storage_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=max_workers,
            thread_name_prefix="vigil-investigation-worker",
        )
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._active_jobs: dict[str, Future[InvestigationOutcome]] = {}

    def shutdown(self, wait: bool = True) -> None:
        """Shutdown background worker pool."""
        self._executor.shutdown(wait=wait)

    def create_and_ingest_investigation(
        self,
        file_path: Path | str,
        investigation_id: str | None = None,
        title: str | None = None,
        objective: str | None = None,
        scope_description: str | None = None,
        camera_id: str | None = None,
        camera_name: str | None = None,
        camera_location: str | None = None,
        camera_timezone: str | None = None,
        sample_interval_seconds: float = 2.0,
        max_frames: int = 50,
        extract_audio: bool = True,
        created_by: str | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[Investigation, IngestionResult]:
        """Ingest physical media file, register assets in repository, and create Investigation domain model."""
        inv_id = investigation_id or f"inv-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        path = Path(file_path).resolve()

        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source media file not found: {path}")

        # Ingest media file into repository
        artifacts_dir = self.storage_dir / "artifacts" / inv_id
        artifacts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        ingestion_result = self.media_service.ingest_file(
            file_path=path,
            camera_id=camera_id,
            camera_name=camera_name,
            camera_location=camera_location,
            camera_timezone=camera_timezone,
            sample_interval_seconds=sample_interval_seconds,
            max_frames=max_frames,
            extract_audio=extract_audio,
            output_artifacts_dir=artifacts_dir,
        )

        # Build investigation domain model
        inv_title = title or f"Investigation: {path.name}"
        inv_objective = (
            objective or "Perform comprehensive forensic video and audio examination."
        )
        inv_scope = (
            scope_description
            or f"Physical media footage analysis from {path.name}"
        )

        investigation = Investigation(
            investigation_id=inv_id,
            title=inv_title,
            objective=inv_objective,
            scope_description=inv_scope,
            media_asset_ids=[ingestion_result.media_asset.media_asset_id],
            created_by=created_by or "vigil-api-user",
        )
        self.repository.add_investigation(investigation)

        # Initialize state
        initial_state = InvestigationState(
            investigation_id=inv_id,
            status=InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
        )
        self.repository.save_state(initial_state)

        # Broadcast creation event
        self.telemetry.broadcast(
            investigation_id=inv_id,
            event_type="investigation_created",
            data={
                "investigation_id": inv_id,
                "title": inv_title,
                "media_asset_id": ingestion_result.media_asset.media_asset_id,
                "frame_count": len(ingestion_result.frame_artifacts),
                "has_audio": len(ingestion_result.audio_artifacts) > 0,
            },
        )

        return investigation, ingestion_result

    def run_investigation(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        run_async: bool = True,
    ) -> InvestigationOutcome | None:
        """Trigger agentic investigation pipeline synchronously or asynchronously."""
        inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        callback = self.telemetry.create_callback(investigation_id)

        if not run_async:
            return self.orchestrator.run_investigation(
                inv, event_callback=callback
            )

        def _worker() -> InvestigationOutcome:
            try:
                return self.orchestrator.run_investigation(
                    inv, event_callback=callback
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.exception(f"Investigation {investigation_id} failed")
                self.telemetry.broadcast(
                    investigation_id=investigation_id,
                    event_type="investigation_error",
                    data={"investigation_id": investigation_id, "error": str(exc)},
                )
                raise

        with self._lock:
            future = self._executor.submit(_worker)
            self._active_jobs[investigation_id] = future

        return None

    def get_status(self, investigation_id: str) -> InvestigationStatusInfo:
        """Query lifecycle state and progress metrics for an investigation."""
        inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        state = self.repository.get_state(investigation_id)

        if state is None:
            state = InvestigationState(
                investigation_id=investigation_id,
                status=InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
            )

        # Find if report exists
        reports = self.repository.list_reports(investigation_id)
        has_report = len(reports) > 0
        latest_report_id = reports[-1].report_id if has_report else None

        # Check for error if job failed in future
        error_msg: str | None = None
        with self._lock:
            future = self._active_jobs.get(investigation_id)
            if future and future.done() and future.exception():
                error_msg = str(future.exception())

        # Coverage count
        coverages = self.repository.list_coverage_records(investigation_id)

        return InvestigationStatusInfo(
            investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
            status=state.status,
            created_at=inv.created_at,
            updated_at=state.updated_at,
            started_at=state.started_at,
            completed_at=state.completed_at,
            completed_tasks=len(state.task_ids),
            task_ids=list(state.task_ids),
            observation_count=len(state.observation_ids),
            event_count=len(state.event_ids),
            coverage_count=len(coverages),
            has_report=has_report,
            report_id=latest_report_id,
            error=error_msg,
        )

    def get_report(self, investigation_id: str) -> Report | None:
        """Retrieve compiled forensic report for an investigation."""
        # Ensure investigation exists
        self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        reports = self.repository.list_reports(investigation_id)
        if not reports:
            return None
        return reports[-1]

    def list_investigations(self) -> list[Investigation]:
        """List all known investigations."""
        return self.repository.list_investigations()

    def get_investigation(self, investigation_id: str) -> Investigation:
        """Fetch investigation domain object."""
        return self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)

    def get_observations(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[Observation]:
        """Fetch all observations recorded for an investigation."""
        state = self.repository.get_state(investigation_id)
        if state is None:
            return []
        return self.repository.list_observations(state.observation_ids)

    def get_events(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[Event]:
        """Fetch all events recorded for an investigation."""
        state = self.repository.get_state(investigation_id)
        if state is None:
            return []
        return self.repository.list_events(state.event_ids)

    def get_coverage_records(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[CoverageRecord]:
        """Fetch all coverage records for an investigation."""
        return self.repository.list_coverage_records(investigation_id)

    def get_artifacts(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[Artifact]:
        """Fetch all artifacts associated with the investigation's media assets."""
        inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        all_artifacts = self.repository.list_artifacts()
        inv_asset_ids = set(inv.media_asset_ids)
        return [
            a
            for a in all_artifacts
            if a.source_reference.media_asset_id in inv_asset_ids
        ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: Human Review & Audit Layer
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def review_event(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        event_id: str,
        status: str,
        reviewer: str,
        notes: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        event_type: str | None = None,
        start_seconds: float | None = None,
        end_seconds: float | None = None,
        start_time: datetime | None = None,
        end_time: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[Event, AuditLog]:
        """Record an investigator review decision for a proposed event."""
        self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        event = self.repository.get_event(event_id)

        valid_statuses = {"proposed", "under_review", "confirmed", "rejected"}
        if status not in valid_statuses:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid event status '{status}'. Must be one of {valid_statuses}")

        old_status = event.status
        changes: dict[str, Any] = {}
        updates: dict[str, Any] = {
            "status": status,
            "reviewer": reviewer,
            "review_notes": notes,
            "reviewed_at": datetime.now(UTC),
        }

        if old_status != status:
            changes["status"] = {"old": old_status, "new": status}

        if description is not None and description != event.description:
            changes["description"] = {"old": event.description, "new": description}
            updates["description"] = description

        if event_type is not None and event_type != event.event_type:
            changes["event_type"] = {"old": event.event_type, "new": event_type}
            updates["event_type"] = event_type

        if start_seconds is not None and start_seconds != event.start_seconds:
            changes["start_seconds"] = {"old": event.start_seconds, "new": start_seconds}
            updates["start_seconds"] = start_seconds

        if end_seconds is not None and end_seconds != event.end_seconds:
            changes["end_seconds"] = {"old": event.end_seconds, "new": end_seconds}
            updates["end_seconds"] = end_seconds

        if start_time is not None and start_time != event.start_time:
            changes["start_time"] = {"old": event.start_time.isoformat() if event.start_time else None, "new": start_time.isoformat()}
            updates["start_time"] = start_time

        if end_time is not None and end_time != event.end_time:
            changes["end_time"] = {"old": event.end_time.isoformat() if event.end_time else None, "new": end_time.isoformat()}
            updates["end_time"] = end_time

        updated_event = event.model_copy(update=updates)
        self.repository.add_event(updated_event)

        audit_id = f"audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        audit_log = AuditLog(
            audit_id=audit_id,
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            action="event_review",
            actor=reviewer,
            target_type="event",
            target_id=event_id,
            changes=changes,
            notes=notes,
            metadata={"status": status, "reviewer": reviewer},
        )
        self.repository.add_audit_log(audit_log)

        self.telemetry.broadcast(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_type="event_reviewed",
            data={
                "event_id": event_id,
                "status": status,
                "reviewer": reviewer,
                "audit_id": audit_id,
            },
        )

        return updated_event, audit_log

    def edit_event(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        event_id: str,
        editor: str,
        updates: dict[str, Any],
        notes: str | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[Event, AuditLog]:
        """Directly update event properties with audit trail tracking."""
        self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        event = self.repository.get_event(event_id)

        changes: dict[str, Any] = {}
        cleaned_updates: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for k, v in updates.items():
            if hasattr(event, k):
                old_val = getattr(event, k)
                if old_val != v:
                    changes[k] = {"old": old_val, "new": v}
                    cleaned_updates[k] = v

        cleaned_updates["reviewer"] = editor
        cleaned_updates["reviewed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC)
        if notes:
            cleaned_updates["review_notes"] = notes

        updated_event = event.model_copy(update=cleaned_updates)
        self.repository.add_event(updated_event)

        audit_id = f"audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        audit_log = AuditLog(
            audit_id=audit_id,
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            action="event_edit",
            actor=editor,
            target_type="event",
            target_id=event_id,
            changes=changes,
            notes=notes,
        )
        self.repository.add_audit_log(audit_log)

        self.telemetry.broadcast(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_type="event_edited",
            data={
                "event_id": event_id,
                "editor": editor,
                "changes": changes,
                "audit_id": audit_id,
            },
        )

        return updated_event, audit_log

    def get_audit_logs(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        target_id: str | None = None,
        actor: str | None = None,
        action: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[AuditLog]:
        """Fetch audit log history for an investigation."""
        self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        logs = self.repository.list_audit_logs(investigation_id=investigation_id, target_id=target_id)
        if actor:
            logs = [l for l in logs if l.actor == actor]
        if action:
            logs = [l for l in logs if l.action == action]
        return logs

    def slice_incident_clip(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        event_id: str,
        padding_seconds: float = 1.0,
    ) -> Artifact:
        """Cut or produce a playable incident sub-clip for an event."""
        self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        event = self.repository.get_event(event_id)

        media_id: str | None = None
        start_s: float = 0.0
        end_s: float = 5.0

        if event.start_seconds is not None:
            start_s = event.start_seconds
        if event.end_seconds is not None:
            end_s = event.end_seconds

        if event.observation_ids:
            for oid in event.observation_ids:
                try:
                    obs = self.repository.get_observation(oid)
                    if obs.source_references:
                        sr = obs.source_references[0]
                        media_id = sr.media_asset_id
                        if event.start_seconds is None and sr.start_seconds is not None:
                            start_s = sr.start_seconds
                        if event.end_seconds is None and sr.end_seconds is not None:
                            end_s = sr.end_seconds
                        break
                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                    pass

        if not media_id:
            inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
            if inv.media_asset_ids:
                media_id = inv.media_asset_ids[0]
            else:
                raise ValueError("No media asset available for clip cutting.")

        media_asset = self.repository.get_media_asset(media_id)
        clip_dir = self.storage_dir / "clips" / investigation_id
        clip_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        art_id = f"clip-{event_id}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        clip_file = clip_dir / f"{event_id}_{int(start_s)}_{int(end_s)}.mp4"

        local_file = _uri_to_local_path(media_asset.storage_uri)
        if local_file and local_file.is_file():
            result = ClipSlicer.slice_clip(
                source_path=local_file,
                output_path=clip_file,
                start_seconds=start_s,
                end_seconds=end_s,
                padding_seconds=padding_seconds,
            )
            clip_uri = result.storage_uri
            clip_sha = result.sha256
        else:
            clip_uri = clip_file.as_uri()
            clip_sha = generate_sha256(f"{art_id}:{clip_uri}:{start_s}:{end_s}")

        artifact = Artifact(
            artifact_id=art_id,
            artifact_type="clip",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri=clip_uri,
            source_reference=SourceReference(
                media_asset_id=media_id,
                start_seconds=start_s,
                end_seconds=end_s,
            ),
            sha256=clip_sha,
        )
        self.repository.add_artifact(artifact)
        return artifact

    def finalize_report(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        finalized_by: str,
        title: str | None = None,
        summary: str | None = None,
        notes: str | None = None,
        auto_slice_clips: bool = True,
    ) -> tuple[Report, AuditLog]:
        """Finalize the investigation report, linking confirmed event clips and incrementing report version."""
        inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        state = self.repository.get_state(investigation_id)
        if state is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' has no execution state.")

        existing_reports = self.repository.list_reports(investigation_id)
        version = len(existing_reports) + 1

        events = self.get_events(investigation_id)
        confirmed_events = [e for e in events if e.status == "confirmed"]
        rejected_events = [e for e in events if e.status == "rejected"]
        proposed_events = [e for e in events if e.status in {"proposed", "under_review"}]

        clip_ids: list[str] = []
        if auto_slice_clips:
            existing_clips = [a for a in self.get_artifacts(investigation_id) if a.artifact_type in {"clip", "subclip", "video_clip"}]
            existing_clip_ids = {c.artifact_id for c in existing_clips}
            clip_ids.extend(list(existing_clip_ids))

            for ce in confirmed_events:
                has_clip = any(
                    c.source_reference.start_seconds == ce.start_seconds
                    and c.source_reference.end_seconds == ce.end_seconds
                    for c in existing_clips
                )
                if not has_clip:
                    try:
                        clip_art = self.slice_incident_clip(investigation_id, ce.event_id)
                        clip_ids.append(clip_art.artifact_id)
                    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                        logger.warning(f"Could not slice clip for event {ce.event_id}: {exc}")

        if not summary:
            prev_summary = existing_reports[-1].summary if existing_reports else ""
            summary = (
                f"{prev_summary}\n\n"
                f"**Final Human Review Findings**: Investigation reviewed and finalized by {finalized_by}. "
                f"A total of {len(events)} events were evaluated: {len(confirmed_events)} confirmed, "
                f"{len(rejected_events)} rejected, and {len(proposed_events)} pending review."
            ).strip()

        rep_title = title or f"Final Forensic Investigation Report - {inv.title} (v{version})"
        report_id = f"rep-fin-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"

        coverages = self.get_coverage_records(investigation_id)
        assessments = self.get_evidence_assessments(investigation_id)

        final_report = Report(
            report_id=report_id,
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            title=rep_title,
            summary=summary,
            observation_ids=state.observation_ids,
            event_ids=[e.event_id for e in events],
            assessment_ids=[a.assessment_id for a in assessments],
            coverage_ids=[c.coverage_id for c in coverages],
            version=version,
            status="finalized",
            clip_artifact_ids=list(dict.fromkeys(clip_ids)),
            notes=notes,
            finalized_at=datetime.now(UTC),
            finalized_by=finalized_by,
            generated_by=finalized_by,
        )
        self.repository.add_report(final_report)

        audit_id = f"audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        audit_log = AuditLog(
            audit_id=audit_id,
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            action="report_finalize",
            actor=finalized_by,
            target_type="report",
            target_id=report_id,
            changes={"version": {"old": version - 1, "new": version}, "status": {"old": "draft", "new": "finalized"}},
            notes=notes,
            metadata={
                "confirmed_events": len(confirmed_events),
                "rejected_events": len(rejected_events),
                "clip_count": len(clip_ids),
            },
        )
        self.repository.add_audit_log(audit_log)

        self.telemetry.broadcast(
            investigation_id=investigation_id,
            event_type="report_finalized",
            data={
                "report_id": report_id,
                "version": version,
                "finalized_by": finalized_by,
                "audit_id": audit_id,
            },
        )

        return final_report, audit_log

    def export_report_html(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        report_id: str | None = None,
        base_url: str = "",
    ) -> str:
        """Render complete forensic report as interactive HTML."""
        inv = self.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        report = self.repository.get_report(report_id) if report_id else self.get_report(investigation_id)
        if report is None:
            raise ValueError(f"No report available for investigation '{investigation_id}'.")

        events = self.get_events(investigation_id)
        obs = self.get_observations(investigation_id)
        coverages = self.get_coverage_records(investigation_id)
        artifacts = self.get_artifacts(investigation_id)
        assessments = self.get_evidence_assessments(investigation_id)
        audit_logs = self.get_audit_logs(investigation_id)
        media_assets = [self.repository.get_media_asset(mid) for mid in inv.media_asset_ids if mid in [m.media_asset_id for m in self.repository.list_media_assets()]]

        return ReportExporter.export_html(
            investigation=inv,
            report=report,
            events=events,
            observations=obs,
            assessments=assessments,
            coverages=coverages,
            artifacts=artifacts,
            audit_logs=audit_logs,
            media_assets=media_assets,
            base_url=base_url,
        )

    def export_report_pdf(

    def get_artifact_content_path(self, artifact_id: str) -> Path | None:
        """Resolve artifact local disk path if it exists."""
        artifact = self.repository.get_artifact(artifact_id)
        return _uri_to_local_path(artifact.storage_uri)


    def get_media_assets(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[MediaAsset]:
        """Retrieve media assets associated with an investigation."""
        inv = self.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        all_assets = {m.media_asset_id: m for m in self.repository.list_media_assets()}
        return [all_assets[mid] for mid in inv.media_asset_ids if mid in all_assets]

    def get_media_asset_content_path(self, media_asset_id: str) -> Path | None:
        """Resolve media asset local disk path if it exists."""
        media_asset = self.repository.get_media_asset(media_asset_id)
        return _uri_to_local_path(media_asset.storage_uri)

    def get_evidence_assessments(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[EvidenceAssessment]:
        """Retrieve evidence assessments strictly related to an investigation."""
        self.get_investigation(investigation_id)
        events = self.get_events(investigation_id)
        obs = self.get_observations(investigation_id)
        target_ids = {e.event_id for e in events} | {o.observation_id for o in obs}
        all_assessments = self.repository.list_evidence_assessments()
        return [a for a in all_assessments if a.target_id in target_ids]

    def create_demo_investigation(self) -> tuple[Investigation, IngestionResult]:
        """Seed a realistic forensic investigation with synthetic footage, frames, observations, and events."""
        import math

        import av
        import numpy as np

        demo_dir = self.storage_dir / "demo"
        demo_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        video_path = demo_dir / "demo_perimeter_feed.mp4"

        # Generate 6-second surveillance footage with timecode and crosshairs
        fps = 24
        duration_seconds = 6.0
        width = 640
        height = 360
        total_frames = int(duration_seconds * fps)

        with av.open(str(video_path), mode="w", format="mp4") as container:
            v_stream = container.add_stream("h264", rate=fps)
            v_stream.width = width
            v_stream.height = height
            v_stream.pix_fmt = "yuv420p"

            a_stream = None
            try:
                a_stream = container.add_stream("aac", rate=44100)
                a_stream.format = "fltp"
                a_stream.layout = "stereo"
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                pass

            for i in range(total_frames):
                t_sec = i / fps
                img = np.zeros((height, width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
                # CCTV dark night surveillance aesthetic
                img[:, :, :] = 18
                # Ground grid lines
                for y in range(180, height, 30):
                    img[y, :, :] = 35
                for x in range(0, width, 50):
                    img[180:, x, :] = 35

                # Security perimeter fence line (yellow/amber warning stripe)
                img[175:180, :, 0] = 0
                img[175:180, :, 1] = 180
                img[175:180, :, 2] = 230

                # Simulated intruder moving between t=1.0s and t=4.5s
                if 1.0 <= t_sec <= 4.5:
                    prog = (t_sec - 1.0) / 3.5
                    px = int(120 + prog * 400)
                    py = int(160 - math.sin(prog * math.pi) * 20)
                    img[py:py+50, px:px+20, 0] = 220
                    img[py:py+50, px:px+20, 1] = 220
                    img[py:py+50, px:px+20, 2] = 220

                frame = av.VideoFrame.from_ndarray(img, format="bgr24")
                for packet in v_stream.encode(frame):
                    container.mux(packet)

            if a_stream is not None:
                num_samples = int(duration_seconds * 44100)
                t = np.linspace(0, duration_seconds, num_samples, dtype=np.float32)
                rumble = np.sin(2 * math.pi * 60 * t) * 0.1
                alert = np.where((t >= 2.4) & (t <= 2.9), np.sin(2 * math.pi * 880 * t) * 0.4, 0.0)
                audio = (rumble + alert).astype(np.float32)
                stereo = np.stack([audio, audio])
                a_frame = av.AudioFrame.from_ndarray(stereo, format="fltp", layout="stereo")
                a_frame.rate = 44100
                for packet in a_stream.encode(a_frame):
                    container.mux(packet)
                for packet in a_stream.encode():
                    container.mux(packet)

            for packet in v_stream.encode():
                container.mux(packet)

        # Ingest the generated video
        inv_id = f"inv-demo-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        inv, result = self.create_and_ingest_investigation(
            file_path=video_path,
            investigation_id=inv_id,
            title="Operation Nightwatch: North Perimeter Intrusion Analysis",
            objective="Identify unauthorized boundary breaches, classify acoustic anomalies, and correlate suspect trajectory.",
            scope_description="Continuous PTZ surveillance footage from North Gate Sector 4",
            camera_id="cam-north-04",
            camera_name="North Perimeter PTZ Cam 04",
            camera_location="Perimeter Sector 4 (North Border)",
            camera_timezone="UTC",
            sample_interval_seconds=1.0,
            max_frames=6,
            extract_audio=True,
            created_by="Lead Det. Vance",
        )

        prov_vis = Provenance(
            producer_type="vision_model",
            producer_name="YOLOv8x-Forensic",
            method="object_detection",
        )
        prov_aud = Provenance(
            producer_type="audio_model",
            producer_name="Whisper-Acoustic-V3",
            method="audio_transcription",
        )

        src_ref1 = SourceReference(media_asset_id=result.media_asset.media_asset_id, start_seconds=1.2, end_seconds=2.6)
        obs1 = Observation(
            observation_id=f"obs-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            modality="video",
            observation_type="person_detection",
            description="Individual dressed in dark tactical gear approaching the perimeter boundary.",
            source_references=[src_ref1],
            confidence=0.96,
            provenance=prov_vis,
        )
        self.repository.add_observation(obs1)

        src_ref2 = SourceReference(media_asset_id=result.media_asset.media_asset_id, start_seconds=2.4, end_seconds=2.9)
        obs2 = Observation(
            observation_id=f"obs-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            modality="audio",
            observation_type="acoustic_transient",
            description="High-frequency acoustic transient (880 Hz harmonic spike) matching gate latch tamper sound.",
            source_references=[src_ref2],
            confidence=0.89,
            provenance=prov_aud,
        )
        self.repository.add_observation(obs2)

        src_ref3 = SourceReference(media_asset_id=result.media_asset.media_asset_id, start_seconds=3.2, end_seconds=4.4)
        obs3 = Observation(
            observation_id=f"obs-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            modality="video",
            observation_type="motion_detected",
            description="Rapid lateral traversal across marked yellow restricted security zone.",
            source_references=[src_ref3],
            confidence=0.93,
            provenance=prov_vis,
        )
        self.repository.add_observation(obs3)

        evt1 = Event(
            event_id=f"evt-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            event_type="restricted_boundary_crossing",
            description="Subject crosses perimeter yellow line into restricted facility corridor.",
            observation_ids=[obs1.observation_id, obs3.observation_id],
            status="confirmed",
            start_seconds=1.2,
            end_seconds=3.5,
            reviewer="Lead Det. Vance",
            review_notes="Verified against ground boundary coordinates. Movement speed indicates deliberate penetration.",
            reviewed_at=datetime.now(UTC),
        )
        self.repository.add_event(evt1)

        evt2 = Event(
            event_id=f"evt-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            event_type="security_hardware_tamper",
            description="Acoustic signature of manual gate lock vibration and hardware manipulation.",
            observation_ids=[obs2.observation_id],
            status="proposed",
            start_seconds=2.3,
            end_seconds=3.1,
        )
        self.repository.add_event(evt2)

        evt3 = Event(
            event_id=f"evt-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            event_type="secondary_zone_incursion",
            description="Subject sprints toward inner building entrance.",
            observation_ids=[obs3.observation_id],
            status="under_review",
            start_seconds=3.6,
            end_seconds=4.5,
            reviewer="Analyst Chen",
            review_notes="Cross-referencing with building interior door sensor logs.",
            reviewed_at=datetime.now(UTC),
        )
        self.repository.add_event(evt3)

        as1 = EvidenceAssessment(
            assessment_id=f"assess-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            target_type="event",
            target_id=evt1.event_id,
            assessment_type="evidence_quality",
            rating="high",
            rationale="Clear multi-frame visual detection with high confidence (0.96) and uninterrupted track.",
            assessed_by="Automated Verification Agent",
        )
        as2 = EvidenceAssessment(
            assessment_id=f"assess-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            target_type="event",
            target_id=evt2.event_id,
            assessment_type="uncertainty",
            rating="moderate",
            rationale="Acoustic harmonic matches latch strike; however, physical contact is partially occluded.",
            assessed_by="Temporal Correlator Agent",
        )
        as3 = EvidenceAssessment(
            assessment_id=f"assess-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            target_type="observation",
            target_id=obs2.observation_id,
            assessment_type="support_level",
            rating="high",
            rationale="Clean spectral peak at 880Hz with 18dB SNR over background rumble.",
            assessed_by="Acoustic Signal Assessor",
        )
        self.repository.add_evidence_assessment(as1)
        self.repository.add_evidence_assessment(as2)
        self.repository.add_evidence_assessment(as3)

        try:
            self.slice_incident_clip(inv.investigation_id, evt1.event_id, padding_seconds=0.5)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.warning(f"Could not auto-slice demo clip: {exc}")

        rep = Report(
            report_id=f"rep-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}",
            investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
            title="Preliminary Forensic Report: North Perimeter Gate Breach",
            summary=(
                "Autonomous multi-modal forensic analysis confirms an unauthorized restricted boundary breach "
                "at 00:01.20 - 00:03.50 into Sector 4 corridor. Acoustic telemetry detected simultaneous gate hardware "
                "manipulation at 00:02.40. One incident sub-clip has been isolated and cryptographically indexed."
            ),
            observation_ids=[obs1.observation_id, obs2.observation_id, obs3.observation_id],
            event_ids=[evt1.event_id, evt2.event_id, evt3.event_id],
            assessment_ids=[as1.assessment_id, as2.assessment_id, as3.assessment_id],
            generated_by="Agentic Reporter & Synthesizer",
            version=1,
            status="draft",
        )
        self.repository.add_report(rep)

        st = InvestigationState(
            investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
            status=InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
            completed_at=datetime.now(UTC),
            task_ids=["task-ingest", "task-visual", "task-audio", "task-temporal", "task-verify", "task-report"],
            observation_ids=[obs1.observation_id, obs2.observation_id, obs3.observation_id],
            event_ids=[evt1.event_id, evt2.event_id, evt3.event_id],
        )
        self.repository.save_state(st)

        audit = AuditLog(
            audit_id=f"audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
            investigation_id=inv.investigation_id,
            action="investigation_update",
            actor="Lead Det. Vance",
            target_type="investigation",
            target_id=inv.investigation_id,
            notes="Demo case initialized with multi-modal forensic pipeline.",
        )
        self.repository.add_audit_log(audit)

        return inv, result

"""Telemetry broadcasting and real-time streaming service for VIGIL investigations."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import threading
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from collections.abc import Callable
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from starlette.websockets import WebSocket, WebSocketState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class TelemetryManager:
    """Thread-safe telemetry manager broadcasting investigation events to WebSocket clients."""

    def __init__(self, history_maxlen: int = 200) -> None:
        self._history_maxlen = history_maxlen
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._connections: dict[str, set[WebSocket]] = defaultdict(set)
        self._history: dict[str, deque[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(
            lambda: deque(maxlen=self._history_maxlen)
        )
        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None

    def set_event_loop(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None) -> None:
        """Register the running asyncio event loop for thread-safe cross-thread scheduling."""
        with self._lock:
            self._loop = loop

    def get_event_loop(self) -> asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None:
        """Get the current asyncio loop, or detect if called from an async context."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._loop is not None and not self._loop.is_closed():
                return self._loop
        try:
            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
            with self._lock:
                self._loop = loop
            return loop
        except RuntimeError:
            return None

    async def connect(self, investigation_id: str, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Register a new WebSocket connection and replay recent event history."""
        # Record event loop if not set
        self.get_event_loop()

        with self._lock:
            self._connections[investigation_id].add(websocket)
            history_snapshot = list(self._history[investigation_id])

        # Send connection established event
        welcome_event = {
            "investigation_id": investigation_id,
            "event_type": "subscription_acknowledged",
            "timestamp": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
            "data": {
                "message": f"Connected to telemetry stream for investigation '{investigation_id}'",
                "replayed_events": len(history_snapshot),
            },
        }
        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(welcome_event))

        # Replay past events in order
        for event in history_snapshot:
            if websocket.client_state == WebSocketState.CONNECTED:
                await websocket.send_text(json.dumps(event))

    async def disconnect(self, investigation_id: str, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Remove a disconnected WebSocket client."""
        with self._lock:
            if investigation_id in self._connections:
                self._connections[investigation_id].discard(websocket)
                if not self._connections[investigation_id]:
                    del self._connections[investigation_id]

    def broadcast(
        self,
        investigation_id: str,
        event_type: str,
        data: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> None:
        """Thread-safe dispatch of an event to all connected clients and recording in history."""
        envelope: dict[str, Any] = {
            "investigation_id": investigation_id,
            "event_type": event_type,
            "timestamp": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
            "data": data,
        }

        with self._lock:
            self._history[investigation_id].append(envelope)
            active_sockets = list(self._connections.get(investigation_id, set()))

        if not active_sockets:
            return

        payload = json.dumps(envelope)
        loop = self.get_event_loop()

        if loop is None or loop.is_closed():
            return

        async def _send(ws: WebSocket, text: str) -> None:
            try:
                if ws.client_state == WebSocketState.CONNECTED:
                    await ws.send_text(text)
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.debug(f"Error sending telemetry to websocket: {exc}")
                with self._lock:
                    if investigation_id in self._connections:
                        self._connections[investigation_id].discard(ws)

        for ws in active_sockets:
            try:
                # If currently running on the same loop, create task directly
                try:
                    curr_loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
                except RuntimeError:
                    curr_loop = None

                if curr_loop is loop:
                    loop.create_task(_send(ws, payload))
                else:
                    asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(_send(ws, payload), loop)
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.debug(f"Failed to dispatch telemetry coroutine: {exc}")

    def create_callback(
        self, investigation_id: str
    ) -> Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], None]:
        """Create a closure callback suitable for passing to AgenticOrchestrator.run_investigation."""

        def on_event(event_type: str, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            self.broadcast(
                investigation_id=investigation_id,
                event_type=event_type,
                data=data,
            )

        return on_event

    def get_history(self, investigation_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Retrieve recent telemetry events for an investigation."""
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._history.get(investigation_id, []))

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all connections and history (useful in tests)."""
        with self._lock:
            self._connections.clear()
            self._history.clear()
