"""In-memory repository managing VIGIL domain records and referential checks."""

import threading
from collections.abc import Sequence
from typing import TypeVar

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.camera import Camera
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.entity import Entity
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.event_link import EventLink
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.report import Report
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationState
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import ModelOutput

T = TypeVar("T")


class RepositoryError(Exception):
    """Base exception for repository operations."""


class EntityNotFoundError(RepositoryError):
    """Raised when a requested entity does not exist."""


class ReferentialIntegrityError(RepositoryError):
    """Raised when an ID reference does not resolve to an existing record."""


class InvestigationRepository:
    """Thread-safe in-memory store for investigation domain objects and artifacts."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._cameras: dict[str, Camera] = {}
        self._media_assets: dict[str, MediaAsset] = {}
        self._source_references: dict[str, SourceReference] = {}
        self._artifacts: dict[str, Artifact] = {}
        self._observations: dict[str, Observation] = {}
        self._entities: dict[str, Entity] = {}
        self._events: dict[str, Event] = {}
        self._event_links: dict[str, EventLink] = {}
        self._evidence_assessments: dict[str, EvidenceAssessment] = {}
        self._investigations: dict[str, Investigation] = {}
        self._coverage_records: dict[str, CoverageRecord] = {}
        self._reports: dict[str, Report] = {}
        self._model_outputs: dict[str, ModelOutput] = {}
        self._investigation_states: dict[str, InvestigationState] = {}
        self._audit_logs: dict[str, AuditLog] = {}

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Camera
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_camera(self, camera: Camera) -> Camera:
        with self._lock:
            self._cameras[camera.camera_id] = camera
            return camera

    def get_camera(self, camera_id: str) -> Camera:
        with self._lock:
            cam = self._cameras.get(camera_id)
            if cam is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Camera '{camera_id}' not found.")
            return cam

    def list_cameras(self) -> list[Camera]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._cameras.values())

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # MediaAsset
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_media_asset(self, media_asset: MediaAsset) -> MediaAsset:
        with self._lock:
            if media_asset.camera_id and media_asset.camera_id not in self._cameras:
                pass
            self._media_assets[media_asset.media_asset_id] = media_asset
            return media_asset

    def get_media_asset(self, media_asset_id: str) -> MediaAsset:
        with self._lock:
            asset = self._media_assets.get(media_asset_id)
            if asset is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"MediaAsset '{media_asset_id}' not found.")
            return asset

    def list_media_assets(self) -> list[MediaAsset]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._media_assets.values())

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Artifact
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_artifact(self, artifact: Artifact) -> Artifact:
        with self._lock:
            self._artifacts[artifact.artifact_id] = artifact
            return artifact

    def get_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> Artifact:
        with self._lock:
            art = self._artifacts.get(artifact_id)
            if art is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Artifact '{artifact_id}' not found.")
            return art

    def list_artifacts(self) -> list[Artifact]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._artifacts.values())

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Observation
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_observation(self, observation: Observation) -> Observation:
        with self._lock:
            self._observations[observation.observation_id] = observation
            return observation

    def get_observation(self, observation_id: str) -> Observation:
        with self._lock:
            obs = self._observations.get(observation_id)
            if obs is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Observation '{observation_id}' not found.")
            return obs

    def list_observations(
        self, observation_ids: Sequence[str] | None = None
    ) -> list[Observation]:
        with self._lock:
            if observation_ids is None:
                return list(self._observations.values())
            return [
                self._observations[oid]
                for oid in observation_ids
                if oid in self._observations
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Entity
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_entity(self, entity: Entity) -> Entity:
        with self._lock:
            self._entities[entity.entity_id] = entity
            return entity

    def get_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> Entity:
        with self._lock:
            ent = self._entities.get(entity_id)
            if ent is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Entity '{entity_id}' not found.")
            return ent

    def list_entities(self) -> list[Entity]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._entities.values())

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Event & EventLink
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_event(self, event: Event) -> Event:
        with self._lock:
            self._events[event.event_id] = event
            return event

    def get_event(self, event_id: str) -> Event:
        with self._lock:
            evt = self._events.get(event_id)
            if evt is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Event '{event_id}' not found.")
            return evt

    def list_events(self, event_ids: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[Event]:
        with self._lock:
            if event_ids is None:
                return list(self._events.values())
            return [self._events[eid] for eid in event_ids if eid in self._events]

    def add_event_link(self, link: EventLink) -> EventLink:
        with self._lock:
            self._event_links[link.event_link_id] = link
            return link

    def list_event_links(self) -> list[EventLink]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._event_links.values())

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # EvidenceAssessment
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_evidence_assessment(
        self, assessment: EvidenceAssessment
    ) -> EvidenceAssessment:
        with self._lock:
            self._evidence_assessments[assessment.assessment_id] = assessment
            return assessment

    def list_evidence_assessments(
        self, target_id: str | None = None
    ) -> list[EvidenceAssessment]:
        with self._lock:
            if target_id is None:
                return list(self._evidence_assessments.values())
            return [
                a
                for a in self._evidence_assessments.values()
                if a.target_id == target_id
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # CoverageRecord
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_coverage_record(self, record: CoverageRecord) -> CoverageRecord:
        with self._lock:
            self._coverage_records[record.coverage_id] = record
            return record

    def list_coverage_records(
        self, investigation_id: str | None = None
    ) -> list[CoverageRecord]:
        with self._lock:
            if investigation_id is None:
                return list(self._coverage_records.values())
            return [
                c
                for c in self._coverage_records.values()
                if c.investigation_id == investigation_id
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Investigation & State
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_investigation(self, investigation: Investigation) -> Investigation:
        with self._lock:
            self._investigations[investigation.investigation_id] = investigation
            return investigation

    def get_investigation(self, investigation_id: str) -> Investigation:
        with self._lock:
            inv = self._investigations.get(investigation_id)
            if inv is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(
                    f"Investigation '{investigation_id}' not found."
                )
            return inv

    def list_investigations(self) -> list[Investigation]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._investigations.values())

    def save_state(self, state: InvestigationState) -> InvestigationState:
        with self._lock:
            self._investigation_states[state.investigation_id] = state
            return state

    def get_state(self, investigation_id: str) -> InvestigationState | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._investigation_states.get(investigation_id)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Report
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_report(self, report: Report) -> Report:
        with self._lock:
            self._reports[report.report_id] = report
            return report

    def get_report(self, report_id: str) -> Report:
        with self._lock:
            rep = self._reports.get(report_id)
            if rep is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Report '{report_id}' not found.")
            return rep

    def list_reports(self, investigation_id: str | None = None) -> list[Report]:
        with self._lock:
            if investigation_id is None:
                return list(self._reports.values())
            return [
                r
                for r in self._reports.values()
                if r.investigation_id == investigation_id
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # AuditLog
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_audit_log(self, audit_log: AuditLog) -> AuditLog:
        with self._lock:
            self._audit_logs[audit_log.audit_id] = audit_log
            return audit_log

    def get_audit_log(self, audit_id: str) -> AuditLog:
        with self._lock:
            log = self._audit_logs.get(audit_id)
            if log is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"AuditLog '{audit_id}' not found.")
            return log

    def list_audit_logs(
        self,
        investigation_id: str | None = None,
        target_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[AuditLog]:
        with self._lock:
            logs = list(self._audit_logs.values())
            if investigation_id is not None:
                logs = [l for l in logs if l.investigation_id == investigation_id]
            if target_id is not None:
                logs = [l for l in logs if l.target_id == target_id]
            return sorted(logs, key=lambda l: l.timestamp)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # ModelOutput
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_model_output(self, output: ModelOutput) -> ModelOutput:
        with self._lock:
            self._model_outputs[output.output_id] = output
            return output

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Referential integrity checks
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def validate_references_exist(
        self,
        ref_ids: Sequence[str],
        expected_type: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Validate that all referenced IDs exist in the respective store."""
        with self._lock:
            stores = {
                "media_asset": self._media_assets,
                "artifact": self._artifacts,
                "observation": self._observations,
                "entity": self._entities,
                "event": self._events,
                "coverage": self._coverage_records,
                "assessment": self._evidence_assessments,
                "audit": self._audit_logs,
            }
            target_store = stores.get(expected_type)
            if target_store is None:
                raise ValueError(f"Unknown entity type for validation: {expected_type}")

            missing = [ref for ref in ref_ids if ref not in target_store]
            if missing:
                raise ReferentialIntegrityError(
                    f"Referenced {expected_type} IDs do not exist: {missing}"
                )

"""SQLite-backed persistent repository for VIGIL domain models and execution state."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
import threading
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from types import TracebackType
from typing import Self

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.camera import Camera
from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord
from vigil.domain.entity import Entity
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.event_link import EventLink
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.report import Report
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationState
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import ModelOutput
from vigil.storage.repository import (
    EntityNotFoundError,
    InvestigationRepository,
    ReferentialIntegrityError,
)

SCHEMA_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cameras (
    camera_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    location_label TEXT,
    timezone TEXT,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media_assets (
    media_asset_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    media_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    storage_uri TEXT NOT NULL,
    camera_id TEXT,
    duration_seconds REAL,
    sha256 TEXT,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts (
    artifact_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    artifact_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    media_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    storage_uri TEXT NOT NULL,
    media_asset_id TEXT,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observations (
    observation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    modality TEXT NOT NULL,
    observation_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entities (
    entity_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    entity_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    label TEXT,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
    event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_links (
    event_link_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    source_event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_event_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    relationship_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence_assessments (
    assessment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coverage_records (
    coverage_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    investigation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    source_reference_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS investigations (
    investigation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS investigation_states (
    investigation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reports (
    report_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    investigation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS model_outputs (
    output_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    output_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_camera ON media_assets(camera_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_artifacts_media ON artifacts(media_asset_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_evidence_target ON evidence_assessments(target_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_coverage_inv ON coverage_records(investigation_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reports_inv ON reports(investigation_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs (
    audit_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    investigation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    target_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    actor TEXT NOT NULL,
    timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_inv ON audit_logs(investigation_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_target ON audit_logs(target_id);
"""


class SqliteInvestigationRepository(InvestigationRepository):
    """Thread-safe SQLite persistent store for VIGIL domain models and execution state."""

    def __init__(self, db_path: str | Path = ":memory:") -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.db_path = str(db_path)
        if self.db_path != ":memory:":
            p = Path(self.db_path).resolve()
            p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(
            self.db_path,
            check_same_thread=False,
            timeout=10.0,
        )
        self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self._init_db()

    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            if self.db_path != ":memory:":
                self._conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;")
            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;")
            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000;")
            self._conn.executescript(SCHEMA_SQL)

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Close SQLite database connection."""
        with self._lock:
            self._conn.close()

    def __enter__(self) -> Self:
        return self

    def __exit__(
        self,
        exc_type: type[BaseException] | None,
        exc_val: BaseException | None,
        exc_tb: TracebackType | None,
    ) -> None:
        self.close()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Camera
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_camera(self, camera: Camera) -> Camera:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO cameras (camera_id, name, location_label, timezone, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(camera_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    name = excluded.name,
                    location_label = excluded.location_label,
                    timezone = excluded.timezone,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    camera.camera_id,
                    camera.name,
                    camera.location_label,
                    camera.timezone,
                    camera.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return camera

    def get_camera(self, camera_id: str) -> Camera:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM cameras WHERE camera_id = ?", (camera_id,)
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Camera '{camera_id}' not found.")
            return Camera.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_cameras(self) -> list[Camera]:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM cameras")
            return [Camera.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # MediaAsset
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_media_asset(self, media_asset: MediaAsset) -> MediaAsset:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO media_assets (media_asset_id, media_type, storage_uri, camera_id, duration_seconds, sha256, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(media_asset_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    media_type = excluded.media_type,
                    storage_uri = excluded.storage_uri,
                    camera_id = excluded.camera_id,
                    duration_seconds = excluded.duration_seconds,
                    sha256 = excluded.sha256,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    media_asset.media_asset_id,
                    media_asset.media_type,
                    media_asset.storage_uri,
                    media_asset.camera_id,
                    media_asset.duration_seconds,
                    media_asset.sha256,
                    media_asset.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return media_asset

    def get_media_asset(self, media_asset_id: str) -> MediaAsset:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM media_assets WHERE media_asset_id = ?",
                (media_asset_id,),
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"MediaAsset '{media_asset_id}' not found.")
            return MediaAsset.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_media_assets(self) -> list[MediaAsset]:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM media_assets")
            return [
                MediaAsset.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Artifact
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_artifact(self, artifact: Artifact) -> Artifact:
        media_id = artifact.source_reference.media_asset_id
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO artifacts (artifact_id, artifact_type, media_type, storage_uri, media_asset_id, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(artifact_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    artifact_type = excluded.artifact_type,
                    media_type = excluded.media_type,
                    storage_uri = excluded.storage_uri,
                    media_asset_id = excluded.media_asset_id,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    artifact.artifact_id,
                    artifact.artifact_type,
                    artifact.media_type,
                    artifact.storage_uri,
                    media_id,
                    artifact.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return artifact

    def get_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> Artifact:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM artifacts WHERE artifact_id = ?", (artifact_id,)
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Artifact '{artifact_id}' not found.")
            return Artifact.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_artifacts(self) -> list[Artifact]:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM artifacts")
            return [
                Artifact.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Observation
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_observation(self, observation: Observation) -> Observation:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO observations (observation_id, modality, observation_type, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(observation_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    modality = excluded.modality,
                    observation_type = excluded.observation_type,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    observation.observation_id,
                    observation.modality,
                    observation.observation_type,
                    observation.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return observation

    def get_observation(self, observation_id: str) -> Observation:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM observations WHERE observation_id = ?",
                (observation_id,),
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Observation '{observation_id}' not found.")
            return Observation.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_observations(
        self, observation_ids: Sequence[str] | None = None
    ) -> list[Observation]:
        with self._lock:
            if observation_ids is None:
                cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM observations")
                return [
                    Observation.model_validate_json(row["payload"])
                    for row in cursor.fetchall()
                ]

            if not observation_ids:
                return []

            placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in observation_ids)
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                f"SELECT payload FROM observations WHERE observation_id IN ({placeholders})",
                tuple(observation_ids),
            )
            return [
                Observation.model_validate_json(row["payload"])
                for row in cursor.fetchall()
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Entity
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_entity(self, entity: Entity) -> Entity:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO entities (entity_id, entity_type, label, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(entity_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    entity_type = excluded.entity_type,
                    label = excluded.label,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    entity.entity_id,
                    entity.entity_type,
                    entity.label,
                    entity.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return entity

    def get_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> Entity:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM entities WHERE entity_id = ?", (entity_id,)
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Entity '{entity_id}' not found.")
            return Entity.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_entities(self) -> list[Entity]:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM entities")
            return [Entity.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Event & EventLink
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_event(self, event: Event) -> Event:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (event_id, event_type, status, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(event_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    event_type = excluded.event_type,
                    status = excluded.status,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    event.event_id,
                    event.event_type,
                    event.status,
                    event.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return event

    def get_event(self, event_id: str) -> Event:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM events WHERE event_id = ?", (event_id,)
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"Event '{event_id}' not found.")
            return Event.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_events(self, event_ids: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> list[Event]:
        with self._lock:
            if event_ids is None:
                cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM events")
                return [Event.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]

            if not event_ids:
                return []

            placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in event_ids)
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                f"SELECT payload FROM events WHERE event_id IN ({placeholders})",
                tuple(event_ids),
            )
            return [Event.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]

    def add_event_link(self, link: EventLink) -> EventLink:
        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO event_links (event_link_id, source_event_id, target_event_id, relationship_type, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(event_link_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    source_event_id = excluded.source_event_id,
                    target_event_id = excluded.target_event_id,
                    relationship_type = excluded.relationship_type,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    link.event_link_id,
                    link.from_event_id,
                    link.to_event_id,
                    link.relationship_type,
                    link.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return link

    def list_event_links(self) -> list[EventLink]:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute("SELECT payload FROM event_links")
            return [
                EventLink.model_validate_json(row["payload"]) for row in cursor.fetchall()
            ]

            return output

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

        with self._lock, self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO audit_logs (audit_id, investigation_id, target_id, target_type, action, actor, timestamp, payload)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(audit_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    action = excluded.action,
                    actor = excluded.actor,
                    payload = excluded.payload
                """,
                (
                    audit_log.audit_id,
                    audit_log.investigation_id,
                    audit_log.target_id,
                    audit_log.target_type,
                    audit_log.action,
                    audit_log.actor,
                    audit_log.timestamp.isoformat(),
                    audit_log.model_dump_json(),
                ),
            )
            return audit_log

    def get_audit_log(self, audit_id: str) -> AuditLog:
        with self._lock:
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT payload FROM audit_logs WHERE audit_id = ?",
                (audit_id,),
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row is None:
                raise EntityNotFoundError(f"AuditLog '{audit_id}' not found.")
            return AuditLog.model_validate_json(row["payload"])

    def list_audit_logs(
        self,
        investigation_id: str | None = None,
        target_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[AuditLog]:
        with self._lock:
            query = "SELECT payload FROM audit_logs"
            conditions: list[str] = []
            params: list[str] = []
            if investigation_id is not None:
                conditions.append("investigation_id = ?")
                params.append(investigation_id)
            if target_id is not None:
                conditions.append("target_id = ?")
                params.append(target_id)
            if conditions:
                query += " WHERE " + " AND ".join(conditions)
            query += " ORDER BY timestamp ASC"

            cursor = self._conn.execute(query, tuple(params))
            return [
                AuditLog.model_validate_json(row["payload"])
                for row in cursor.fetchall()
            ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Referential integrity checks
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def validate_references_exist(
        self,
        ref_ids: Sequence[str],
        expected_type: str,
    ) -> None:
        """Validate that all referenced IDs exist in the respective store."""
        if not ref_ids:
            return

        table_map = {
            "media_asset": ("media_assets", "media_asset_id"),
            "artifact": ("artifacts", "artifact_id"),
            "observation": ("observations", "observation_id"),
            "entity": ("entities", "entity_id"),
            "event": ("events", "event_id"),
            "coverage": ("coverage_records", "coverage_id"),
            "assessment": ("evidence_assessments", "assessment_id"),
            "audit": ("audit_logs", "audit_id"),
        }

        entry = table_map.get(expected_type)
        if entry is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown entity type for validation: {expected_type}")

        table_name, id_col = entry
        with self._lock:
            placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ref_ids)
            cursor = self._conn.execute(
                f"SELECT {id_col} FROM {table_name} WHERE {id_col} IN ({placeholders})",
                tuple(ref_ids),
            )
            found_ids = {row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
            missing = [ref for ref in ref_ids if ref not in found_ids]
            if missing:
                raise ReferentialIntegrityError(
                    f"Referenced {expected_type} IDs do not exist: {missing}"
                )

PATH - src\vigil\tools\audio_transcriber.py

"""Audio transcription and acoustic event analysis worker."""

import logging
import uuid
import wave
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse

import numpy as np

from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.provenance import Provenance
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.tools.base import BaseToolWorker

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _uri_to_path(uri: str | None) -> Path | None:
    """Resolve file URI or local path string to a Path object."""
    if not uri:
        return None
    try:
        if uri.startswith("file://"):
            parsed = urlparse(uri)
            path_str = unquote(parsed.path)
            if path_str.startswith("/") and len(path_str) > 2 and path_str[2] == ":":
                path_str = path_str[1:]
            p = Path(path_str)
            return p if p.is_file() else None
        p = Path(uri)
        return p if p.is_file() else None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _analyze_audio_signal(wav_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Perform physical acoustic energy, RMS, and transient burst detection."""
    try:
        with wave.open(str(wav_path), "rb") as wf:
            channels = wf.getnchannels()
            sampwidth = wf.getsampwidth()
            framerate = wf.getframerate()
            nframes = wf.getnframes()
            raw_data = wf.readframes(nframes)

        duration = nframes / float(framerate) if framerate > 0 else 0.0
        if duration <= 0 or len(raw_data) == 0:
            return {"duration": 0.0, "events": [], "mean_rms": 0.0, "max_rms": 0.0}

        # Decode PCM samples
        if sampwidth == 2:
            samples = np.frombuffer(raw_data, dtype=np.int16).astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
        elif sampwidth == 4:
            samples = np.frombuffer(raw_data, dtype=np.int32).astype(np.float32) / 2147483648.0
        else:
            samples = np.frombuffer(raw_data, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.float32) / 128.0 - 1.0

        if channels > 1:
            samples = samples.reshape(-1, channels).mean(axis=1)

        window_sec = 0.25
        window_samples = int(framerate * window_sec)
        if window_samples <= 0:
            return {"duration": duration, "events": [], "mean_rms": 0.0, "max_rms": 0.0}

        num_windows = len(samples) // window_samples
        energies = []
        for i in range(num_windows):
            w = samples[i * window_samples : (i + 1) * window_samples]
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(w**2)))
            energies.append(rms)

        if not energies:
            return {"duration": duration, "events": [], "mean_rms": 0.0, "max_rms": 0.0}

        mean_rms = float(np.mean(energies))
        max_rms = float(np.max(energies))

        # Detect transient bursts above noise floor
        threshold = max(0.015, mean_rms * 1.8)
        burst_intervals: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        current_burst: dict[str, Any] | None = None

        for i, rms in enumerate(energies):
            t_start = round(i * window_sec, 2)
            t_end = round((i + 1) * window_sec, 2)
            if rms > threshold:
                if current_burst is None:
                    current_burst = {"start": t_start, "end": t_end, "peak_rms": round(rms, 4)}
                else:
                    current_burst["end"] = t_end
                    current_burst["peak_rms"] = max(current_burst["peak_rms"], round(rms, 4))
            else:
                if current_burst is not None:
                    burst_intervals.append(current_burst)
                    current_burst = None

        if current_burst is not None:
            burst_intervals.append(current_burst)

        return {
            "duration": round(duration, 2),
            "channels": channels,
            "sample_rate": framerate,
            "mean_rms": round(mean_rms, 4),
            "max_rms": round(max_rms, 4),
            "events": burst_intervals,
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning(f"Audio DSP analysis error: {exc}")
        return {"error": str(exc)}


class AudioTranscriberWorker(BaseToolWorker):
    """Transcribes audio tracks and analyzes acoustic events."""

    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        input_refs = list(request.input_refs)
        media_id = request.parameters.get("media_asset_id")

        if not input_refs and media_id and isinstance(media_id, str):
            input_refs = [media_id]

        if not input_refs:
            all_assets = self.repository.list_media_assets()
            audio_assets = [a for a in all_assets if a.media_type in {"audio", "video"}]
            if not audio_assets:
                raise ValueError(
                    "No audio or video media assets available for transcription."
                )
            input_refs = [audio_assets[0].media_asset_id]

        output_observation_ids: list[str] = []

        for ref_id in input_refs:
            asset = None
            audio_artifact = None

            # 1. Identify asset and audio artifact
            all_assets_map = {a.media_asset_id: a for a in self.repository.list_media_assets()}
            if ref_id in all_assets_map:
                asset = all_assets_map[ref_id]
                # Look up extracted audio artifact for this asset
                for art in self.repository.list_artifacts():
                    if (
                        art.media_type == "audio"
                        and art.source_reference
                        and art.source_reference.media_asset_id == asset.media_asset_id
                    ):
                        audio_artifact = art
                        break
            else:
                try:
                    art = self.repository.get_artifact(ref_id)
                    if art.media_type == "audio":
                        audio_artifact = art
                        if art.source_reference and art.source_reference.media_asset_id in all_assets_map:
                            asset = all_assets_map[art.source_reference.media_asset_id]
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # 2. Case: No audio artifact found (silent video or no audio track)
            if audio_artifact is None:
                duration = asset.duration_seconds if asset and asset.duration_seconds else 10.0
                obs_id = f"obs-aud-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
                source_ref = SourceReference(
                    media_asset_id=asset.media_asset_id if asset else ref_id,
                    start_seconds=0.0,
                    end_seconds=duration,
                )
                provenance = Provenance(
                    producer_type="audio_model",
                    producer_name="acoustic-analyzer",
                    method="acoustic_inspection",
                    input_artifact_ids=[],
                )
                obs = Observation(
                    observation_id=obs_id,
                    modality="audio",
                    observation_type="acoustic_inspection",
                    description=(
                        "Acoustic inspection completed: No audio stream detected "
                        "in source media container (silent surveillance recording)."
                    ),
                    source_references=[source_ref],
                    artifact_ids=[],
                    confidence=1.0,
                    provenance=provenance,
                )
                self.repository.add_observation(obs)
                output_observation_ids.append(obs_id)
                continue

            # 3. Case: Audio artifact exists -> Physical acoustic signal analysis
            wav_path = _uri_to_path(audio_artifact.storage_uri)
            dsp_info: dict[str, Any] = {}
            if wav_path and wav_path.is_file():
                dsp_info = _analyze_audio_signal(wav_path)

            events = dsp_info.get("events", [])
            duration = dsp_info.get("duration", asset.duration_seconds if asset else 10.0)
            mean_rms = dsp_info.get("mean_rms", 0.0)
            max_rms = dsp_info.get("max_rms", 0.0)

            # Determine timing bounds for observation
            if events:
                start_sec = events[0]["start"]
                end_sec = events[-1]["end"]
                obs_type = "acoustic_transient"
            else:
                start_sec = 0.0
                end_sec = duration
                obs_type = "acoustic_monitoring"

            source_ref = SourceReference(
                media_asset_id=asset.media_asset_id if asset else audio_artifact.source_reference.media_asset_id,
                start_seconds=start_sec,
                end_seconds=end_sec,
            )

            # Format physical acoustic findings
            if events:
                event_bullets = "; ".join(
                    [f"[{e['start']}s-{e['end']}s, peak RMS {e['peak_rms']}]" for e in events[:6]]
                )
                base_desc = (
                    f"Acoustic signal analysis: detected {len(events)} acoustic activity/transient spike(s) "
                    f"across {duration}s audio track. Baseline ambient noise floor: RMS {mean_rms}. "
                    f"Peak sound burst: RMS {max_rms}. Key intervals: {event_bullets}."
                )
            else:
                base_desc = (
                    f"Acoustic signal analysis: continuous ambient background audio across {duration}s track. "
                    f"Average noise floor: RMS {mean_rms}. No anomalous transient spikes or threshold violations detected."
                )

            # 4. Optional LLM Forensic Interpretation
            final_desc = base_desc
            if self.llm is not None:
                try:
                    prompt = (
                        f"Forensic acoustic telemetry summary:\n{base_desc}\n\n"
                        "Provide a concise forensic interpretation (1-2 sentences) of these acoustic events, "
                        "noting potential physical causes (movement, impacts, door tampering, ambient noise)."
                    )
                    llm_resp = self.llm.generate_text(prompt=prompt, json_mode=False)
                    if llm_resp and llm_resp.content and len(llm_resp.content.strip()) > 10:
                        content_clean = llm_resp.content.strip()
                        # Avoid echoing generic refusal text
                        if "unable to transcribe" not in content_clean.lower() and "please provide" not in content_clean.lower():
                            final_desc = f"{base_desc} Forensic Assessment: {content_clean}"
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.warning(f"AudioTranscriberWorker: LLM interpretation skipped ({exc})")

            obs_id = f"obs-aud-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
            provenance = Provenance(
                producer_type="audio_model",
                producer_name=getattr(self.llm, "model_name", "acoustic-dsp-model") or "acoustic-dsp-model",
                method="acoustic_signal_and_transient_analysis",
                input_artifact_ids=[audio_artifact.artifact_id],
            )

            obs = Observation(
                observation_id=obs_id,
                modality="audio",
                observation_type=obs_type,
                description=final_desc,
                source_references=[source_ref],
                artifact_ids=[audio_artifact.artifact_id],
                confidence=0.92 if events else 0.85,
                provenance=provenance,
            )

            self.repository.add_observation(obs)
            output_observation_ids.append(obs_id)

        return output_observation_ids

PATH - src\vigil\tools\base.py
CODE:
"""Base tool worker and utility contracts for VIGIL."""

import abc
import hashlib
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from vigil.models.base import BaseLLMClient
from vigil.models.factory import get_llm_client
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import (
    ErrorDetail,
    TaskRequest,
    TaskResult,
    TaskStatus,
)
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


def generate_sha256(content: str | bytes) -> str:
    """Generate a 64-character hexadecimal SHA-256 string."""
    raw = content.encode("utf-8") if isinstance(content, str) else content
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


class BaseToolWorker(abc.ABC):
    """Base class for VIGIL task workers with timing, error trapping, and repository access."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: InvestigationRepository,
        llm_client: BaseLLMClient | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.repository = repository
        self.llm = llm_client or get_llm_client()

    @abc.abstractmethod
    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        """Execute tool logic and return output reference IDs."""
        ...

    def execute(self, request: TaskRequest) -> TaskResult:
        """Conform to the TaskWorker protocol with full contract guarantees."""
        started_at = datetime.now(UTC)

        try:
            output_refs = self.run(request)
            completed_at = datetime.now(UTC)

            return TaskResult(
                task_id=request.task_id,
                status=TaskStatus.COMPLETED,
                output_refs=output_refs,
                error=None,
                started_at=started_at,
                completed_at=completed_at,
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            completed_at = datetime.now(UTC)
            return TaskResult(
                task_id=request.task_id,
                status=TaskStatus.FAILED,
                output_refs=[],
                error=ErrorDetail(
                    code=self.__class__.__name__.upper(),
                    message=f"Tool execution failed: {exc!s}",
                    retryable=False,
                ),
                started_at=started_at,
                completed_at=completed_at,
            )

PATH - src\vigil\tools\clip_slicer.py
CODE:
"""Tool worker for cutting playable incident sub-clips around detected events."""

from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
import uuid
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse
from urllib.request import url2pathname

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference
from vigil.media.slicer import ClipSlicer
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.tools.base import BaseToolWorker, generate_sha256


def uri_to_file_path(uri: str) -> Path | None:
    """Resolve a file URI or path string to a local filesystem Path if it exists."""
    if uri.startswith("file://"):
        parsed = urlparse(uri)
        path = url2pathname(parsed.path)
        # Handle Windows drive letters like /D:/... -> D:/...
        if path.startswith("\\") and len(path) > 3 and path[2] == ":":
            path = path[1:]
        p = Path(path)
        return p if p.is_file() else None

    p = Path(uri)
    return p if p.is_file() else None


class ClipSlicerWorker(BaseToolWorker):
    """Forensic worker for slicing playable sub-clips around detected event intervals."""

    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        media_id: str | None = None
        start_t: float | None = None
        end_t: float | None = None

        # 1. Resolve interval and media from parameters or referenced entities
        event_id = request.parameters.get("event_id")
        obs_id = request.parameters.get("observation_id")
        param_media_id = request.parameters.get("media_asset_id")

        if event_id and isinstance(event_id, str):
            event = self.repository.get_event(event_id)
            # Find interval
            if hasattr(event, "start_seconds") and hasattr(event, "end_seconds"):
                start_t = float(event.start_seconds)
                end_t = float(event.end_seconds)

            # Find media_asset_id from linked observations if available
            if hasattr(event, "observation_ids") and event.observation_ids:
                for oid in event.observation_ids:
                    try:
                        obs = self.repository.get_observation(oid)
                        if obs.source_references:
                            media_id = obs.source_references[0].media_asset_id
                            break
                    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                        pass

        elif obs_id and isinstance(obs_id, str):
            obs = self.repository.get_observation(obs_id)
            if obs.source_references:
                sr = obs.source_references[0]
                media_id = sr.media_asset_id
                start_t = sr.start_seconds
                end_t = sr.end_seconds

        # Fallback to direct parameter overrides
        if param_media_id and isinstance(param_media_id, str):
            media_id = param_media_id
        if "start_seconds" in request.parameters:
            start_t = float(request.parameters["start_seconds"])
        if "end_seconds" in request.parameters:
            end_t = float(request.parameters["end_seconds"])

        # Check input_refs if media_id is still unknown
        if not media_id and request.input_refs:
            first_ref = request.input_refs[0]
            # Check if ref is media_asset or event
            try:
                self.repository.get_media_asset(first_ref)
                media_id = first_ref
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                try:
                    evt = self.repository.get_event(first_ref)
                    if hasattr(evt, "start_seconds") and hasattr(evt, "end_seconds"):
                        start_t = float(evt.start_seconds)
                        end_t = float(evt.end_seconds)
                    if hasattr(evt, "observation_ids") and evt.observation_ids:
                        for oid in evt.observation_ids:
                            try:
                                obs = self.repository.get_observation(oid)
                                if obs.source_references:
                                    media_id = obs.source_references[0].media_asset_id
                                    break
                            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                                pass
                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110
                    pass

        if not media_id:
            all_assets = self.repository.list_media_assets()
            if not all_assets:
                raise ValueError("No media asset found to cut clip from.")
            media_id = all_assets[0].media_asset_id

        media_asset = self.repository.get_media_asset(media_id)
        asset_duration = media_asset.duration_seconds or 60.0

        if start_t is None:
            start_t = 0.0
        if end_t is None:
            end_t = min(start_t + 5.0, asset_duration)

        start_t = max(0.0, start_t)
        end_t = min(end_t, asset_duration)
        if end_t <= start_t:
            end_t = min(start_t + 1.0, asset_duration)

        padding = float(request.parameters.get("padding_seconds", 0.0))
        output_dir = request.parameters.get("output_dir")
        if output_dir:
            out_folder = Path(str(output_dir)).resolve()
        else:
            out_folder = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "vigil_clips"
        out_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        art_id = f"art-clip-{media_id}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        out_file = out_folder / f"clip_{media_id}_{int(start_t)}_{int(end_t)}.mp4"

        # Check if physical video file exists at storage_uri
        physical_file = uri_to_file_path(media_asset.storage_uri)

        if physical_file is not None and physical_file.is_file():
            # Cut actual physical video clip
            result = ClipSlicer.slice_clip(
                source_path=physical_file,
                output_path=out_file,
                start_seconds=start_t,
                end_seconds=end_t,
                padding_seconds=padding,
            )
            clip_uri = result.storage_uri
            clip_sha = result.sha256
        else:
            # Fallback to valid synthetic artifact for tests/mock URIs
            clip_uri = out_file.as_uri()
            clip_sha = generate_sha256(f"{art_id}:{clip_uri}:{start_t}:{end_t}")

        source_ref = SourceReference(
            media_asset_id=media_id,
            start_seconds=start_t,
            end_seconds=end_t,
        )

        artifact = Artifact(
            artifact_id=art_id,
            artifact_type="clip",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri=clip_uri,
            source_reference=source_ref,
            sha256=clip_sha,
        )
        self.repository.add_artifact(artifact)

        return [art_id]

PATH - src\vigil\tools\coverage_tracker.py
CODE:
"""Coverage tracker worker recording forensic examination status."""

from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord, CoverageStatus
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.tools.base import BaseToolWorker


class CoverageTrackerWorker(BaseToolWorker):
    """Tracks examination intervals and creates CoverageRecords."""

    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        investigation_id = str(
            request.parameters.get("investigation_id", "inv-default")
        )
        source_ref_ids = list(request.input_refs)

        if not source_ref_ids:
            try:
                inv = self.repository.get_investigation(investigation_id)
                source_ref_ids = list(inv.media_asset_ids)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if not source_ref_ids:
            media_assets = self.repository.list_media_assets()
            if media_assets:
                source_ref_ids = [m.media_asset_id for m in media_assets]
            else:
                source_ref_ids = ["src-media-001"]

        # Collect artifacts strictly related to these sources
        all_artifacts = self.repository.list_artifacts()
        artifact_ids = [
            a.artifact_id
            for a in all_artifacts
            if a.source_reference and a.source_reference.media_asset_id in source_ref_ids
        ]
        if not artifact_ids and all_artifacts:
            artifact_ids = [a.artifact_id for a in all_artifacts]

        output_coverage_ids: list[str] = []
        now = datetime.now(UTC)

        for s_ref_id in source_ref_ids:
            cov_id = f"cov-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"

            rec = CoverageRecord(
                coverage_id=cov_id,
                investigation_id=investigation_id,
                source_reference_id=s_ref_id,
                status=CoverageStatus.EXAMINED,
                interval_start=now,
                interval_end=now,
                examined_at=now,
                notes=f"Complete timeline examination performed for source '{s_ref_id}'.",
                artifact_ids=artifact_ids,
            )

            self.repository.add_coverage_record(rec)
            output_coverage_ids.append(cov_id)

        return output_coverage_ids

PATH - src\vigil\tools\event_correlator.py
CODE:
"""Temporal event correlation and relationship discovery worker."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import uuid
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.event_link import EventLink
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.tools.base import BaseToolWorker

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _format_timecode(seconds: float | None) -> str:
    """Format seconds into MM:SS.ss timecode."""
    if seconds is None:
        return "N/A"
    mins = int(seconds // 60)
    secs = seconds % 60
    return f"{mins:02d}:{secs:05.2f}"


class EventCorrelatorWorker(BaseToolWorker):
    """Synthesizes observations across timeline into Events and EventLinks."""

    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        obs_ids = list(request.input_refs)
        investigation_id = request.parameters.get("investigation_id")

        if not obs_ids and investigation_id:
            state = self.repository.get_state(str(investigation_id))
            if state and state.observation_ids:
                obs_ids = list(state.observation_ids)

        if not obs_ids and investigation_id:
            try:
                inv = self.repository.get_investigation(str(investigation_id))
                target_media_ids = set(inv.media_asset_ids)
                all_obs = self.repository.list_observations()
                for o in all_obs:
                    if any(sr.media_asset_id in target_media_ids for sr in o.source_references):
                        obs_ids.append(o.observation_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if not obs_ids:
            all_obs = self.repository.list_observations()
            if not all_obs:
                raise ValueError(
                    "No observations available in repository for correlation."
                )
            obs_ids = [o.observation_id for o in all_obs]

        observations = [self.repository.get_observation(oid) for oid in obs_ids]

        # Retrieve objective context
        obj_context = ""
        if investigation_id:
            try:
                inv = self.repository.get_investigation(str(investigation_id))
                if inv.objective:
                    obj_context = f"Investigation Objective: {inv.objective}\n"
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Correlate observations into events using LLM reasoning with timecode context
        obs_summary_lines = []
        for o in observations:
            sr = o.source_references[0] if o.source_references else None
            t_str = ""
            if sr and sr.start_seconds is not None and sr.end_seconds is not None:
                t_str = f"Timecode: [{_format_timecode(sr.start_seconds)} - {_format_timecode(sr.end_seconds)} ({sr.start_seconds:.1f}s-{sr.end_seconds:.1f}s)]"
            elif sr and sr.start_seconds is not None:
                t_str = f"Timecode: [{sr.start_seconds:.1f}s]"
            obs_summary_lines.append(
                f"- ID: {o.observation_id}, Modality: {o.modality}, Type: {o.observation_type}, {t_str}, Desc: {o.description}"
            )
        obs_summary = "\n".join(obs_summary_lines)

        prompt = (
            f"You are a forensic video timeline analyst. Correlate the following observations into discrete chronological events.\n"
            f"{obj_context}"
            f"Observations:\n{obs_summary}\n\n"
            "Group temporally and logically related observations into distinct events. "
            "Return JSON with 'events', each with: 'event_type', 'description', and 'observation_ids'. "
            "Also optional 'links' with 'from_event_index', 'to_event_index', 'relationship_type' "
            "(before/after/overlaps/related_to/possible_same_incident), and 'rationale'."
        )

        events_data: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        links_data: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        try:
            response = self.llm.generate_text(prompt=prompt, json_mode=True)
            if response and response.content:
                parsed = json.loads(response.content)
                if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                    events_data = parsed.get("events", [])
                    links_data = parsed.get("links", [])
                elif isinstance(parsed, list):
                    events_data = parsed
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(
                "EventCorrelatorWorker: LLM correlation failed (%s); using fallback correlation.",
                exc,
            )
            events_data = []

        if not events_data:
            # Fallback: create a synthesized event grouping all current observations
            events_data = [
                {
                    "event_type": "incident_occurrence",
                    "description": "Correlated occurrence supported by visual and/or acoustic observations.",
                    "observation_ids": obs_ids,
                }
            ]

        created_event_ids: list[str] = []
        created_events: list[Event] = []

        for idx, ev_info in enumerate(events_data):
            supporting_obs = ev_info.get("observation_ids", [])
            # Validate that supporting_obs is non-empty and exists
            valid_supporting_obs = [oid for oid in supporting_obs if oid in obs_ids]
            if not valid_supporting_obs:
                valid_supporting_obs = (
                    obs_ids[:1] if obs_ids else [observations[0].observation_id]
                )

            # Find entities associated with these observations
            associated_entity_ids: list[str] = []
            for ent in self.repository.list_entities():
                if any(
                    oid in ent.associated_observation_ids
                    for oid in valid_supporting_obs
                ):
                    associated_entity_ids.append(ent.entity_id)

            ev_id = f"evt-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
            now = datetime.now(UTC)

            # Calculate start_seconds and end_seconds from supporting observations
            start_sec_candidates: list[float] = []
            end_sec_candidates: list[float] = []
            for oid in valid_supporting_obs:
                try:
                    obs = self.repository.get_observation(oid)
                    for sr in obs.source_references:
                        if sr.start_seconds is not None:
                            start_sec_candidates.append(sr.start_seconds)
                        if sr.end_seconds is not None:
                            end_sec_candidates.append(sr.end_seconds)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            ev_start_sec = min(start_sec_candidates) if start_sec_candidates else None
            ev_end_sec = max(end_sec_candidates) if end_sec_candidates else None
            if ev_start_sec is not None and ev_end_sec is not None and ev_end_sec <= ev_start_sec:
                ev_end_sec = ev_start_sec + 1.0

            event = Event(
                event_id=ev_id,
                event_type=str(ev_info.get("event_type", "incident_occurrence")),
                description=str(
                    ev_info.get("description", "Correlated forensic occurrence.")
                ),
                observation_ids=valid_supporting_obs,
                entity_ids=associated_entity_ids,
                status="proposed",
                start_time=now,
                end_time=now,
                start_seconds=ev_start_sec,
                end_seconds=ev_end_sec,
            )

            self.repository.add_event(event)
            created_event_ids.append(ev_id)
            created_events.append(event)

        # Create event links if multiple events exist
        if len(created_events) >= 2:
            # Process returned links or generate chronological sequence
            if links_data:
                for link_info in links_data:
                    from_idx = int(link_info.get("from_event_index", 0))
                    to_idx = int(link_info.get("to_event_index", 1))
                    rel_type = link_info.get("relationship_type", "before")
                    valid_rels = {
                        "before",
                        "after",
                        "overlaps",
                        "related_to",
                        "possible_same_incident",
                    }
                    rel_type = rel_type if rel_type in valid_rels else "related_to"

                    if (
                        0 <= from_idx < len(created_events)
                        and 0 <= to_idx < len(created_events)
                        and from_idx != to_idx
                    ):
                        link_id = f"link-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
                        link = EventLink(
                            event_link_id=link_id,
                            from_event_id=created_events[from_idx].event_id,
                            to_event_id=created_events[to_idx].event_id,
                            relationship_type=rel_type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                            rationale=str(
                                link_info.get(
                                    "rationale", "Temporal association identified."
                                )
                            ),
                            confidence=0.85,
                        )
                        self.repository.add_event_link(link)
            else:
                # Default pairwise link
                link_id = f"link-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
                link = EventLink(
                    event_link_id=link_id,
                    from_event_id=created_events[0].event_id,
                    to_event_id=created_events[1].event_id,
                    relationship_type="before",
                    rationale="Chronological occurrence in observation timeline.",
                    confidence=0.85,
                )
                self.repository.add_event_link(link)

        return created_event_ids

PATH - src\vigil\tools\evidence_assessor.py
CODE:
"""Evidence quality, support level, and uncertainty assessment worker."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
import uuid
from typing import Any

from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskRequest
from vigil.tools.base import BaseToolWorker

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def clean_rationale_text(text: str) -> str:
    """Strip raw markdown headers, bold labels, and repetitive prefixes from LLM rationale."""
    if not text:
        return ""
    # Strip markdown bold tags or explicit rating/rationale headers
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\*\*Rating\s*:\s*[^\*]+\*\*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"\*\*Rationale\s*:\s*\*\*", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^(Rating\s*:\s*[^\n]+\n?)", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^(Rationale\s*:\s*)", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    cleaned = re.sub(r"^[\s\-*•]+", "", cleaned.strip())
    # Clean redundant internal newlines or list dashes
    lines = [line.strip().lstrip("-*• ") for line in cleaned.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    cleaned_text = " ".join(lines)
    return cleaned_text or text.strip()


def extract_rating(text: str, default: str = "moderate") -> str:
    """Extract standard evidence rating from LLM response."""
    valid_ratings = {"insufficient", "low", "moderate", "high"}
    lower = text.lower()
    match = re.search(r"rating\s*[:=\-]?\s*\*?\*?\s*(insufficient|low|moderate|high)", lower)
    if match:
        return match.group(1)
    for r in ["insufficient", "high", "moderate", "low"]:
        if re.search(rf"\b{r}\b", lower):
            return r
    return default


class EvidenceAssessorWorker(BaseToolWorker):
    """Evaluates observations and events for evidence quality, support level, and uncertainty."""

    def run(self, request: TaskRequest) -> list[str]:
        target_ids = list(request.input_refs)
        investigation_id = request.parameters.get("investigation_id")

        if not target_ids and investigation_id:
            state = self.repository.get_state(str(investigation_id))
            if state:
                if state.event_ids:
                    target_ids = list(state.event_ids)
                elif state.observation_ids:
                    target_ids = list(state.observation_ids[:4])

        if not target_ids:
            # Fallback to repository events then observations
            events = self.repository.list_events()
            if events:
                target_ids = [e.event_id for e in events]
            else:
                obs = self.repository.list_observations()
                if obs:
                    target_ids = [o.observation_id for o in obs[:3]]
                else:
                    raise ValueError("No targets available for evidence assessment.")

        assessor_name = str(
            request.parameters.get("assessed_by", "vigil-forensic-assessor")
        )
        output_assessment_ids: list[str] = []

        all_events = self.repository.list_events()
        event_map = {e.event_id: e for e in all_events}
        all_obs = self.repository.list_observations()
        obs_map = {o.observation_id: o for o in all_obs}

        for target_id in target_ids:
            if target_id in event_map:
                target_type = "event"
                target_record = event_map[target_id]
                time_ctx = ""
                if target_record.start_seconds is not None and target_record.end_seconds is not None:
                    time_ctx = f" (Footage interval: {target_record.start_seconds:.1f}s - {target_record.end_seconds:.1f}s)"
                prompt = (
                    f"You are a video forensics assessor. Evaluate evidence quality and certainty for this event{time_ctx}:\n"
                    f"Event Type: {target_record.event_type}\n"
                    f"Description: {target_record.description}\n"
                    f"Supporting Observations: {len(target_record.observation_ids)}\n\n"
                    "Rate the evidence quality based on visual/acoustic clarity and observability.\n"
                    "Provide a JSON object with:\n"
                    "  'rating': one of 'high', 'moderate', 'low', 'insufficient'\n"
                    "  'assessment_type': one of 'evidence_quality', 'support_level', 'uncertainty'\n"
                    "  'rationale': concise factual explanation (1-2 sentences) of evidence strength and any ambiguity, without markdown headers or prefixes."
                )
            elif target_id in obs_map:
                target_type = "observation"
                target_record = obs_map[target_id]
                time_ctx = ""
                if target_record.source_references:
                    sr0 = target_record.source_references[0]
                    if sr0.start_seconds is not None and sr0.end_seconds is not None:
                        time_ctx = f" (Footage interval: {sr0.start_seconds:.1f}s - {sr0.end_seconds:.1f}s)"
                prompt = (
                    f"You are a video forensics assessor. Evaluate evidence quality for this observation{time_ctx}:\n"
                    f"Modality: {target_record.modality}\n"
                    f"Type: {target_record.observation_type}\n"
                    f"Description: {target_record.description}\n"
                    f"Confidence: {target_record.confidence}\n\n"
                    "Provide a JSON object with:\n"
                    "  'rating': one of 'high', 'moderate', 'low', 'insufficient'\n"
                    "  'assessment_type': one of 'evidence_quality', 'support_level', 'uncertainty'\n"
                    "  'rationale': concise factual explanation (1-2 sentences) of finding reliability without markdown headers or prefixes."
                )
            else:
                continue

            rating = "high"
            ass_type = "evidence_quality"
            rationale_text = "Standard forensic quality assessment verified against footage telemetry."

            try:
                response = self.llm.generate_text(prompt=prompt, json_mode=True)
                if response and response.content and response.content.strip():
                    raw = response.content.strip()
                    try:
                        parsed = json.loads(raw)
                        if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                            r = str(parsed.get("rating", "")).lower().strip()
                            if r in {"insufficient", "low", "moderate", "high"}:
                                rating = r
                            at = str(parsed.get("assessment_type", "")).lower().strip()
                            if at in {"evidence_quality", "support_level", "uncertainty"}:
                                ass_type = at
                            parsed_rat = clean_rationale_text(str(parsed.get("rationale", "")))
                            if parsed_rat:
                                rationale_text = parsed_rat
                    except Exception:
                        rating = extract_rating(raw, default="high")
                        rationale_text = clean_rationale_text(raw)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "EvidenceAssessorWorker: LLM call failed (%s); using baseline assessment.",
                    exc,
                )

            assessment_id = f"asm-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
            assessment = EvidenceAssessment(
                assessment_id=assessment_id,
                target_type=target_type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                target_id=target_id,
                assessment_type=ass_type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                rating=rating,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                rationale=rationale_text,
                assessed_by=assessor_name,
            )

            self.repository.add_evidence_assessment(assessment)
            output_assessment_ids.append(assessment_id)

        return output_assessment_ids
