### How we expect VIGIL to work

1. Investigator submits footage. The API receives the investigation request and passes it to application services.
2. Media is ingested and indexed. VIGIL validates the input, extracts relevant metadata, prepares media for analysis, and stores the media or durable references to it.
3. The planner creates an investigation plan. Based on the objective and available capabilities, it proposes the analysis tasks required. The plan may include visual analysis, audio analysis, temporal correlation, and evidence verification.
4. The orchestrator manages execution. It coordinates agents, dependencies, execution state, and checkpoints. It determines what can run, handles results, and supports controlled replanning.
5. Visual and audio agents analyze the footage. They use tools and model adapters to identify potentially relevant objects, actions, sounds, speech, or other signals. Each output should retain its timestamp or time interval and its source reference.
6. Temporal and correlation analysis combines findings. It relates observations from different times and modalities to identify sequences, overlaps, and potentially related events.
7. Evidence verification assesses findings. It checks supporting evidence, uncertainty, conflicts, and whether a conclusion is sufficiently supported. Findings that need further work can return to the orchestrator.
8. The report writer produces the investigation report. It combines validated findings into a detailed, structured report with timelines, evidence references, and relevant video or audio clips.
9. Human review and audit complete the process. Investigators can review the report, and report versions and review decisions are recorded.

The diagram represents the intended architecture, not necessarily the current implementation. The order of execution can vary: independent audio and visual tasks may run in parallel, while dependent tasks must wait for the information they need.

## Essential VIGIL requirements

These requirements should guide every future design decision.

- Timestamp everything relevant. Preserve the media's original time information, use video-relative timestamps for exact positions within footage, and record absolute timestamps where they can be reliably established. Distinguish a point in time from an interval.
- Keep evidence traceable. Every finding should link to the source footage, relevant time interval, and any derived artifact or analysis that supports it.
- Provide incident clips. The system should identify relevant intervals and produce or reference playable clips with start and end timestamps. Clips should be derived from the original media and retain enough context for investigators to review them.
- Analyze audio and video together. A sound, spoken phrase, visible action, and associated event may provide complementary evidence. The system should correlate them without assuming that temporal coincidence proves causation.
- Distinguish detection from conclusion. A model's detection is an observation, not automatically a verified incident. Reports must represent uncertainty and conflicting evidence honestly.
- Generate detailed, evidence-grounded reports. Reports should contain an executive summary, event timeline, relevant observations, evidence clips, supporting sources, verification status, uncertainty, and limitations as appropriate.
- Support long-running investigations. Execution state and checkpoints should make progress recoverable, with a clear record of completed, failed, and outstanding tasks.
- Keep components modular. Model providers, analysis tools, agents, the planner, scheduler, repositories, and reporting should be replaceable without redesigning the entire system.

## Instructions for models planning future implementation

A model working on VIGIL should first inspect the latest repository, existing tests, documentation, and architecture diagram. It should identify what is implemented, partially implemented, missing, or inconsistent with the intended workflow before proposing code changes.

It should then:

1. Map existing files and interfaces to the architecture above.
2. Identify the next smallest meaningful implementation stage and its prerequisites.
3. Define the required contracts and responsibilities before implementing dependent components.
4. Preserve existing working behavior and avoid duplicating capabilities already present.
5. Implement one coherent stage at a time, with tests and documentation.
6. Run the appropriate project checks and report actual results.
7. Explain the next stage only after establishing that the current stage meets its exit criteria.

The model must not assume that all components in the diagram already exist, nor should it build every agent at once simply because those agents appear in the target architecture.

The central vision: VIGIL is an evidence-oriented, multimodal video investigation system. It takes footage and an investigation objective, analyzes audio and visual information, correlates events over time, verifies findings, and produces detailed reports with timestamped, reviewable clips from the original footage. Its agentic architecture should make this process adaptive and extensible, while its contracts, evidence traceability, and execution controls keep it reliable and auditable.

# VIGIL Frontend API Integration & Data Contracts

## REST API Integration

The VIGIL UI consumes the FastAPI backend exposed under `/api/v1/investigations`.

### 1. Ingestion & Case Catalog

| Endpoint | Method | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `/api/v1/investigations` | `GET` | List all registered investigations with basic metadata. |
| `/api/v1/investigations` | `POST` | Multipart upload of video footage, creating an investigation and initiating background processing. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/demo` | `POST` | Seeds a rich synthetic demo investigation with video, frames, audio, observations, and events. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}` | `GET` | Fetch full domain model of an investigation. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/status` | `GET` | Poll status, completed tasks, observation counts, event counts, and report readiness. |

---

### 2. Media Streaming & Artifact Retrieval

| Endpoint | Method | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/media` | `GET` | List registered physical media assets for this investigation. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/media/primary/content` | `GET` | Stream primary source video footage file (`video/mp4`) for browser playback. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/media/{media_id}/content` | `GET` | Stream specific media asset file. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/artifacts` | `GET` | List extracted artifacts (JPEG frames, WAV audio, H.264 incident clips). |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/artifacts/{artifact_id}/content` | `GET` | Stream or download artifact content file (image, audio, or video clip). |

---

### 3. Forensic Findings & Verification

| Endpoint | Method | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/observations` | `GET` | Retrieve raw model detections and acoustic findings with provenance. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/events` | `GET` | Retrieve correlated events, optionally filtered with `?status=confirmed`. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/assessments` | `GET` | Retrieve evidence quality and uncertainty assessments. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/events/{event_id}/review` | `POST` | Submit human verification (`confirmed`, `rejected`, `under_review`) with audit notes. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/events/{event_id}/clip` | `POST` | Slice an isolated H.264 incident clip around the event interval. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/audit` | `GET` | Retrieve chronological audit trail records. |

#### Event Review Payload Example
```json
{
  "status": "confirmed",
  "reviewer": "Det. Vance #402",
  "notes": "Verified against gate sensor and high-confidence frame detections.",
  "event_type": "restricted_boundary_crossing",
  "start_seconds": 1.2,
  "end_seconds": 3.5
}
```

---

### 4. Deliverables & Export

| Endpoint | Method | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/report` | `GET` | Retrieve structured report domain record. Query `?format=markdown` for text summary. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/report/finalize` | `POST` | Finalize report, increment version, and optionally auto-slice clips for all confirmed events. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/export/html` | `GET` | Export self-contained interactive HTML deliverable with playable embedded clips. |
| `/api/v1/investigations/{id}/export/pdf` | `GET` | Export court-ready, publication-grade PDF deliverable. |

#### Report Finalize Payload Example
```json
{
  "finalized_by": "Chief Inspector Davis",
  "title": "Official Forensic Examination Report",
  "notes": "Case concluded. All confirmed incidents verified with video clips.",
  "auto_slice_clips": true
}
```

---

## WebSocket Telemetry Protocol

### Connection URL
```text
ws://localhost:8000/api/v1/investigations/{investigation_id}/telemetry
```

### Heartbeat
To keep connection alive through proxies and firewalls:
- Client transmits: `{"type": "ping"}` or `"ping"`
- Server responds: `{"event_type": "pong"}`

### Message Payload Schema
```json
{
  "event_type": "task_started | observation_detected | event_correlated | event_reviewed | report_finalized | state_changed",
  "timestamp": "2026-10-20T23:45:00.000Z",
  "investigation_id": "inv-demo-perimeter-01",
  "data": {
    "task_type": "visual_detection",
    "message": "Visual agent extracted 6 keyframes",
    "details": {}
  }
}
```

### UI Event Mapping
The UI matches `event_type` to activate agent roster chips:

| Received `event_type` | Agent Chip Activated | UI Action Triggered |
|---|---|---|
| `plan_created` | `Planner` | Adds telemetry entry |
| `task_started` | Corresponding Agent | Highlights active worker |
| `observation_detected` | `Visual` or `Audio` | Appends observation, increments counter |
| `event_correlated` | `Temporal` | Renders new timeline marker & event card |
| `coverage_updated` | `Verifier` | Refreshes coverage & evidence assessments |
| `report_finalized` | `Reporter` | Updates deliverable status badge |
| `state_changed` | All | Full dashboard state refresh |

CODE:
# VIGIL Video Forensics Frontend & Mission Control Suite

## Overview

The **VIGIL Frontend Mission Control** is a mission-critical web application designed for human forensic investigators to collaborate with autonomous, multimodal video-and-audio analysis agents.

Grounded in the requirements defined in [`documentation/architectures/VIGIL_vison_modelContext.txt`](../architectures/VIGIL_vison_modelContext.txt), the UI bridges raw footage, automated agentic observations, temporal correlation, human verification, and tamper-evident deliverable generation.

---

## Key System Capabilities

| Capability | Frontend Component | Forensic Value |
|---|---|---|
| **Autonomous Telemetry** | Agent Mission Control Panel | Real-time observation of Planner, Visual, Audio, Temporal, Verifier, and Reporter agent execution states via WebSockets. |
| **Multimodal Synchronized Player** | Center Media Station | Frame-accurate playback of primary surveillance video and sliced incident clips with SHA-256 integrity metadata. |
| **Multi-Track Forensic Timeline** | Interactive Scrubber & Marker Track | Interactive timecode scrub bar with color-coded event intervals (`confirmed`, `proposed`, `under_review`, `rejected`) and hover inspection. |
| **Human Review Station** | Verification & Triage Panel | One-click event verification, rejection (false positives), interval adjustment, reviewer badge assignment, and audit notes logging. |
| **On-Demand Clip Slicing** | Instant Incident Slicer | Direct H.264 video sub-clip extraction around event boundaries or playhead with configurable temporal padding. |
| **Evidence Intelligence Drawer** | Bottom Expandable Repository | Tabbed views for Extracted JPEG Frames (click-to-seek), Raw Model Observations, Evidence Quality/Uncertainty Assessments, Immutable Audit Logs, and Report Summary. |
| **Cryptographic Deliverables** | Deliverables Export Menu | One-click export of publication-grade PDF documents, interactive standalone HTML packages with embedded sub-clips, and Markdown summaries. |
| **Rapid Evaluation Seeder** | Demo Case Generator | 1-click synthetic case generation (`✨ Demo Case`) to immediately inspect the full end-to-end pipeline without uploading massive video files. |

---

## Directory Structure

```text
documentation/frontend/
├── README.md                               # This documentation index
├── ui_architecture_and_components.md       # Architectural breakdown of UI layers and state
├── user_guide_and_workflows.md             # Investigator operational guide & hotkeys
└── api_integration_and_contracts.md        # REST & WebSocket integration specifications

src/vigil/ui/
├── index.html                              # Mission control HTML layout
├── style.css                               # Cyber-forensic dark HUD styling
└── app.js                                  # Application state, video player HUD, & API bindings
```

---

## Quick Start: Launching the Frontend

### 1. Start the VIGIL API Server
From the project root:
```powershell
uv run uvicorn vigil.api.app:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```

### 2. Access the Forensic Mission Control
Open your browser to:
```text
http://localhost:8000/
# or
http://localhost:8000/dashboard
```

### 3. Load or Ingest Footage
- Click **`✨ Demo Case`** (or press <kbd>D</kbd>) to instantly load a pre-configured multi-modal forensic investigation with synthetic footage, frames, observations, and verified events.
- Or click **`+ Ingest Footage`** (or press <kbd>N</kbd>) to upload local MP4/MKV video footage.

# VIGIL UI Architecture & Component Specification

## Architectural Philosophy

The VIGIL UI is built around an **Investigator-Centric Mission Control** paradigm. Recognizing that machine learning detections represent *hypotheses* rather than courtroom-ready *conclusions*, the user interface emphasizes:

1. **Traceability**: Every event links back to source time intervals and supporting observations.
2. **Immutability & Audit**: Human interventions are timestamped, attributed to investigator badges, and recorded into an immutable audit trail.
3. **Multimodal Parity**: Visual detections (objects, motion, boundary crosses) and acoustic events (speech, transients, frequency anomalies) are presented on an equal temporal footing.
4. **Zero-Latency Telemetry**: Live agent updates stream directly through WebSockets without manual polling.

```
+---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
|                                    TOP NAVIGATION BAR                                 |
| [Brand Radar] [Case Selector] [✨ Demo] | [Status Badge] [WS Dot] | [Keys] [+ Ingest] [Export v] |
+-------------------------------+---------------------------------------+---------------+
|     LEFT: MISSION CONTROL     |         CENTER: MEDIA STATION         | RIGHT: REVIEW |
|                               |                                       |               |
| * Agent Roster Chips          | * Primary / Sub-clips Tabs            | * Filter Pills|
|   (Planner, Visual, Audio,    | * Main Video Player + HUD Overlay     |   (All, Prop, |
|    Temporal, Verifier, Rep)   | * Playback Toolbar (-2s, Step, +2s,   |    Conf, Rej) |
| * Case Metrics HUD            |   Speed, Cut Clip)                    | * Search Bar  |
|   (Frames, Obs, Evts, Clips)  | * Multi-Track Forensic Timeline Bar   | * Event Cards |
| * Live Telemetry Stream       |   (Confirmed, Proposed, Rej Markers)  |   - Interval  |
|                               | * Incident Clips Gallery Grid         |   - Notes     |
|                               |                                       |   - Actions   |
+-------------------------------+---------------------------------------+---------------+
|                           BOTTOM DRAWER: EVIDENCE INTELLIGENCE                        |
| [Frames Gallery] | [Raw Observations] | [Evidence Assessments] | [Audit Trail] | [Report] |
+---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

---

## Component Breakdown

### 1. Top Navigation Bar
- **Branding & Radar Pulse**: Glowing pulse indicator denoting active forensic monitor state.
- **Active Case Picker**: Dynamic dropdown populated from `GET /api/v1/investigations`. Allows instantaneous switching between active investigations.
- **Demo Case Seeder (`✨ Demo Case`)**: Triggers `POST /api/v1/investigations/demo`, populating a full multi-modal security breach case with video, extracted frames, observations, events, and reports.
- **Case Status Badge**: Displays current pipeline state (`CREATED`, `RUNNING`, `COMPLETED`, `FAILED`).
- **WebSocket Telemetry Pill**: Indicates live stream connection status (`WS: CONNECTED` in neon green with pulse, or `WS: DISCONNECTED` in crimson).
- **Export Deliverables Menu**: Dropdown providing instant access to:
  - Court-ready PDF export (`/export/pdf`).
  - Self-contained interactive HTML export with embedded video clips (`/export/html`).
  - Markdown summary view (`/report?format=markdown`).
  - Finalize & Sign Case modal.

### 2. Left Column: Agent Mission Control
- **Agent Status Roster**:
  - `Planner`: Visualizes objective decomposition and task graphs.
  - `Visual`: Extracted frames inspection and object detection.
  - `Audio`: Demuxing and acoustic transcription.
  - `Temporal`: Multi-modal timeline correlation.
  - `Verifier`: Evidence quality, support level, and coverage analysis.
  - `Reporter`: Synthesis of forensic findings.
  - *Active State Animation*: Agent chips glow and pulse when relevant WebSocket telemetry messages are received.
- **Case Quick Metrics Bar**:
  - Displays instant counts of extracted frames, raw observations, correlated events, and sliced incident clips.
- **Live Telemetry Feed**:
  - Monospaced, chronological feed of agent state transitions, detections, and task completions.
  - Color-coded severity borders (cyan for actions, green for success/confirmations, red for errors).
  - Clear log action button.

### 3. Center Column: Media & Forensic Station
- **Media Tabs**: Toggle between full **Primary Footage** and isolated **Incident Sub-clips**.
- **Cyber Video HUD Overlay**:
  - In-screen camera identifier badge (`CAM: NORTH-04`).
  - Frame-accurate millisecond counter.
- **Playback Controls**:
  - Frame stepping forward/backward by 0.1s (<kbd>J</kbd> / <kbd>L</kbd>).
  - Speed selector: `0.25x`, `0.5x`, `1.0x`, `1.5x`, `2.0x`.
  - Quick Cut Clip (<kbd>X</kbd>): Slices an incident clip around the active playhead with 1-second padding.
- **Multi-Track Forensic Timeline**:
  - Scrub bar with dynamic progress fill and scrub-on-click/drag.
  - Color-coded event markers:
    - `Confirmed`: Neon Emerald (`#10b981`)
    - `Proposed`: Amber (`#f59e0b`)
    - `Under Review`: Sky Cyan (`#06b6d4`)
    - `Rejected`: Crimson (`#ef4444`)
  - Interactive hover tooltip displaying the precise timecode and event summary.

### 4. Right Column: Human Review Station (Phase 5)
- **Review Filters**: Quick toggles for `All`, `Proposed`, `Confirmed`, and `Rejected` events.
- **Keyword Search**: Real-time filtering by category, description, or reviewer badge.
- **Interactive Event Cards**:
  - Category tag and status badge.
  - Footage time interval (`start_seconds – end_seconds`).
  - Evidence description and prior investigator review notes.
  - Action buttons:
    - `✔ Confirm`: Confirms incident.
    - `✖ Reject`: Dismisses as false positive.
    - `✎ Edit`: Opens review modal to edit interval, category, or notes.
    - `▶ Seek`: Instantly sets video playhead to event start.
    - `✂ Cut Clip`: Extracts an isolated, reviewable H.264 video sub-clip.

### 5. Bottom Drawer: Evidence Intelligence & Audit Repository
An expandable multi-tab panel that slides up from the base:
- **Tab 1: Extracted Frames**:
  - Horizontal gallery of visual keyframes.
  - Hover reveals timestamp and frame ID; clicking immediately jumps video player to that exact moment.
- **Tab 2: Observations**:
  - Comprehensive table of raw model findings.
  - Modality badges (`video`, `audio`, `image`), finding type, timecode interval, confidence score bar, and model provenance.
- **Tab 3: Evidence Assessments**:
  - Quality, support level, and uncertainty assessments.
  - Colored ratings (`high`, `moderate`, `low`, `insufficient`).
  - Rationale and assessor entity.
- **Tab 4: Audit Trail**:
  - Tamper-evident chronological table of all investigator decisions.
  - Displays timestamp (UTC), actor badge, action, target record, state delta (old -> new), and justification notes.
- **Tab 5: Forensic Report**:
  - Executive summary preview with version tracking and direct PDF/HTML download links.

PATH - documentation\frontend\user_guide_and_workflows.md
CODE:
# VIGIL Frontend Operational User Guide & Workflows

## Standard Investigator Workflow

This guide walks through an end-to-end investigation using the VIGIL Mission Control UI.

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    Ingest["1. Ingest Footage\nor Load Demo"] --> LiveMon["2. Monitor Agent\nTelemetry (WS)"]
    LiveMon --> Scrutinize["3. Scrutinize Video,\nFrames & Audio"]
    Scrutinize --> Verify["4. Human Review:\nConfirm/Reject/Slice"]
    Verify --> Finalize["5. Finalize & Sign\nCase Deliverable"]
    Finalize --> Export["6. Export PDF / HTML\nCourt-Ready Report"]
```

---

### Step 1: Ingest Video Footage or Seed Demo Case

#### Option A: Seed Demo Case (Recommended for quick testing)
1. Click the **`✨ Demo Case`** button in the top navigation bar, or press <kbd>D</kbd>.
2. VIGIL will generate synthetic surveillance video with night-vision crosshairs and timecodes, extract frames and audio, detect intruder motion and audio transients, correlate events, and index one incident sub-clip.
3. The UI will automatically switch to the newly created demo investigation.

#### Option B: Upload Real Surveillance Footage
1. Click **`+ Ingest Footage`** in the top-right header, or press <kbd>N</kbd>.
2. Drag and drop your `.mp4`, `.mkv`, or `.avi` file onto the drop-zone (or click to browse).
3. Fill in the investigation details:
   - **Case Title**: e.g., `Breach Analysis: South Perimeter Gate`.
   - **Camera ID**: e.g., `cam-south-02`.
   - **Forensic Objective**: e.g., `Identify unauthorized entry into restricted zone between 00:00 and 01:00`.
   - **Sample Interval (s)**: Recommended `1.0s` or `2.0s`.
   - **Max Frames**: e.g., `30` to `50`.
   - **Extract Audio**: Set to `Yes` to demux acoustic tracks.
4. Click **`Launch Autonomous Agents`**. The modal closes and the case will appear in the active dropdown.

---

### Step 2: Live Agent Monitoring

As soon as an investigation is active, the left **Agent Mission Control** panel connects to the WebSocket stream:
- The **WS Indicator** turns green (`WS: CONNECTED`).
- As agents execute, their roster chips light up and pulse in blue glow.
- The **Telemetry Feed** displays real-time updates: frame extraction, visual detections, acoustic speech transcription, and multi-modal correlation.

---

### Step 3: Video Playback & Frame Scrutiny

In the center **Media Station**:
- Use the **Playback Toolbar** to review footage:
  - Press <kbd>Space</kbd> to Play / Pause.
  - Use <kbd>&larr;</kbd> / <kbd>&rarr;</kbd> to seek +/- 2 seconds.
  - Use <kbd>J</kbd> / <kbd>L</kbd> for frame-by-frame scrutiny (+/- 0.1s).
  - Adjust playback speed to `0.5x` or `0.25x` for fine motion analysis.
- Inspect the **Interactive Timeline**:
  - Yellow/Amber markers indicate proposed events requiring review.
  - Emerald markers indicate confirmed events.
  - Hover over any marker to see the timecode and event summary.
  - Click on any marker to jump the playhead to that exact moment.
- Inspect the **Extracted Frames Gallery** in the bottom drawer:
  - Click any frame thumbnail to seek the video player to that frame.

---

### Step 4: Human Review & Incident Clip Slicing

In the right **Human Review Station**:
1. Review the proposed events generated by the temporal correlation agent.
2. For each event:
   - Click **`✔ Confirm`** to mark the event as verified. A review modal will open where you can verify the time interval, enter your investigator badge ID, and record rationale notes.
   - Click **`✖ Reject`** if the event is a false positive (e.g. animal or camera glare).
   - Click **`✎ Edit`** to adjust start/end seconds or refine the category.
   - Click **`▶ Seek`** to preview the event in the video player.
   - Click **`✂ Cut Clip`** to immediately extract a standalone, reviewable H.264 video sub-clip with 1.0-second temporal padding.
3. Switch to the **Incident Sub-clips** tab in the center media panel to play back sliced incident clips.

---

### Step 5: Finalize & Sign Case Deliverable

Once all events are reviewed:
1. Click **`Export Deliverable ▾`** in the top-right header and select **`🔒 Finalize & Sign Case`**.
2. Enter:
   - **Lead Investigator Name / Badge**: e.g., `Chief Det. Vance`.
   - **Deliverable Title**: e.g., `Final Forensic Examination Report`.
   - **Chain of Custody Remarks**: Note evidence integrity and verification findings.
   - **Auto-slice clips**: Check this box to guarantee all confirmed events have sliced sub-clips embedded in the deliverable.
3. Click **`Finalize & Lock Deliverable`**.
4. The report status transitions to `FINALIZED`, a new version number is minted, and an immutable entry is added to the audit trail.

---

### Step 6: Export Deliverable

From the **`Export Deliverable ▾`** menu:
- **Download PDF Report**: Generates a publication-ready PDF containing case metadata, event tables, uncertainty assessments, and audit logs.
- **Open HTML Report**: Opens a self-contained interactive forensic HTML document with embedded video players for all sliced incident clips.
- **View Markdown Summary**: Displays a lightweight Markdown report suitable for case management records.

---

## Keyboard Shortcuts Reference

| Key | Action | Context |
|---|---|---|
| <kbd>Space</kbd> | Play / Pause footage | Video Player |
| <kbd>&larr;</kbd> / <kbd>&rarr;</kbd> | Seek -2.0s / +2.0s | Video Player |
| <kbd>J</kbd> / <kbd>L</kbd> | Frame step -0.1s / +0.1s | Video Player |
| <kbd>X</kbd> | Quick Cut Clip around playhead | Video Player |
| <kbd>F</kbd> | Toggle Fullscreen | Video Player |
| <kbd>D</kbd> | Seed & Load Demo Case | Global |
| <kbd>N</kbd> | Open Ingestion Modal | Global |
| <kbd>E</kbd> | Toggle Export Menu | Global |
| <kbd>?</kbd> | Show Keyboard Shortcuts Cheat Sheet | Global |
| <kbd>Esc</kbd> | Close any open modal or menu | Global |

PATH - tests\media_test_helpers.py
CODE:
"""Test helpers for generating real physical media files for testing."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import wave
from pathlib import Path

import av
import numpy as np


def create_test_video(
    path: Path | str,
    duration_seconds: float = 2.0,
    fps: int = 24,
    width: int = 320,
    height: int = 240,
    with_audio: bool = True,
) -> Path:
    """Generate a valid MP4 test video with visual motion and synthetic audio."""
    file_path = Path(path).resolve()
    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    total_frames = int(duration_seconds * fps)

    with av.open(str(file_path), mode="w", format="mp4") as container:
        v_stream = container.add_stream("h264", rate=fps)
        v_stream.width = width
        v_stream.height = height
        v_stream.pix_fmt = "yuv420p"

        a_stream = None
        if with_audio:
            try:
                a_stream = container.add_stream("aac", rate=44100)
                a_stream.format = "fltp"
                a_stream.layout = "stereo"
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                a_stream = None

        # Write video frames
        for i in range(total_frames):
            img = np.zeros((height, width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
            # Add dynamic colored pattern
            img[:, :, 0] = (i * 10) % 255
            img[:, :, 1] = 120
            img[:, :, 2] = 200
            frame = av.VideoFrame.from_ndarray(img, format="bgr24")
            for packet in v_stream.encode(frame):
                container.mux(packet)

        # Write audio samples if stream initialized
        if a_stream is not None:
            num_samples = int(duration_seconds * 44100)
            t = np.linspace(0, duration_seconds, num_samples, dtype=np.float32)
            sine = np.sin(2 * math.pi * 440 * t)
            stereo_audio = np.stack([sine, sine])
            a_frame = av.AudioFrame.from_ndarray(stereo_audio, format="fltp", layout="stereo")
            a_frame.rate = 44100
            for packet in a_stream.encode(a_frame):
                container.mux(packet)
            for packet in a_stream.encode():
                container.mux(packet)

        for packet in v_stream.encode():
            container.mux(packet)

    return file_path


def create_test_wav(
    path: Path | str,
    duration_seconds: float = 2.0,
    sample_rate: int = 16000,
    frequency: float = 440.0,
) -> Path:
    """Generate a standard 16-bit PCM WAV audio file."""
    file_path = Path(path).resolve()
    file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    num_samples = int(duration_seconds * sample_rate)
    t = np.linspace(0, duration_seconds, num_samples, dtype=np.float32)
    sine = (np.sin(2 * math.pi * frequency * t) * 32767).astype(np.int16)

    with wave.open(str(file_path), "wb") as wf:
        wf.setnchannels(1)  # Mono
        wf.setsampwidth(2)  # 16-bit
        wf.setframerate(sample_rate)
        wf.writeframes(sine.tobytes())

    return file_path

"""End-to-end tests for the LeadInvestigatorAgent and InvestigationPlanner."""

from vigil.agent.investigator import LeadInvestigatorAgent
from vigil.agent.planner import InvestigationPlanner
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.models.mock import MockLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationStatus
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


def test_investigation_planner() -> None:
    planner = InvestigationPlanner()
    inv = Investigation(
        investigation_id="inv-plan-001",
        title="Unauthorized Gate Entry Investigation",
        objective="Determine identity and timing of unauthorized entry",
        scope_description="Gate camera feed from 10:00 to 10:15 UTC",
        media_asset_ids=["media-asset-1"],
    )
    tasks = planner.plan_investigation(inv)
    assert len(tasks) >= 6
    task_types = [t.task_type for t in tasks]
    assert "frame_sampling" in task_types
    assert "visual_analysis" in task_types
    assert "event_correlation" in task_types
    assert "report_generation" in task_types


def test_lead_investigator_agent_full_run() -> None:
    repo = InvestigationRepository()
    mock_llm = MockLLMClient()

    # Pre-populate media asset
    asset = MediaAsset(
        media_asset_id="media-cam-north-01",
        media_type="video",
        storage_uri="file:///media/storage/gate_cam.mp4",
        duration_seconds=20.0,
    )
    repo.add_media_asset(asset)

    agent = LeadInvestigatorAgent(
        repository=repo,
        llm_client=mock_llm,
    )

    inv = Investigation(
        investigation_id="inv-live-001",
        title="Perimeter Breach at North Gate",
        objective="Verify perimeter breach and identify associated entities",
        scope_description="North gate camera feed recorded at 2026-10-19T22:00:00Z",
        media_asset_ids=["media-cam-north-01"],
        created_by="officer-smith",
    )

    outcome = agent.run_investigation(inv)

    # Assert investigation completed successfully
    assert outcome.state.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED
    assert outcome.state.started_at is not None
    assert outcome.state.completed_at is not None
    assert len(outcome.task_results) > 0

    # Verify findings were recorded in state and repository
    assert len(outcome.state.task_ids) == len(outcome.task_results)
    assert len(outcome.state.observation_ids) > 0
    assert len(outcome.state.event_ids) > 0

    # Verify final report
    assert outcome.report is not None
    assert outcome.report.investigation_id == "inv-live-001"
    assert len(outcome.report.observation_ids) > 0
    assert len(outcome.report.event_ids) > 0
    assert len(outcome.report.assessment_ids) > 0
    assert len(outcome.report.coverage_ids) > 0
    assert outcome.report.summary != ""


def test_agent_run_investigation_with_event_callback() -> None:
    repo = InvestigationRepository()
    mock_llm = MockLLMClient()

    asset = MediaAsset(
        media_asset_id="media-event-01",
        media_type="video",
        storage_uri="file:///media/storage/event_cam.mp4",
        duration_seconds=10.0,
    )
    repo.add_media_asset(asset)

    agent = LeadInvestigatorAgent(
        repository=repo,
        llm_client=mock_llm,
    )

    inv = Investigation(
        investigation_id="inv-cb-001",
        title="Callback Test Investigation",
        objective="Verify event notifications",
        scope_description="Testing event callback",
        media_asset_ids=["media-event-01"],
    )

    recorded_events: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []

    def on_event(event_type: str, data: dict) -> None:
        recorded_events.append((event_type, data))

    outcome = agent.run_investigation(inv, event_callback=on_event)
    assert outcome.state.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED

    event_types = [ev[0] for ev in recorded_events]
    assert "state_changed" in event_types
    assert "plan_created" in event_types
    assert "task_started" in event_types
    assert "task_completed" in event_types
    assert "observation_detected" in event_types
    assert "investigation_completed" in event_types

CODE:
"""Unit tests for specialized forensic agents."""

from vigil.agent.registry import create_default_executor
from vigil.agent.specialized import (
    AudioAgent,
    EvidenceVerificationAgent,
    ReportWriterAgent,
    TemporalCorrelationAgent,
    VisualAgent,
)
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.models.mock import MockLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import (
    InvestigationState,
    InvestigationStatus,
)
from vigil.orchestration.service import OrchestrationService
from vigil.orchestration.task_types import TaskStatus
from vigil.storage.repository import InvestigationRepository


def setup_forensic_environment() -> tuple[
    InvestigationRepository, OrchestrationService, InvestigationState, MediaAsset
]:
    repo = InvestigationRepository()
    mock_llm = MockLLMClient()
    executor = create_default_executor(repository=repo, llm_client=mock_llm)
    service = OrchestrationService(executor=executor)

    asset = MediaAsset(
        media_asset_id="media-test-001",
        media_type="video",
        storage_uri="file:///storage/video.mp4",
        duration_seconds=20.0,
    )
    repo.add_media_asset(asset)

    inv = Investigation(
        investigation_id="inv-spec-001",
        title="Test Investigation",
        objective="Verify specialized agents",
        scope_description="Testing timeline",
        media_asset_ids=["media-test-001"],
    )
    repo.add_investigation(inv)

    state = InvestigationState(
        investigation_id="inv-spec-001",
        status=InvestigationStatus.CREATED,
    )
    repo.save_state(state)

    return repo, service, state, asset


def test_visual_and_audio_agents() -> None:
    repo, service, state, asset = setup_forensic_environment()

    # Visual agent
    vis_agent = VisualAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    outcome_frames = vis_agent.extract_frames(
        state=state, media_asset_ids=[asset.media_asset_id], max_frames=3
    )
    assert outcome_frames.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert len(outcome_frames.task_result.output_refs) > 0

    outcome_analysis = vis_agent.analyze_frames(
        state=outcome_frames.state,
        artifact_ids=outcome_frames.task_result.output_refs,
    )
    assert outcome_analysis.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert len(outcome_analysis.task_result.output_refs) > 0

    # Audio agent
    audio_agent = AudioAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    outcome_audio = audio_agent.analyze_audio(
        state=outcome_analysis.state, media_refs=[asset.media_asset_id]
    )
    assert outcome_audio.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert len(outcome_audio.task_result.output_refs) > 0


def test_correlation_verification_and_report_agents() -> None:
    repo, service, state, asset = setup_forensic_environment()

    # Pre-populate visual frames & analysis
    vis_agent = VisualAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    f_out = vis_agent.extract_frames(
        state=state, media_asset_ids=[asset.media_asset_id], max_frames=2
    )
    a_out = vis_agent.analyze_frames(
        state=f_out.state, artifact_ids=f_out.task_result.output_refs
    )

    # Correlation agent
    corr_agent = TemporalCorrelationAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    c_out = corr_agent.correlate(
        state=a_out.state,
        observation_ids=a_out.task_result.output_refs,
    )
    assert c_out.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    assert len(c_out.task_result.output_refs) > 0

    # Verification agent
    verif_agent = EvidenceVerificationAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    v_out = verif_agent.verify_evidence(
        state=c_out.state,
        target_ids=c_out.task_result.output_refs,
    )
    assert v_out.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED

    cov_out = verif_agent.evaluate_coverage(
        state=v_out.state,
        investigation_id="inv-spec-001",
        source_ref_ids=[asset.media_asset_id],
    )
    assert cov_out.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED

    # Report writer agent
    rep_agent = ReportWriterAgent(repository=repo, service=service)
    r_out = rep_agent.generate_report(
        state=cov_out.state,
        investigation_id="inv-spec-001",
        title="Specialized Agent Report",
    )
    assert r_out.task_result.status == TaskStatus.COMPLETED
    report_id = r_out.task_result.output_refs[0]
    report = repo.get_report(report_id)
    assert report.investigation_id == "inv-spec-001"
    assert report.title == "Specialized Agent Report"

"""Unit tests for InvestigationService and TelemetryManager."""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from tests.media_test_helpers import create_test_video
from vigil.models.mock import MockLLMClient
from vigil.orchestration.investigation_state import InvestigationStatus
from vigil.services.investigation_service import InvestigationService
from vigil.services.telemetry import TelemetryManager
from vigil.storage.repository import EntityNotFoundError
from vigil.storage.sqlite_repository import SqliteInvestigationRepository


def test_telemetry_manager_broadcast_and_history() -> None:
    telemetry = TelemetryManager(history_maxlen=10)
    inv_id = "inv-test-telemetry"

    # Broadcast events
    telemetry.broadcast(inv_id, "test_event_1", {"msg": "hello"})
    telemetry.broadcast(inv_id, "test_event_2", {"msg": "world"})

    history = telemetry.get_history(inv_id)
    assert len(history) == 2
    assert history[0]["event_type"] == "test_event_1"
    assert history[0]["data"]["msg"] == "hello"
    assert history[1]["event_type"] == "test_event_2"

    # Callback generator
    cb = telemetry.create_callback(inv_id)
    cb("callback_event", {"status": "ok"})
    history3 = telemetry.get_history(inv_id)
    assert len(history3) == 3
    assert history3[2]["event_type"] == "callback_event"


def test_investigation_service_lifecycle_sync(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    video_path = tmp_path / "service_test.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.5, fps=24, with_audio=True)

    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(":memory:")
    telemetry = TelemetryManager()
    service = InvestigationService(
        repository=repo,
        telemetry=telemetry,
        storage_dir=tmp_path / "storage",
        orchestrator=None,  # defaults with mock LLM if not configured
    )
    # Ensure orchestrator uses mock LLM for testing
    service.orchestrator.llm = MockLLMClient()

    # 1. Create and ingest
    inv, result = service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
        file_path=video_path,
        title="Service Unit Test Investigation",
        objective="Verify service lifecycle",
        scope_description="Testing sync investigation execution",
        camera_id="cam-service-01",
        sample_interval_seconds=0.5,
        max_frames=4,
    )

    assert inv.investigation_id.startswith("inv-")
    assert result.media_asset.media_asset_id.startswith("media-")
    assert len(result.frame_artifacts) > 0

    # Status right after creation
    status_created = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)
    assert status_created.status == InvestigationStatus.CREATED
    assert status_created.has_report is False
    assert status_created.completed_tasks == 0

    # 2. Run investigation synchronously
    outcome = service.run_investigation(inv.investigation_id, run_async=False)
    assert outcome is not None
    assert outcome.state.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED
    assert outcome.report is not None

    # Status after completion
    status_done = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)
    assert status_done.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED
    assert status_done.has_report is True
    assert status_done.observation_count > 0
    assert status_done.event_count > 0
    assert status_done.coverage_count > 0

    # 3. Retrieve report
    report = service.get_report(inv.investigation_id)
    assert report is not None
    assert report.investigation_id == inv.investigation_id

    # 4. Query entities
    obs_list = service.get_observations(inv.investigation_id)
    assert len(obs_list) > 0
    evt_list = service.get_events(inv.investigation_id)
    assert len(evt_list) > 0
    cov_list = service.get_coverage_records(inv.investigation_id)
    assert len(cov_list) > 0
    art_list = service.get_artifacts(inv.investigation_id)
    assert len(art_list) > 0

    # 5. List investigations
    invs = service.list_investigations()
    assert len(invs) == 1
    assert invs[0].investigation_id == inv.investigation_id

    service.shutdown()


def test_investigation_service_async_execution(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    video_path = tmp_path / "async_test.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.0, fps=24, with_audio=False)

    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(":memory:")
    telemetry = TelemetryManager()
    service = InvestigationService(
        repository=repo,
        telemetry=telemetry,
        storage_dir=tmp_path / "storage",
    )
    service.orchestrator.llm = MockLLMClient()

    inv, _ = service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
        file_path=video_path,
        title="Async Test Investigation",
        sample_interval_seconds=0.5,
        max_frames=3,
    )

    # Run async
    service.run_investigation(inv.investigation_id, run_async=True)

    # Wait for completion (up to 15s)
    deadline = time.time() + 15.0
    completed = False
    while time.time() < deadline:
        st = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)
        if st.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED:
            completed = True
            break
        time.sleep(0.2)

    assert completed is True
    status_done = service.get_status(inv.investigation_id)
    assert status_done.has_report is True

    service.shutdown()


def test_investigation_service_errors(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(":memory:")
    service = InvestigationService(repository=repo)

    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
            file_path=tmp_path / "non_existent.mp4"
        )

    with pytest.raises(EntityNotFoundError):
        service.get_status("unknown-inv-id")

    with pytest.raises(EntityNotFoundError):
        service.get_report("unknown-inv-id")

    service.shutdown()

def test_post_investigations_with_file_upload_sync(test_setup) -> None:
    client, _service, tmp_path = test_setup
    video_path = tmp_path / "upload_sample.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.0, fps=24, with_audio=True)

    with open(video_path, "rb") as f:
        res = client.post(
            "/api/v1/investigations",
            files={"file": ("upload_sample.mp4", f, "video/mp4")},
            data={
                "title": "Uploaded Breach Footage",
                "objective": "Identify intruders",
                "camera_id": "cam-gate-01",
                "sample_interval_seconds": "0.5",
                "max_frames": "3",
                "run_async": "false",
            },
        )

    assert res.status_code == 202
    data = res.json()
    assert "investigation_id" in data
    assert data["title"] == "Uploaded Breach Footage"
    assert data["camera_id"] == "cam-gate-01"
    assert data["frame_count"] > 0
    assert data["status"] == "completed"

    inv_id = data["investigation_id"]

    # 1. Check status endpoint
    status_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/status")
    assert status_res.status_code == 200
    st_data = status_res.json()
    assert st_data["status"] == "completed"
    assert st_data["has_report"] is True
    assert st_data["observation_count"] > 0
    assert st_data["event_count"] > 0

    # 2. Check report endpoint (JSON)
    rep_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/report")
    assert rep_res.status_code == 200
    rep_data = rep_res.json()
    assert rep_data["investigation_id"] == inv_id
    assert rep_data["summary"] != ""

    # 3. Check report endpoint (Markdown)
    rep_md_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/report?format=markdown")
    assert rep_md_res.status_code == 200
    assert "text/markdown" in rep_md_res.headers["content-type"]
    assert "# " in rep_md_res.text
    assert "Executive Summary" in rep_md_res.text

    # 4. Check list investigations
    list_res = client.get("/api/v1/investigations")
    assert list_res.status_code == 200
    items = list_res.json()
    assert len(items) == 1
    assert items[0]["investigation_id"] == inv_id

    # 5. Check investigation details
    det_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}")
    assert det_res.status_code == 200
    assert det_res.json()["investigation_id"] == inv_id

    # 6. Check query sub-endpoints
    obs_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/observations")
    assert obs_res.status_code == 200
    assert len(obs_res.json()) > 0

    evt_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/events")
    assert evt_res.status_code == 200
    assert len(evt_res.json()) > 0

    cov_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/coverage")
    assert cov_res.status_code == 200
    assert len(cov_res.json()) > 0

    art_res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/artifacts")
    assert art_res.status_code == 200
    assert len(art_res.json()) > 0


def test_get_report_conflict_when_running(test_setup, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client, service, _ = test_setup
    video_path = tmp_path / "running_test.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.0)

    # Ingest without running
    inv, _ = service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
        file_path=video_path,
        title="Pending Run",
    )

    # State is CREATED -> report query should return 409 Conflict
    res = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv.investigation_id}/report")
    assert res.status_code == 409
    assert "Report has not yet been generated" in res.json()["detail"]

def test_create_demo_and_media_streaming(media_setup):
    client, _service, _ = media_setup

    # 1. Create demo case
    res_demo = client.post("/api/v1/investigations/demo")
    assert res_demo.status_code == 201
    data = res_demo.json()
    inv_id = data["investigation_id"]
    assert inv_id.startswith("inv-demo-")
    assert data["status"] == "completed"
    assert data["frame_count"] > 0
    assert data["has_audio"] is True

    # 2. Get media assets list
    res_media = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/media")
    assert res_media.status_code == 200
    media_list = res_media.json()
    assert len(media_list) >= 1
    media_asset_id = media_list[0]["media_asset_id"]

    # 3. Stream primary media
    res_stream_prim = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/media/primary/content")
    assert res_stream_prim.status_code == 200
    assert "video/mp4" in res_stream_prim.headers["content-type"]
    assert len(res_stream_prim.content) > 0

    # 4. Stream by media asset ID
    res_stream_id = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/media/{media_asset_id}/content")
    assert res_stream_id.status_code == 200
    assert "video/mp4" in res_stream_id.headers["content-type"]

    # 5. Get evidence assessments
    res_assess = client.get(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/assessments")
    assert res_assess.status_code == 200
    assess_list = res_assess.json()
    assert len(assess_list) >= 1
    assert "assessment_type" in assess_list[0]

        event_type="noise_trigger",
        description="Loud crash sound.",
        observation_ids=["obs-test-02"],
        status="proposed",
        start_seconds=2.6,
        end_seconds=3.2,
    )
    repo.add_event(event1)
    repo.add_event(event2)

    state = InvestigationState(
        investigation_id="inv-rev-test",
        status=InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED,
        completed_at=now,
        event_ids=["evt-test-01", "evt-test-02"],
        observation_ids=["obs-test-01", "obs-test-02"],
    )
    repo.save_state(state)

    report = Report(
        report_id="rep-test-01",
        investigation_id="inv-rev-test",
        title="Initial Draft Report",
        summary="Automated agent analysis recorded 2 events.",
        generated_by="vigil-agent",
        version=1,
        status="draft",
    )
    repo.add_report(report)

    app = create_app()

    app.dependency_overrides[get_telemetry_manager] = lambda: telemetry

    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        yield client, service, video_file
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def test_review_event_endpoints(review_setup) -> None:
    client, _service, _ = review_setup

    # 1. POST review event -> confirmed
    res = client.post(
        "/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events/evt-test-01/review",
        json={
            "status": "confirmed",
            "reviewer": "investigator-dan",
            "notes": "Verified badge unauthorized on camera.",
            "description": "Unauthorized worker entered restricted bay.",
        },
    )
    assert res.status_code == 200
    data = res.json()
    assert data["event"]["status"] == "confirmed"
    assert data["event"]["reviewer"] == "investigator-dan"
    assert data["event"]["description"] == "Unauthorized worker entered restricted bay."
    assert data["audit_log"]["action"] == "event_review"
    assert data["audit_log"]["changes"]["status"]["new"] == "confirmed"

    # 2. POST review event 2 -> rejected
    res2 = client.post(
        "/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events/evt-test-02/review",
        json={
            "status": "rejected",
            "reviewer": "investigator-dan",
            "notes": "Normal machinery operation sound.",
        },
    )
    assert res2.status_code == 200
    assert res2.json()["event"]["status"] == "rejected"

    # 3. GET events with status filter
    res_conf = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events?status=confirmed")
    assert res_conf.status_code == 200
    assert len(res_conf.json()) == 1
    assert res_conf.json()[0]["event_id"] == "evt-test-01"

    res_rej = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events?status=rejected")
    assert res_rej.status_code == 200
    assert len(res_rej.json()) == 1
    assert res_rej.json()[0]["event_id"] == "evt-test-02"

    # 4. GET single event
    res_single = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events/evt-test-01")
    assert res_single.status_code == 200
    assert res_single.json()["status"] == "confirmed"

def test_finalize_and_export_endpoints(review_setup) -> None:
    client, _service, _ = review_setup

    # First confirm an event so an incident clip is sliced during finalization
    client.post(
        "/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/events/evt-test-01/review",
        json={
            "status": "confirmed",
            "reviewer": "lead-agent-smith",
            "notes": "Confirmed incident on tape.",
        },
    )

    res_fin = client.post(
        "/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/report/finalize",
        json={
            "finalized_by": "lead-agent-smith",
            "notes": "Full review complete, clips compiled.",
            "auto_slice_clips": True,
        },
    )
    assert res_fin.status_code == 200
    fin_data = res_fin.json()
    assert fin_data["version"] == 2
    assert fin_data["report"]["status"] == "finalized"
    assert fin_data["report"]["finalized_by"] == "lead-agent-smith"

    # Export HTML
    res_html = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/export/html")
    assert res_html.status_code == 200
    assert "text/html" in res_html.headers["content-type"]
    assert "VIGIL Forensic Report" in res_html.text
    assert "<video" in res_html.text

    # Export PDF
    res_pdf = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/export/pdf")
    assert res_pdf.status_code == 200
    assert "application/pdf" in res_pdf.headers["content-type"]
    assert res_pdf.content.startswith(b"%PDF-")

    # Format query on /report
    res_rep_html = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/report?format=html")
    assert res_rep_html.status_code == 200
    assert "text/html" in res_rep_html.headers["content-type"]

    res_rep_pdf = client.get("/api/v1/investigations/inv-rev-test/report?format=pdf")
    assert res_rep_pdf.status_code == 200
    assert "application/pdf" in res_rep_pdf.headers["content-type"]

PATH - tests\unit\api\test_websocket_telemetry.py
CODE:
"""Unit tests for VIGIL WebSocket real-time telemetry streaming."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from starlette.testclient import TestClient

from tests.media_test_helpers import create_test_video
from vigil.api.app import create_app
from vigil.api.dependencies import (
    get_investigation_service,
    get_telemetry_manager,
)
from vigil.models.mock import MockLLMClient
from vigil.services.investigation_service import InvestigationService
from vigil.services.telemetry import TelemetryManager
from vigil.storage.sqlite_repository import SqliteInvestigationRepository


@pytest.fixture
def ws_test_setup(tmp_path: Path):
    """Fixture providing app and client with WebSocket support."""
    repo = SqliteInvestigationRepository(":memory:")
    telemetry = TelemetryManager()
    service = InvestigationService(
        repository=repo,
        telemetry=telemetry,
        storage_dir=tmp_path / "storage",
    )
    service.orchestrator.llm = MockLLMClient()

    app = create_app()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_investigation_service] = lambda: service
    app.dependency_overrides[get_telemetry_manager] = lambda: telemetry

    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        yield client, service, telemetry, tmp_path
    finally:
        service.shutdown(wait=False)


def test_websocket_connect_and_ping(ws_test_setup) -> None:
    client, _, _, _ = ws_test_setup
    inv_id = "inv-ws-ping"

    with client.websocket_connect(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/telemetry") as ws:
        # First message is connection acknowledgment
        init_data = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert init_data["event_type"] == "subscription_acknowledged"
        assert init_data["investigation_id"] == inv_id

        # Send ping
        ws.send_text("ping")
        pong = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert pong["event_type"] == "pong"


def test_websocket_alias_and_event_replay(ws_test_setup) -> None:
    client, _, telemetry, _ = ws_test_setup
    inv_id = "inv-ws-replay"

    # Pre-populate some history in telemetry before client connects
    telemetry.broadcast(inv_id, "pre_event_1", {"index": 1})
    telemetry.broadcast(inv_id, "pre_event_2", {"index": 2})

    with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/investigations/{inv_id}") as ws:
        # 1. Welcome message
        welcome = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert welcome["event_type"] == "subscription_acknowledged"
        assert welcome["data"]["replayed_events"] == 2

        # 2. Replayed event 1
        ev1 = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert ev1["event_type"] == "pre_event_1"
        assert ev1["data"]["index"] == 1

        # 3. Replayed event 2
        ev2 = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert ev2["event_type"] == "pre_event_2"
        assert ev2["data"]["index"] == 2


def test_websocket_live_investigation_streaming(ws_test_setup) -> None:
    client, service, _, tmp_path = ws_test_setup
    video_path = tmp_path / "ws_stream.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.0, fps=24, with_audio=False)

    inv, _ = service.create_and_ingest_investigation(
        file_path=video_path,
        title="Live WebSocket Stream Test",
        sample_interval_seconds=0.5,
        max_frames=3,
    )
    inv_id = inv.investigation_id

    received_event_types: list[str] = []

    with client.websocket_connect(f"/api/v1/investigations/{inv_id}/telemetry") as ws:
        # Initial ack
        ack = json.loads(ws.receive_text())
        assert ack["event_type"] == "subscription_acknowledged"

        # Run investigation synchronously (which dispatches events to telemetry)
        service.run_investigation(inv_id, run_async=False)

        # Collect live streamed messages
        while True:
            try:
                # Use a small timeout or non-blocking check
                raw = ws.receive_text()
                data = json.loads(raw)
                received_event_types.append(data["event_type"])
                if data["event_type"] == "investigation_completed":
                    break
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                break

    # Verify all expected lifecycle telemetry events were received
    assert "state_changed" in received_event_types
    assert "plan_created" in received_event_types
    assert "task_started" in received_event_types
    assert "task_completed" in received_event_types
    assert "observation_detected" in received_event_types
    assert "investigation_completed" in received_event_types

CODE:
"""Unit tests for Phase 5 AuditLog, Event review fields, and Report versioning domain schemas."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from vigil.domain.audit import AuditLog
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.report import Report


def test_audit_log_valid_creation() -> None:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    log = AuditLog(
        audit_id="audit-001",
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        action="event_review",
        actor="investigator-alice",
        timestamp=now,
        target_type="event",
        target_id="evt-001",
        changes={"status": {"old": "proposed", "new": "confirmed"}},
        notes="Verified suspect match on gate camera.",
        metadata={"camera_id": "cam-gate-01"},
    )

    assert log.audit_id == "audit-001"
    assert log.investigation_id == "inv-001"
    assert log.action == "event_review"
    assert log.actor == "investigator-alice"
    assert log.timestamp == now
    assert log.target_type == "event"
    assert log.target_id == "evt-001"
    assert log.changes["status"]["new"] == "confirmed"
    assert log.notes == "Verified suspect match on gate camera."


def test_audit_log_rejects_blank_actor() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        AuditLog(
            audit_id="audit-002",
            investigation_id="inv-001",
            action="event_review",
            actor="   ",
            target_type="event",
            target_id="evt-001",
        )


def test_audit_log_immutability() -> None:
    log = AuditLog(
        audit_id="audit-003",
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        action="event_review",
        actor="investigator-bob",
        target_type="event",
        target_id="evt-001",
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        log.actor = "someone-else"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_event_with_review_and_seconds_fields() -> None:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    event = Event(
        event_id="evt-001",
        event_type="perimeter_breach",
        description="Individual climbs south perimeter fence.",
        observation_ids=["obs-001"],
        status="confirmed",
        start_seconds=12.5,
        end_seconds=18.0,
        reviewer="badge-4402",
        review_notes="Clear footage, verified breach.",
        reviewed_at=now,
    )

    assert event.status == "confirmed"
    assert event.start_seconds == 12.5
    assert event.end_seconds == 18.0
    assert event.reviewer == "badge-4402"
    assert event.review_notes == "Clear footage, verified breach."
    assert event.reviewed_at == now


def test_event_rejects_reversed_seconds() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Event end seconds cannot precede start seconds"):
        Event(
            event_id="evt-002",
            event_type="entry",
            description="Reversed test",
            observation_ids=["obs-001"],
            start_seconds=20.0,
            end_seconds=10.0,
        )


def test_report_versioning_and_finalization_fields() -> None:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    report = Report(
        report_id="rep-001",
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        title="Final Forensic Report",
        summary="Detailed incident report.",
        generated_by="investigator-lead",
        version=2,
        status="finalized",
        clip_artifact_ids=["clip-001", "clip-002"],
        notes="All findings verified and court-ready.",
        finalized_at=now,
        finalized_by="lead-detective-carter",
    )

    assert report.version == 2
    assert report.status == "finalized"
    assert report.clip_artifact_ids == ["clip-001", "clip-002"]
    assert report.notes == "All findings verified and court-ready."
    assert report.finalized_at == now
    assert report.finalized_by == "lead-detective-carter"

"""Tests for VIGIL evidence and interpretation schemas."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from vigil.domain.entity import Entity
from vigil.domain.event import Event
from vigil.domain.event_link import EventLink
from vigil.domain.evidence_assessment import EvidenceAssessment
from vigil.domain.observation import Observation
from vigil.domain.provenance import Provenance
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference


def make_provenance() -> Provenance:
    """Create valid provenance for test observations."""
    return Provenance(
        producer_type="vision_model",
        producer_name="test-vision-model",
        method="frame_description",
    )


def make_observation(
    observation_id: str = "obs_001",
) -> Observation:
    """Create a valid test observation."""
    return Observation(
        observation_id=observation_id,
        modality="video",
        observation_type="person_detection",
        description="A person appears in the frame.",
        source_references=[
            SourceReference(
                media_asset_id="media_001",
                start_seconds=10.0,
                end_seconds=12.0,
            )
        ],
        provenance=make_provenance(),
    )


def test_observation_requires_source_reference() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Observation(
            observation_id="obs_001",
            modality="video",
            observation_type="person_detection",
            description="A person appears.",
            source_references=[],
            provenance=make_provenance(),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):

            observation_id="obs_001",
            modality="video",
            observation_type="person_detection",
            description="A person appears.",
            source_references=[
                SourceReference(

                    start_seconds=10.0,

                )
            ],
            provenance=make_provenance(),
            confidence=1.5,
        )


def test_provenance_rejects_duplicate_artifact_ids() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Provenance(
            producer_type="vision_model",
            producer_name="test-model",
            method="detection",
            input_artifact_ids=["artifact_001", "artifact_001"],
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Entity(
            entity_id="entity_001",
            entity_type="person",
            associated_observation_ids=[],
        )

    entity = Entity(

        )

    link = EventLink(
        event_link_id="link_001",
        from_event_id="event_001",
        to_event_id="event_002",
        relationship_type="before",
        rationale="The first event preceded the second.",
    )

    assert link.relationship_type == "before"

        target_id="obs_001",
        assessment_type="evidence_quality",
        rating="moderate",

        assessed_by="reviewer_001",
    )

    assert assessment.target_id == "obs_001"

        EvidenceAssessment(
            assessment_id="assessment_001",
            target_type="event",
            target_id="event_001",

            assessed_by="reviewer_001",
        )

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from vigil.domain.coverage import CoverageRecord, CoverageStatus
from vigil.domain.investigation import Investigation
from vigil.domain.report import Report


def test_investigation_accepts_valid_inputs() -> None:
    investigation = Investigation(
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        title="Warehouse review",
        objective="Establish the sequence of events",
        scope_description="Review the north entrance camera",
        media_asset_ids=["media-001"],
        source_reference_ids=["source-001"],
    )

    assert investigation.investigation_id == "inv-001"
    assert investigation.media_asset_ids == ["media-001"]


def test_investigation_rejects_duplicate_media_ids() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Investigation(
            investigation_id="inv-001",
            title="Warehouse review",
            objective="Establish the sequence",
            scope_description="North entrance",
            media_asset_ids=["media-001", "media-001"],
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Investigation(
            investigation_id="inv-001",
            title="Warehouse review",
            objective="Establish the sequence",
            scope_description="North entrance",

        )

            investigation_id="inv-001",
            title="Warehouse review",
            objective="   ",
            scope_description="North entrance",
        )


def test_coverage_accepts_examined_source() -> None:
    record = CoverageRecord(
        coverage_id="cov-001",
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        source_reference_id="source-001",
        status=CoverageStatus.EXAMINED,
        interval_start=datetime(2026, 10, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        interval_end=datetime(2026, 10, 15, 10, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        examined_at=datetime(2026, 10, 15, 10, 5, tzinfo=UTC),
        artifact_ids=["artifact-001"],
    )

    assert record.status == CoverageStatus.EXAMINED
    assert record.artifact_ids == ["artifact-001"]


def test_coverage_rejects_examined_without_timestamp() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        CoverageRecord(
            coverage_id="cov-001",
            investigation_id="inv-001",
            source_reference_id="source-001",
            status=CoverageStatus.EXAMINED,
        )


def test_coverage_allows_failed_without_examined_at() -> None:
    record = CoverageRecord(
        coverage_id="cov-002",
        investigation_id="inv-001",
        source_reference_id="source-002",
        status=CoverageStatus.FAILED,
        notes="Source could not be decoded",
    )

    assert record.status == CoverageStatus.FAILED


def test_coverage_rejects_reversed_interval() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        CoverageRecord(

            interval_end=datetime(2026, 10, 15, 10, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):

            source_reference_id="source-001",

        )

            source_reference_id="source-001",

        )

PATH - tests\unit\domain\test_shared_contracts.py
CODE:
"""Tests for reusable VIGIL domain contract types."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import TypeAdapter, ValidationError

from vigil.domain.common import (
    ConfidenceScore,
    Identifier,
    ReferenceIds,
    Timestamp,
)


def test_identifier_accepts_nonblank_string() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(Identifier)

    assert adapter.validate_python("media-001") == "media-001"


def test_identifier_strips_surrounding_whitespace() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(Identifier)

    assert adapter.validate_python("  media-001  ") == "media-001"


def test_identifier_rejects_blank_string() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(Identifier)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python("   ")


def test_timestamp_accepts_timezone_aware_datetime() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(Timestamp)
    value = datetime(2026, 10, 15, 9, 0, tzinfo=UTC)

    assert adapter.validate_python(value) == value


def test_timestamp_rejects_naive_datetime() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(Timestamp)
    value = datetime(2026, 10, 15, 9, 0)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python(value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [0.0, 0.5, 1.0])
def test_confidence_accepts_values_in_range(value: float) -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ConfidenceScore)

    assert adapter.validate_python(value) == value


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [-0.1, 1.1, float("inf"), float("nan")])
def test_confidence_rejects_invalid_values(value: float) -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ConfidenceScore)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python(value)


def test_reference_ids_accept_unique_values() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ReferenceIds)

    assert adapter.validate_python(["media-001", "media-002"]) == [
        "media-001",
        "media-002",
    ]


def test_reference_ids_reject_duplicates() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ReferenceIds)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python(["media-001", "media-001"])


def test_reference_ids_reject_blank_values() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ReferenceIds)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python(["media-001", "   "])


def test_reference_ids_reject_duplicates_after_normalization() -> None:
    adapter = TypeAdapter(ReferenceIds)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        adapter.validate_python(["media-001", " media-001 "])

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from vigil.domain.artifact import Artifact
from vigil.domain.camera import Camera
from vigil.domain.media_asset import MediaAsset
from vigil.domain.source_reference import SourceReference


def test_camera_accepts_valid_metadata() -> None:
    camera = Camera(
        camera_id="cam_001",
        name="Parking Entrance",
        timezone="Asia/Kolkata",
        clock_offset_seconds=0.0,
    )

    assert camera.camera_id == "cam_001"


def test_camera_rejects_unknown_timezone() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Camera(
            camera_id="cam_001",
            name="Parking Entrance",
            timezone="Not/A_Timezone",
        )


def test_media_asset_rejects_invalid_checksum() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MediaAsset(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri="file:///recording.mp4",
            sha256="invalid",
        )


def test_media_asset_rejects_naive_timestamp() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MediaAsset(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri="file:///recording.mp4",
            recorded_at=datetime(2026, 10, 14, 10, 0),
        )


def test_source_reference_rejects_invalid_interval() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        SourceReference(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            start_seconds=15.0,
            end_seconds=10.0,
        )


def test_source_reference_accepts_valid_interval() -> None:
    reference = SourceReference(
        media_asset_id="media_001",
        start_seconds=10.0,
        end_seconds=15.0,
        source_timestamp=datetime(2026, 10, 14, 10, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
    )

    assert reference.end_seconds > reference.start_seconds


def test_artifact_rejects_incompatible_media_type() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Artifact(
            artifact_id="artifact_001",
            artifact_type="frame",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri="file:///frame.mp4",
            source_reference=SourceReference(
                media_asset_id="media_001",
                start_seconds=10.0,
                end_seconds=11.0,
            ),
        )


def test_artifact_accepts_valid_frame() -> None:
    artifact = Artifact(
        artifact_id="artifact_001",
        artifact_type="frame",
        media_type="image",
        storage_uri="file:///frame.jpg",
        source_reference=SourceReference(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            start_seconds=10.0,
            end_seconds=11.0,
        ),
    )

    assert artifact.artifact_type == "frame"
    assert artifact.media_type == "image"


def test_unexpected_fields_are_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Camera(
            camera_id="cam_001",
            name="Parking Entrance",
            unexpected_field="not_allowed",
        )


def test_camera_identifier_uses_shared_contract() -> None:
    camera = Camera(
        camera_id="  cam_001  ",
        name="Parking Entrance",
    )

    assert camera.camera_id == "cam_001"


def test_media_asset_rejects_blank_camera_reference() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MediaAsset(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            media_type="video",
            storage_uri="file:///recording.mp4",
            camera_id="   ",
        )


def test_source_reference_rejects_blank_media_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        SourceReference(
            media_asset_id="   ",
            start_seconds=1.0,
            end_seconds=2.0,
        )


def test_artifact_uses_shared_identifier_contract() -> None:
    artifact = Artifact(
        artifact_id="  artifact_001  ",
        artifact_type="frame",
        media_type="image",
        storage_uri="file:///frame.jpg",
        source_reference=SourceReference(
            media_asset_id="media_001",
            start_seconds=10.0,
            end_seconds=11.0,
        ),
    )

    assert artifact.artifact_id == "artifact_001"

CODE:
"""Unit tests for physical media decoding, JPEG extraction, and WAV demuxing."""

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from tests.media_test_helpers import create_test_video
from vigil.media.decoder import MediaDecoder


def test_extract_frames_from_video(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    video_path = tmp_path / "test_extract.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=2.0, fps=24, width=320, height=240)

    out_frames_dir = tmp_path / "frames"
    frames = MediaDecoder.extract_frames(
        file_path=video_path,
        output_dir=out_frames_dir,
        sample_interval_seconds=0.5,
        max_frames=5,
    )

    assert len(frames) > 0
    assert len(frames) <= 5

    for f in frames:
        assert Path(f.file_path).is_file()
        assert f.width == 320
        assert f.height == 240
        assert len(f.sha256) == 64
        assert f.timestamp_seconds >= 0.0


def test_demux_audio_to_wav(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    video_path = tmp_path / "test_demux.mp4"
    create_test_video(video_path, duration_seconds=1.5, with_audio=True)

    wav_out = tmp_path / "audio" / "extracted.wav"
    demuxed = MediaDecoder.demux_audio_to_wav(
        file_path=video_path,
        output_path=wav_out,
        target_sample_rate=16000,
    )

    assert Path(demuxed.file_path).is_file()
    assert demuxed.sample_rate == 16000
    assert demuxed.channels in {1, 2}
    assert demuxed.duration_seconds > 0.0
    assert len(demuxed.sha256) == 64


def test_demux_audio_on_video_without_audio_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    silent_video = tmp_path / "silent.mp4"
    create_test_video(silent_video, duration_seconds=1.0, with_audio=False)

    wav_out = tmp_path / "silent.wav"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="No audio stream found"):
        MediaDecoder.demux_audio_to_wav(
            file_path=silent_video,
            output_path=wav_out,
        )


def test_decoder_non_existent_file_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        MediaDecoder.extract_frames("missing.mp4", "output")
